Conclusion: 该工作将多模态3D HPE的精度与受试者级隐私保护统一在一个框架内，提出可操作的隐私审计与用户级DP训练方案，为多模态人体感知的隐私风险提供了系统性视角。

Abstract: Multimodal 3D Human Pose Estimation (3D HPE) combines complementary information from RGB, LiDAR, and mmWave radar, but models trained on correlated observations from the same individuals, raise privacy risks overlooked by record level analysis. We present a unified framework for multimodal 3D HPE that couples kinematics-induced sensor fusion with subject level privacy auditing and private training. First, our multimodal model aligns modality specific joint representation, injects skeletal structure and adaptively aggregates complementary sensor evidence for accurate pose prediction. Second, we formulate a black-box subject membership inference attack for 3D HPE, complemented by an empirical pointwise maximal leakage analysis, which characterizes how individual attack score outcomes change inference about the membership outcome. Third, we instantiate user-level differential privacy via Action Temporal Stratification, a population weighted within-subject sampling strategy that enforces action and temporal coverage. We evaluate our framework on the MM-Fi dataset across three diverse experimental protocols. Source-code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [36] [When Predicting Nothing Beats SAM 3: Revisiting Evaluation in Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02946)
*Jihwan Hong,Woohyeon Park,Jaeik Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FaVOS 基准，针对目标仅在长视频中间歇可见的"低时间可见度"VOS 场景，指出标准 J&F 指标在缺失帧上奖赏空预测、导致退化为存在性分类，并提出 Volumetric J&F 以体积化时空评价缓解该偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VOS 基准假设目标在大部分帧中显著可见，而真实长视频中目标常间歇性出现；此时标准 J&F 会给目标缺失帧上的空掩码高奖励，使评测退化为"缺席分类"，连简单空掩码预测器都能击败 SAM 3 等强模型，说明指标与实用性能脱节。

Method: 构建 FaVOS 基准以在低时间可见度下评测 VOS 方法；分析并揭示 J&F 在缺失帧奖励空预测的机制；提出 Volumetric J&F，将掩码序列视为时空体积进行评价，降低目标缺席奖励的支配作用，同时保留对分割质量与时间结构的敏感性。

Result: 在低可见度场景下，标准 J&F 使空掩码平凡预测器优于 SAM 3 等强模型；Volumetric J&F 抑制了缺席奖励的主导性，并维持对分割精度和时间连贯性的区分度。

Conclusion: FaVOS 与 Volumetric J&F 揭示了现有 VOS 评测指标在间歇可见目标下的根本错配，为该类实际场景提供了更合理的评测范式，但本身属基准与指标工作，未提出新的分割模型或跨域泛化的重建/生成方法。

Abstract: Video Object Segmentation (VOS) in complex and long videos is increasingly important for real-world applications, where target objects often appear only intermittently within long temporal horizons. However, existing benchmarks largely focus on temporally salient objects that remain visible for most of the video. To address this gap, we introduce FaVOS (A Benchmark for Video Object Segmentation with Fractional Temporal Visibility), a benchmark designed to evaluate VOS methods under low temporal visibility. We show that, in this regime, the standard J&F metric can collapse VOS evaluation into absence classification, because empty predictions receive high rewards on target-absent frames. Consequently, even a trivial empty-mask predictor can outperform strong models such as SAM 3, revealing a fundamental mismatch between current metrics and practical VOS performance. To mitigate this issue, we propose Volumetric J&F, which evaluates mask sequences as spatio-temporal volumes and reduces the dominance of target-absence rewards while preserving sensitivity to segmentation quality and temporal structure. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [37] [TerraVis: Towards Evaluation of World-Grounded Visual Consistency in Text-to-Image Generation via MLLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02959)
*Shuai Fu,Jing Gu,Jian Zhou,Zicheng Duan,Gengze Zhou,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TerraVis 框架，用结构化分类法与多阶段 MLLM 评估流程，量化文生图图像在"世界一致性"（物体结构、交互、场景层面）上的失效，并验证其与人类判断的相关性最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保真度、美学、偏好与文图对齐指标无法捕捉"视觉上好看但物理/结构上不可能"的生成失败（畸形结构、错误解剖、物理不可能交互、空间关系不一致），需要一个新的评估维度。

Method: 定义覆盖物体级、交互级、场景级失效的世界一致性违规分类法（18 种类型）；对给定图像先由 MLLM 判断是否可评估，再检测违规类型并区分 minor/major，据此计算总体世界一致性得分。

Result: 在两个常用基准、多种开源与闭源文生图模型上，TerraVis 与人类世界一致性判断的相关性优于现有指标；并且传统指标表现优异的模型仍存在显著世界一致性失败。

Conclusion: 世界一致性应作为与保真度/美学/对齐互补的评估维度，TerraVis 可系统化量化、诊断与比较此类失败。

Abstract: Recent text-to-image models have made substantial progress in photorealism, aesthetics, and text-image alignment. Yet visually appealing images can still violate real-world plausibility, exhibiting malformed object structures, impossible anatomy, physically implausible interactions, or inconsistent spatial relationships. Such failures are not well captured by existing fidelity, aesthetics, preference, or alignment metrics. To address this gap, we introduce TerraVis, a framework for evaluating world-grounded visual consistency in generated images. TerraVis defines a structured taxonomy of world-consistency violations spanning object-, interaction-, and scene-level failures, and employs a multi-stage evaluation framework to identify and quantify them. Given an image, TerraVis first uses an MLLM to assess its eligibility for evaluation, then detects violations across 18 taxonomy-defined types and classifies them as minor or major to derive an overall world-consistency score. Across diverse open-source and proprietary text-to-image models on two widely used benchmarks, TerraVis achieves the strongest correlation with human judgments of world consistency among existing metrics. Our benchmark results further show that models that achieve strong performance on conventional metrics can still exhibit substantial world-consistency failures. These findings highlight world consistency as a complementary evaluation dimension and demonstrate that TerraVis enables systematic quantification, diagnosis, and comparison of such failures. Our code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [38] [Post-Training Frontier Text-to-Image Models by Composing Preference and Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02967)
*Yuanhao Ban,I-Hung Hsu,Anastasios Angelopoulos,Wei-Lin Chiang,Ion Stoica,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向开放域文生图的后训练配方，通过组合偏好奖励与基于评分量表(rubric)的奖励来优化模型，避免简单加权平均带来的次优优化行为；在Arena T2I榜单上RL训练的Flux2dev比基座高69 Elo，后训练的Ideogram-4达1223.5 Elo。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一奖励信号无法覆盖人类偏好的全部维度，现有文生图后训练(post-training)难以同时兼顾整体美学偏好与提示忠实度，且存在奖励黑客(reward hacking)风险。

Method: 构建互补奖励系统：(1) 偏好奖励——基于大规模人类偏好数据、以Bradley-Terry目标训练，捕捉整体美学与感知偏好；(2) rubric-based奖励——显式评估提示忠实度及其他期望属性，并提供对抗奖励黑客的保护。针对异构奖励融合难题，指出朴素加权平均导致次优优化行为，提出一种简单的奖励组合策略以更好平衡偏好优化与rubric满足度；采用RL进行后训练。

Result: 在Arena文生图排行榜上，RL训练的Flux2dev的Elo比基座模型高69分；后训练的Ideogram-4超过榜单上所有开源模型，达到Elo 1223.5（SOTA声明基于2026年9月4日榜单快照）。同时发布了Arena-T2I-Training，一个1K训练数据子集，可复现部分全量训练增益。

Conclusion: 前沿生成模型训练的有效奖励需具备对用户意图的广泛覆盖性与对优化过程利用(exploitation)的鲁棒性；互补奖励组合优于朴素加权平均。发布小规模训练子集以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Recent text-to-image generation models have achieved remarkable visual quality, but improving them through post-training remains challenging because no single reward signal captures the full range of human preference. In this work, we develop a simple and effective post-training recipe for open-domain text-to-image generation based on the composition of complementary reward signals. Our reward system consists of two main components: a preference reward, trained on large-scale human preference data using a Bradley-Terry objective to capture overall human aesthetic and perceptual preferences, and rubric-based rewards, which explicitly evaluate prompt faithfulness and other desirable properties while providing safeguards against reward hacking. A key challenge is how to combine these heterogeneous reward signals. We show that a naive weighted average leads to suboptimal optimization behavior, and propose a simple reward composition strategy that more effectively balances preference optimization with rubric satisfaction. In the Arena text-to-image leaderboard ( this https URL ), our RL-trained Flux2dev achieves an Elo rating 69 points above the base model, and our post-trained Ideogram-4 surpasses every open-source model on the leaderboard, reaching an Elo of 1223.5. (Claims of state-of-the-art performance are based on the Arena leaderboard snapshot as of September 4, 2026.) Our results suggest that effective rewards for frontier generative-model training require broad coverage of user intent and robustness to exploitation under optimization. To support reproducible research, we release Arena-T2I-Training, a 1K subset of training data that recovers some gains of full-scale training, providing a resource that we hope will facilitate future work on post-training for text-to-image models.

</details>


### [39] [Rethinking Fixed Temporal Grids: Frequency-Disentangled Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03012)
*Yunjiao Zhou,Junlang Qian,Gen Li,Xinying Guo,Lihua Xie,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreqMo提出尺度自适应的人体运动表示，将运动分解为小波频带以分离时间尺度上的动态，并用统一频率残差量化（UFRQ）在共享码本中编码所有频带，压缩三倍token并支持稳定的单阶段生成，在保持高频细节上取得SOTA保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人体运动生成方法在均匀时间网格上把运动编码为等长token，但人体运动本质上是时间异质的：缓慢的全局轨迹与脚部触地、关节冲量等快速瞬态事件共存。统一时间分辨率会纠缠不同频率的运动，使缓慢区域冗余、快速细节被平滑掉。

Method: 提出FreqMo尺度自适应运动表示，基于小波频带分解运动，跨时间尺度分离动态，同时保持时间定位与精确重构；提出统一频率残差量化（UFRQ），在单个共享码本中编码全部频带。

Result: token序列压缩约三倍，支持稳定的单阶段生成；实验达到SOTA保真度并显著改善高频保持，该分解可迁移到连续扩散骨干网络。

Conclusion: 频率域多尺度分解为运动tokenization提供了更符合运动本质的表示，兼顾压缩率、高频细节与生成稳定性，且与扩散等连续生成范式兼容。

Abstract: Most human motion generation methods encode motion as tokens on a uniform temporal grid, where every token spans the same fixed time window. Human motion, however, is temporally heterogeneous: slowly evolving global trajectories coexist with rapid transient events such as foot contacts and joint impulses. Forcing such multi-scale dynamics onto tokens of identical temporal resolution entangles motion frequencies, leaving slow regions redundant while smoothing out the rapid details that distinguish realistic motion. We propose \textbf{FreqMo}, a scale-adaptive motion representation that decomposes motion into wavelet frequency bands, separating dynamics across temporal scales while preserving temporal localization and exact reconstruction. Unified Frequency Residual Quantization (UFRQ) then encodes all bands within a single shared codebook, compressing the token sequence threefold and enabling stable single-stage generation. Experiments show FreqMo attains SOTA fidelity with substantially improved high-frequency preservation, and the same decomposition transfers to continuous diffusion backbones.

</details>


### [40] [RYOPO: Bringing End-to-End Category-Level Object Pose Estimation into Real Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03013)
*Hakjin Lee,Junghoon Seo,Jaehoon Sim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出查询式端到端RGB-D集合预测器，联合检测分割物体并估计9-DoF位姿，无需CAD先验与独立实例分割器，在RTX A6000上达31.8 FPS实时全帧位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高精度RGB-D类别级位姿估计方法多依赖外部分割与逐物体裁剪编码，导致流程分离、计算与物体数相关，难以实时推理。

Method: 端到端可训练的查询式RGB-D集合预测器：共享图像与场景编码避免重复裁剪编码；查询条件几何通路将观测3D点与RGB特征关联到物体查询并融合场景上下文；以点描述子驱动显式位姿状态，通过位姿条件交叉注意力与循环残差修正进行物体中心式细化。

Result: 在NOCS上显著超越已发表的RGB-D联合检测与位姿估计结果；在REAL275与HouseCat6D的全物体评估下与两阶段方法竞争力相当，并实现31.8 FPS实时全帧位姿估计。

Conclusion: 端到端查询式设计可同时兼顾精度与实时性，摆脱CAD形状先验与独立分割器依赖，为机器人抓取等实时场景下的类别级位姿估计提供可行方案。

Abstract: Category-level object pose estimation predicts the rotation, translation, and metric size of unseen instances within known categories. Many accurate RGB-D methods rely on external instance segmentation and crop-based pose estimation, introducing separate stages and object-dependent processing costs that hinder real-time inference. To bring accurate pose estimation into real time, we present \ours{}, an end-to-end trainable query-based RGB-D set predictor. It jointly detects and segments objects and estimates their \mbox{9-DoF} poses without explicit CAD-derived shape priors or a separately trained instance segmentor. Shared image and scene encoding avoids repeated per-object crop encoding. A query-conditioned geometry pathway associates observed 3D points and RGB features with object queries and incorporates shared scene context. Object-centric refinement uses the resulting point descriptors to update an explicit pose state through pose-conditioned cross-attention and recurrent residual corrections. On NOCS, \ours{} substantially improves on published RGB-D joint detection and pose estimation results. It achieves competitive performance compared with two-stage methods under all-object evaluation on REAL275 and HouseCat6D, while enabling real-time full-frame pose estimation at $31.8$ FPS on an RTX~A6000. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [41] [OmniAct3D: Leveraging Foundation Geometry and Evidence-Grounded Reasoning for Panoramic 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03015)
*Runtong Wu,Fei Teng,Di Wen,Guoqiang Zhao,Kunyu Peng,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniAct3D 将透视训练的视觉基础模型（VFM）3D 检测器适配到球面 ERP 全景图像，通过射线几何适配、视觉-动作推理链与朝向专家模块实现连续 360° 环绕检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动具身智能体需要准确的 3D 检测，VFM 可提供可迁移的视觉与几何先验，但现有 VFM 检测器仅依赖窄视场单目图像或离散透视视图，难以实现连贯的环绕感知。ERP 能在单图内编码连续 360° 场景，然而其几何与视觉信息组织方式与透视图像不同，直接迁移困难：目标相关线索难以建模、定位与保留。

Method: 提出 OmniAct3D 框架，在保留 VFM 可迁移先验的前提下将透视检测器适配到 ERP。包含三个模块：(1) ERP-Ray Geometry Adapter（ERGA-Ray）建模球面视线射线与周期性空间结构以解决几何不匹配；(2) Visual-Action Reasoning Chain（VARC）在场景级上下文中将每个假设锚定到相关全景证据并转化为结构化几何动作以定位证据；(3) Appearance-Guided Heading Expert（AGHE）在固定 token 预算下对目标区域高分辨率重编码以恢复局部线索并估计朝向。

Result: 在 Spheriverse 上较此前最佳 3D 检测器提升 2.96 NDS；在 PanoMMOcc 上较未适配的 VFM 基线提升 24.87 mAP。目标特定的几何适配下，VARC 保留同配置 mAP 的 95–98%，表明目标级 3D 推理可跨传感配置复用。

Conclusion: OmniAct3D 表明透视预训练的 VFM 检测器可有效迁移至 ERP 全景 3D 检测，同时保持可迁移先验；显式几何适配与结构化推理使目标级 3D 推理具备跨传感配置的可复用性。源码将公开。

Abstract: Accurate 3D detection is essential for mobile embodied agents, while Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) offer transferable visual and geometric priors. Yet existing VFM-based 3D detectors rely on narrow-view monocular images or discrete perspective views, limiting coherent surround perception; equirectangular projection (ERP) instead encodes a continuous 360 scene in a single image. Direct transfer remains difficult because ERP organizes geometry and visual information differently, making object-relevant cues hard to model, localize, and preserve. We propose OmniAct3D, a framework that adapts perspective-trained VFM detectors to ERP while preserving transferable VFM priors. To resolve geometric mismatch, the ERP-Ray Geometry Adapter (ERGA-Ray) models spherical viewing rays and periodic spatial structure. To localize evidence in scene-wide context, the Visual-Action Reasoning Chain (VARC) grounds each hypothesis in relevant panoramic evidence and converts it into a structured geometric action. To recover local cues lost under fixed token budgets, the Appearance-Guided Heading Expert (AGHE) re-encodes object regions at higher resolution for heading estimation. Experiments show that OmniAct3D improves over the previous best 3D detector by 2.96 NDS points on Spheriverse and over the unadapted VFM baseline by 24.87 mAP points on PanoMMOcc. With target-specific geometry adaptation, VARC retains 95--98% of the same-configuration mAP, indicating reusable object-level 3D reasoning across sensing configurations. The source code will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [42] [ReSCUE: Re-translation with Sentence Commitment for Unsegmented Long-Form Simultaneous Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03022)
*Sihan Ren,Gaozheng Li,Yuanshang Quan,Yiming Qin,Fuyi Yang,Chang Liu,Lan Xu,Minye Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReSCUE：面向未分段长视频流的手语同步翻译统一框架，通过推理感知训练、稳定重翻译与句子提交机制，在低延迟下逼近离线oracle质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语翻译（SLT）方法多局限于句级、离线设定，假设输入已预分段，无法应对连续未分段的真实视频流；且同步翻译在部分输入、非手语停顿、多句上下文下缺乏对齐训练与推理的机制，重翻译还易产生输出闪烁。

Method: 提出ReSCUE统一框架：1) 推理感知训练（inference-aware training），显式处理部分输入、非手语停顿与多句上下文；2) 稳定重翻译（stabilized re-translation），实现低延迟且可修正的预测并抑制输出闪烁；3) 句子提交机制（sentence commitment），用于在线分段与记忆管理。

Result: 在标准句级基准上，ReSCUE在低延迟设定下取得最低延迟与最佳翻译质量；在长视频未分段数据集上，其翻译质量接近使用真值句边界的离线oracle系统，同时延迟显著更低。

Conclusion: ReSCUE验证了以流式条件对齐训练与推理的可行性，使同步手语翻译在真实流式场景中具备实用性，可作为长视频在线SLT的统一方案。

Abstract: Simultaneous Sign Language Translation (SLT) is critical for real-time communication, yet existing methods remain largely confined to sentence-level, offline settings that assume pre-segmented inputs. These assumptions hinder deployment in realistic scenarios involving continuous, unsegmented video streams. We present ReSCUE, a unified framework for simultaneous SLT on unsegmented long-form sign language videos that aligns training and inference with realistic streaming conditions. ReSCUE combines inference-aware training to handle partial inputs, non-signing pauses, and multi-sentence contexts, stabilized re-translation to enable low-latency yet revisable predictions with reduced output flicker, and a sentence commitment mechanism for online segmentation and memory management. Experiments on standard sentence-level benchmarks show that ReSCUE achieves lower latency and the best translation quality under low-latency settings. On long-form unsegmented datasets, ReSCUE approaches the translation quality of oracle offline systems that use ground-truth sentence boundaries, while operating at substantially lower latency, demonstrating its practicality for real-world streaming scenarios.

</details>


### [43] [CrowdOcc: Monocular Semantic Scene Completion for Quadruped Robots in Crowded Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03031)
*Feiyang Chen,Jincheng Hu,Yiduo Chen,Jihao Li,Yue Liang,Bingzhao Gao,Yanjun Huang,Yuanjian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrowdOcc 提出面向四足机器人的 RGB-D 数据集与单目语义场景补全（SSC）框架，针对拥挤室内场景中人际遮挡导致的几何破坏与人体占据预测不完整问题，通过静态-动态解耦标注构建 25.1K 帧数据，并设计法向引导几何融合与人体中心稀疏交互模块，在场景不相交测试集上达到 15.80 IoU / 11.40 mIoU / 46.23 Human IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在真实拥挤室内环境中的单目语义场景补全研究不足：人体与场景遮挡破坏静态几何完整性，且人体占据预测常不完整或空间错位，现有 SSC 方法缺乏针对该场景的数据与建模手段。

Method: 构建 CrowdOcc 数据集（11 个室内场景、25.1K 帧 RGB-D 与语义占据标注，采用静态-动态解耦生成标注）；框架包含 (i) Normal Guided Scene Geometry Fusion（NGSGF），在深度感知提升基础上引入表面法向线索以增强遮挡鲁棒的几何补全；(ii) Human-Centric Sparse Interaction（HCSI），在 3D 中稀疏建模人与人及局部人-场景关系。

Result: 在 CrowdOcc 场景不相交测试集上取得 SOTA：15.80 IoU、11.40 mIoU、46.23 Human IoU，并展示对未见室内场景的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CrowdOcc 为拥挤室内四足机器人场景提供了数据集与单目 SSC 方法基准，验证了法向引导几何融合与人体中心稀疏交互在遮挡鲁棒补全上的有效性，具备跨场景泛化性。

Abstract: Monocular semantic scene completion (SSC) for quadruped robots remains underexplored in real crowded indoor environments, where human-scene occlusion disrupts static geometry and human occupancy predictions are often incomplete or spatially misplaced. We present CrowdOcc, an RGB-D dataset and monocular SSC framework for this setting. CrowdOcc contains 25.1K frames from 11 indoor scenes, with semantic occupancy annotations constructed through static dynamic decoupling. Our framework combines: (i) Normal Guided Scene Geometry Fusion (NGSGF) to complement depth-aware lifting with surface-normal cues for occlusion robust geometry; and (ii) Human-Centric Sparse Interaction (HCSI) to selectively model human-human and local human scene relations in 3D. Our method achieves state-of-the-art SSC performance on CrowdOcc's scene-disjoint test set, reaching 15.80 IoU, 11.40 mIoU, and 46.23 Human IoU, demonstrating generalization to unseen indoor scenes.

</details>


### [44] [Parasitic Co-Denoising: Unlocking 3D Human Motion Generation in a Frozen Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03047)
*Yunjiao Zhou,Junlang Qian,Lihua Xie,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 从冻结的文本到视频扩散模型Wan2.1中"寄生地"解码3D人体运动，提出共享去噪调度、σ自适应多层融合的PMD解码器，无需修改宿主、无需独立运动模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模文本到视频扩散模型从未接受显式3D运动监督，却能生成逼真的人体运动，作者想探究这种隐式知识能否在不训练独立运动模型的前提下转化为显式3D运动生成能力。探针实验显示Wan2.1的中间状态在整条去噪调度上均存在可恢复的运动信号，而非仅存在于干净输出中。

Method: 提出parasitic co-denoising范式：运动从宿主模型沿其去噪调度解码，而非由独立生成器产生。实例化为Parasitic Motion Decoder（PMD），一个高效流匹配解码器，复用宿主的噪声调度，通过σ自适应多层融合读取宿主中间特征，且不修改宿主模型。

Result: PMD的运动覆盖能力来自宿主而非运动数据，在文本-运动对齐指标上以极小比例的可训练参数领先专用运动生成器；同时单次前向即可成对生成视频与运动，这是仅运动基线无法做到的。

Conclusion: 视频扩散模型的中间去噪状态蕴含可提取的3D运动表征，"寄生式协同去噪"可将该隐式知识转为显式运动生成，兼得对齐质量、参数效率与视频-运动配对输出。

Abstract: Despite never being supervised on explicit 3D motion, large-scale text-to-video diffusion models synthesize realistic human motion in their generated videos. We ask whether this implicit knowledge can be turned into explicit 3D motion generation, without training a separate motion model. Probing a frozen Wan2.1 reveals that a recoverable motion signal is present in its intermediate states across the entire denoising schedule, not confined to the clean output. Motivated by this, we introduce parasitic co-denoising, a paradigm in which motion is decoded from the host model along its denoising schedule rather than produced by an independent generator. We instantiate it as the Parasitic Motion Decoder (PMD), an efficient flow-matching decoder that shares the host's noise schedule and reads its intermediate features through a $\sigma$-adaptive multi-layer fusion, leaving the host unmodified. Drawing its coverage from the host rather than from motion data, PMD leads dedicated motion generators on text-motion alignment at a small fraction of their trainable parameters, while producing paired video and motion in a single pass that motion-only baselines cannot match.

</details>


### [45] [BeeWhere: Segmenting Bumble Bee Colonies to Quantify Behavioral Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03051)
*Roberta Hunt,August Easton-Calabria,James Crall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 BeeWhere，结合 ArUco 标记检测与 YOLO 实例分割，从高分辨率熊蜂蜂群图像/视频中量化个体与群体行为，在遮挡条件下优于纯标记跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会性蜜蜂是重要传粉者与集体行为模型系统，但巢内环境密集遮挡，可扩展的个体/群体行为测量困难。基于基准标记（ArUco）的跟踪在标记被遮挡时会失效，且无法提供身体轮廓、空间语境和未标记个体的信息。

Method: AI 辅助标注与分析流程：将 ArUco 检测与深度学习实例分割结合。以熊蜂（Bombus impatiens）微蜂群为测试对象，标注 483 帧、8,443 个蜜蜂实例，并额外标注花粉球、巢结构与巢室边界，训练 YOLO 实例分割模型用于下游行为分析。

Result: 实例分割支持基于身体轮廓的行为指标量化，包括最近邻距离、与巢结构邻近度、巢内空间占据和随时间变化的检测计数。在新烟碱类农药暴露的探索性验证中，BeeWhere 相比标记跟踪提高了检测率，尤其在蜜蜂部分被遮挡或成像条件恶劣时，并捕捉到仅靠标记跟踪无法发现的处理相关空间组织变化。

Conclusion: 实例分割可补充基准标记跟踪，在困难的蜂群条件下恢复具有行为意义的信号，为密集遮挡环境下的群体行为量化提供可扩展方案。

Abstract: Social bees are important pollinators that support biodiversity and crop pollination globally and serve as important model systems for collective behavior, but scalable measurement of individual- and colony-level behavior remains difficult in dense, occluded nest environments. Existing monitoring workflows use fiducial tags (e.g., ArUco) to preserve individual identity, yet tag-based tracking can fail when markers are obscured and provide limited information about body extent, spatial context, and untagged individuals. We present BeeWhere, an AI-assisted annotation and analysis workflow that combines ArUco detections with deep-learnt instance segmentations to quantify bumble bee behavior from high-resolution colony images and videos. Using bumble bee (Bombus impatiens) microcolonies as a test case, we annotate 483 frames containing 8,443 bee instances. We additionally annotate pollen balls, nest structures, and chamber boundaries, and train YOLO instance segmentation models for downstream behavioral analysis. Instance segmentations enable quantification of important behavioral metrics based on body contours, including nearest-neighbor distance, proximity to nest structures, spatial occupancy within the nest, and detection counts over time. We apply the BeeWhere models to tag-based tracking in an exploratory validation study assessing the behavioral impacts of neonicotinoid pesticide exposure. BeeWhere increased detection rates compared to tag-based tracking, particularly when bees were partially obscured or under challenging imaging conditions, and also captured treatment-associated changes in bee spatial organization not captured using tag-based tracking alone. These results suggest that instance segmentation can complement fiducial-marker tracking by recovering behaviorally meaningful signals under challenging colony conditions.

</details>


### [46] [Where to Look Is Not How to Fix: Pre-Denoising Diagnostics and Modality-Dependent Control in Diffusion Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03068)
*Fangzheng Wu,Brian Summa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出锚点-压力（anchor–stress）协议，用纯文本的 Compositional Stress Index（CSI）诊断文生图扩散模型的组合性缺陷，并通过六提示定位实验发现"诊断-控制解耦"：可诊断风险的表示坐标（深层文本编码器）不等于改善生成的干预坐标（解码器交叉注意力）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型在属性-物体组合（compositional）生成上常失败，但缺乏对"压力在哪里可被检测"和"干预如何改变输出"这两个问题的联合刻画。作者希望建立受控协议，把上游文本编码器的诊断坐标与下游去噪器的干预效应联系起来，判断诊断能否指导有效干预。

Method: 构建 anchor–stress 受控协议，同时评估文本编码器诊断与去噪器干预；提出纯文本的 Compositional Stress Index（CSI），在 SD1.5、SDXL、SD3 文本通路上区分常见与稀有组合，作为上游诊断坐标；设计六提示匹配的定位研究，对比残差最小化嵌入适配器、下游交叉注意力干预、选择性增强/削减与广域消融在 SD1.5 与 SDXL 各去噪块上的效应，以 CHR（颜色命中率）等指标度量。

Result: 残差最小化嵌入适配器改善表示拟合；下游交叉注意力干预使 CHR 提升 0.0272。SD1.5 与 SDXL 去噪块中，最大正号诊断可达性均值出现在深层编码器，而选择性增强的最大正 CHR 响应出现在解码器块；选择性削减与广域消融揭示模态与架构依赖的差异，如广域消融 SDXL 解码器交叉注意力导致 CHR 显著下降。

Conclusion: 在受控属性-物体组合设定下存在"诊断-控制解耦"：组合缺陷可在去噪前被诊断，但暴露风险的表示坐标未必就是能改善生成的坐标或模态。这意味着诊断指标不宜直接当作干预指导信号。

Abstract: Understanding compositional failures in text-to-image diffusion requires identifying both where stress is detectable and how intervention changes the output. We study these questions through a controlled anchor--stress protocol that jointly evaluates text-encoder diagnostics and denoiser interventions. We introduce a text-only Compositional Stress Index (CSI), which separates common from rare compositions across SD1.5, SDXL, and the SD3 text path and provides an upstream diagnostic coordinate. A matched six-prompt localization study links intervention location to distinct outcomes: residual-minimizing embedding adapters improve representation fit, while downstream cross-attention intervention increases color hit rate (CHR) by 0.0272. Across SD1.5 and SDXL denoiser blocks, the largest positive signed diagnostic-accessibility mean occurs at the deep encoder, whereas selective boost has its largest positive mean CHR response at decoder blocks. Selective subtraction and broad ablation reveal further modality- and architecture-dependent responses, including a substantial CHR decrease when SDXL decoder cross-attention is broadly ablated. We find a diagnosis-control dissociation under our controlled attribute-object composition setting: compositional defects are diagnosable before denoising, but the representation coordinate that exposes risk is not necessarily the coordinate or modality that improves generation.

</details>


### [47] [NegT2IBench: When Negation Changes the Picture. A Polarity Benchmark for Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03084)
*Omar Elfatairy,Maria A. Bravo,Jessica Bader,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 NegT2IBench，一个含 4,800 条提示的否定约束 T2I 基准，通过分离正/负陈述极性揭示模型"满足要求"与"规避禁止"是两种可区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 T2I 基准只衡量请求内容是否出现，普遍忽视对否定约束（如"非红色的杯子"）的满足能力；而否定评测在 prompt 设计与判定上都有独特难点。

Method: 构建 NegT2IBench：4,800 条提示，覆盖 2 种属性类型与 4 类关系；按极性组织，正向陈述数与否定陈述数各取 0–2，二者独立变化以解耦否定效应与提示复杂度效应；采用基于检测器的可复现、可审计评分，可定位具体失败条目。

Result: 在 600 张三标注者图像上，该检测评分与人类一致性接近高达其 30 倍规模的视觉-语言评判模型，且 GPU 显存占用仅为其一小部分。跨 11 个 T2I 模型、211,200 张图像，其中 9 个在单条否定陈述上得分低于单条正向陈述；逐条评分显示颜色损失最大、邻近关系近于零，41.5% 的失败陈述恰好渲染出提示所禁止的内容。

Conclusion: 渲染所需内容与抑制所禁内容是两种不同能力，聚合式组合得分无法区分；NegT2IBench 直接度量后者，为诊断否定失败与开发改进方法提供受控测试平台。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models are judged by benchmarks that measure whether requested content appears, but these benchmarks largely overlook the complementary ability to satisfy negated constraints, for example, generating "a non-red cup." Measuring negation raises challenges not faced by affirmation-based benchmarks and requires careful prompt and evaluation design. We introduce NegT2IBench, a benchmark of 4,800 prompts covering two attribute types and four relation categories. Prompts are organized by polarity: the number of positive statements that must hold and negated statements that must not, each ranging from 0 to 2. Varying the two independently separates the effect of negation from the effect of prompt complexity. Our detector-based scoring is reproducible, auditable, and pinpoints which requirement failed. On 600 images with three-annotator labels, it agrees with humans as closely as vision-language judges up to 30x larger, while using only a fraction of their GPU memory. Across eleven T2I models and 211,200 images, nine score lower on a single negated statement than on a single positive one. Per-statement scoring reveals that the loss is largest for color and near zero for proximity, and that 41.5% of failed statements render exactly what the prompt forbids. Rendering what a prompt asks for and withholding what it forbids are distinct capabilities that an aggregate compositional score cannot distinguish. NegT2IBench measures the latter directly, providing a controlled testbed for diagnosing negation failures and developing methods to overcome them.

</details>


### [48] [Beyond Single Videos: Benchmarking and Active Evidence Seeking for E-Commerce Cross-Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03099)
*Jinghan Zhao,Yiman Hu,Liang Wu,Jian Xu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出电商跨视频推理基准 AdsCVR（2483 视频、6110 问答对、6 个推理维度）与智能体框架 AdSeek，通过多轮动态调用视觉/音频工具主动采集证据，并用离线轨迹矫正机制衔接 RL 与 SFT，在测试集达 74.30%（较 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 提升 27.90 个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型以单视频理解为重心，跨视频比较能力有限，而电商视频信息密集、消费者与商家常需跨视频对比；跨视频推理需在大量冗余帧中定位细粒度证据，并融合视觉细节、语音与屏幕文字，静态均匀采样难以胜任。

Method: 1）AdsCVR 基准：2483 个视频、6110 个问答对，覆盖六个推理维度；2）AdSeek 智能体框架：多轮探索中动态选择视觉与音频工具，以主动证据获取替代静态均匀采样；3）离线轨迹矫正机制：在 RL 生成轨迹中识别推理错误与缺失的多模态证据并修正，将矫正轨迹作为 SFT 监督信号以缓解 RL 学到的偏置；4）矫正式自举流水线：初始 RL 暴露推理瓶颈→SFT 矫正→最终 RL 进一步优化策略。

Result: AdSeek 在 AdsCVR 测试集取得 74.30% 准确率，较其 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 主干提升 27.90 个百分点；并在开放域 CrossVid 基准上具备泛化能力，验证主动证据采集的有效性。

Conclusion: 跨视频多模态推理可通过智能体式主动证据获取与“RL—SFT 矫正—RL”自举范式显著提升性能；该范式对稀疏奖励下的信用分配问题提供了可行解法，但评测场景限于电商/视频对比领域，属通用多模态智能体与基准工作。

Abstract: E-commerce videos are information-dense and frequently compared by consumers evaluating products and merchants assessing marketing strategies. However, existing multimodal models mainly focus on single-video understanding and have limited ability to compare information across videos. We introduce AdsCVR, the first e-commerce cross-video reasoning benchmark, containing 2,483 videos and 6,110 question-answer pairs across six reasoning dimensions. Cross- video reasoning requires models to locate fine-grained evidence among many redundant frames and integrate visual details, speech, and on-screen text. We therefore propose AdSeek, an agentic framework that dynamically selects visual and audio tools during multi-turn exploration, replacing static uniform sampling with active evidence acquisition. To address the sparse credit assignment of reinforcement learning, we develop an offline trajectory rectification mechanism that identifies reasoning errors and missing multimodal evidence in RL-generated trajectories. The corrected trajectories provide supervised fine-tuning signals that reduce biases learned during RL. This mechanism supports a rectified bootstrapping pipeline in which initial RL exposes reasoning bottlenecks, supervised fine-tuning corrects them, and a final RL stage further improves the policy. AdSeek achieves 74.30 percent accuracy on the AdsCVR test split, outperforming its Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct backbone by 27.90 percentage points. It also generalizes to the open- domain CrossVid benchmark, demonstrating effective active evidence gathering.

</details>


### [49] [A Benchmark for Spatially Grounded Gesture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03105)
*Anna Deichler,Rishabh Dabral,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Anindita Ghosh,Jonas Beskow*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出空间接地的指向手势生成基准：约2K VR对话指向标注片段、时/空/自然度分离评测协议，并给出flow-matching基线MM-Conv-Flow；发现几何接地精度可超越人类指向但自然度无提升，说明需分维度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享空间交流中指向手势将语言锚定到环境，但现有分布类指标只要手势"看起来自然"就会奖励指向错误物体的手势，缺乏判断生成手势是否指向正确指称对象的通用评测框架。

Method: 构建约2K条来自自然VR对话的指向标注片段，含真实3D指称对象；定义需在对话语音中决定何时、如何、指向何处的手势生成任务；设计分离时间对齐、空间接地与感知自然度的评测协议；提供flow-matching基线MM-Conv-Flow，并与独立检索式系统及真人捕获动作对比。

Result: 几何接地（指向正确性）可超过真人指向水平，但感知自然度并未随之提升；两类指标解耦，表明指称手势质量必须沿独立维度衡量。

Conclusion: 指向手势生成不能仅用外观自然度或分布指标评价，需引入显式空间接地度量；该基准与协议为空间接地手势生成提供了可复用的评测范式。

Abstract: Communication in shared space interweaves verbal and non-verbal signals, and pointing gestures anchor language to the environment: "put the cup on that one" is uninterpretable without the gesture that fixes the referent. Yet no common framework exists for evaluating whether generated gestures indicate their intended referent; distributional metrics reward a gesture aimed at the wrong object as long as it looks natural. We introduce a benchmark for spatially grounded gesture generation, comprising ~2K pointing-annotated clips from naturalistic VR dialogue with ground-truth 3D referents, a task in which systems must decide when, how and where to point within conversational speech, and a protocol that separates temporal alignment, spatial grounding and perceived naturalness. We also provide a flow-matching baseline, MM-Conv-Flow. Evaluating it alongside an independent retrieval-based system and captured human motion, we find that geometric grounding can exceed that of human pointing without any gain in perceived naturalness, showing that referential gesture quality must be measured along separate dimensions.

</details>


### [50] [In-Distribution Forcing for Long Video Generation at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03120)
*Jeongwoo Shin,Youngyoon Choi,Sangwoo Jo,Hyunmog Kim,Sungjoon Choi,Joonseok Lee,Jaewoong Choi,Jaemoo Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 In-Distribution Forcing（ID-Forcing）测试时框架，通过 self-caching 机制在源头避免 OOD 的 KV 条目，将短时长自回归视频扩散模型无缝扩展到分钟级长视频生成，显著缓解漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在短时长生成上表现优异，但长视频生成仍面临漂移问题（颜色/纹理偏移、运动动态衰减）。现有 KV conditioning 方法假设缓存 KV 条目仍在分布内，而超出训练时长后该假设失效，导致缓存条目本身变为 OOD，即 KV-provenance 问题。

Method: ID-Forcing 在测试时对齐 KV 缓存与 KV conditioning 的配置。核心机制为 self-caching：每个 chunk 在缓存时不 attend 先前的 KV 条目，使滚动窗口保持在分布内，从而在源头阻止 OOD KV 条目的产生。

Result: 在标准视频生成基准上保持竞争力，同时在缓解漂移方面大幅优于先前工作，并通过自研漂移指标与用户研究双重验证。

Conclusion: 将 OOD 问题定位到 KV 条目来源，用 self-caching 实现分布内滚动窗口，是一种无需重训练即可延长自回归视频生成时长的有效测试时方案。

Abstract: Modern autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models excel at short-horizon video generation, yet generating long videos remains challenging due to drifting, where colors and textures shift, and motion dynamics decay. Existing works primarily rely on KV conditioning, which selects or modifies cached key-value (KV) entries to mitigate drifting. However, we observe that KV conditioning alone is insufficient as it assumes cached KV entries remain in-distribution. This assumption fails beyond the training horizon: nothing constrains the construction of KV entries during rollout, giving rise to the KV-provenance problem where cached entries themselves become out-of-distribution (OOD). To address this, we propose In-Distribution Forcing (ID-Forcing), a test-time framework that aligns both KV caching and KV conditioning with training configurations. Its key mechanism, self-caching, prevents OOD KV entries at their source. Each chunk is cached without attending to prior KV entry, keeping the rolling window exactly in-distribution. Consequently, ID-Forcing seamlessly extends short-horizon models to minute-scale video generation. Extensive evaluations show that our method remains competitive on standard video generation benchmark while substantially outperforming prior work in mitigating drifting, as validated by both our drift metrics and a user study.

</details>


### [51] [Foresight: planning future perception in streaming VLMs without retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03123)
*Ashok Prasad Neupane,Dipan Bartaula,Ankit Belbase,Saugat Adhikari,Samip Ghimire,Saroj Poudel,Binod Bhattarai,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FORESIGHT 双流架构，利用流式 VLM 的“未来预判”能力在推理时以免训练方式动态重配置计算：一个 LLM 处理流入 token，另一个 Siamese LLM 提前预判未来上下文并生成计算计划，经 schema 引导解码与 diff 更新在线执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视觉语言模型的计算路径在推理期间固定不变，无法适应不断变化的场景动态，而不同未来事件需要不同层次和形式的感知，因此需要让计算随流自适应。

Method: FORESIGHT 采用双流架构：两个共享权重、输入编码器与 KV cache 的 Siamese LLM。第一个持续处理到来的 token，第二个跑在流之前进行未来上下文预判、计算规划与任务响应生成，且不打断流式推理。计划决定何时再推理、届时检查什么、以多密采样，并将瞬态证据与持久控制分离。计划通过 schema 引导解码与轻量 diff 更新的重配置协议在线执行。

Result: 在冻结的 Qwen3-VL-8B 上，OmniPro 在线评测取得 23.0 平均联合 F1，比最强已训练基线高 9.5%；StreamingBench 提升 6.7，OVO-Bench 提升 15.4，证据在视频流中较晚出现时增益最大达 18.7。

Conclusion: 流式 VLM 天然具备对近期未来的预判能力，可据此在免训练条件下动态配置未来计算，兼顾可靠预判、与流式推理并发的规划以及低开销在线重配置。

Abstract: Existing streaming vision-language models (VLMs) continuously perceive and reason over visual streams, but their computational pathways remain fixed throughout inference. Consequently, they cannot adapt computation to evolving scene dynamics, where different future events demand different levels and forms of perception. We show that streaming VLMs inherently possess the ability to anticipate the immediate future, and leverage this capability to dynamically configure future computation in a training-free manner. Realizing such anticipatory computation, however, is very challenging: future anticipation must be sufficiently reliable to guide computation, planning must run concurrently with streaming inference, and online reconfiguration must incur negligible overhead. To address these challenges, we introduce FORESIGHT, a dual-stream architecture comprising two Siamese LLMs with shared weights, input encoders, and KV cache. The first LLM continuously processes incoming tokens, while the second runs ahead of the stream to anticipate future context, plan future computation, and generate task responses without interrupting streaming inference. Each plan decides when to reason next, what to check then, and how densely to sample, keeping transient evidence separate from persistent control. The resulting computation plan is executed online through an efficient reconfiguration protocol with schemaguided decoding and lightweight diff-based updates, enabling dynamic adaptation with low overhead. With a frozen Qwen3-VL-8B backbone, FORESIGHT achieves 23.0 mean joint F1 on OmniPro Online evaluation beating strongest trained baseline by 9.5%, while improving the backbone by 6.7 on StreamingBench and 15.4 on OVO-Bench, with the largest gain of 18.7 when evidence arrives later in the video stream. Our source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [52] [Behavior Pack Optimization for Video MLLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03141)
*Zhaolu Kang,Shiyu Liu,Tailong Luo,Wei Zhang,Yingjie He,Lei Wei,Guansu Wang,Liang He,Siheng Wang,Guangyuan Dong,Jiaqi Su,Shuang Chen,Haoyu Ji,Qishi Zhan,Kaiyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视频多模态大模型依赖外观与语言先验而非时序证据，作者提出行为包优化（BPO），用反事实视图下的输出包联合评分并采用锚点相对优势，在多个视频问答基准上显著提升时序鲁棒性与弃答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频多模态大模型在视频问答基准上分数不断攀升，但打乱帧序、遮蔽关键证据段或遮挡目标物体几乎不改变预测结果，说明准确率来自外观与语言先验而非问题所要求的时序证据。作者将根源归因于后训练的基本单元：奖励仅针对原始片段的单个回答计算，模型从未被要求在不同视图间保持一致行为。

Method: 提出行为包优化（BPO）：用按问题类型选择的反事实视图下的一组输出构成"行为包"替代单次回答，并进行联合评分。包奖励要求：干预无关时保持稳定、关键证据被移除时表现敏感、证据完全缺失时弃答。为在小包规模下稳定优化，采用锚点相对优势——以原始视图上的回答作为每个提示的参考，而非在混合视图上取组均值。

Result: 在 TempCompass、MVBench、NExT-QA 上，相比同一起点 SFT 检查点、预算匹配的 vanilla GRPO 基线，BPO 使 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 的宏平均准确率提升 4.7 个百分点，时序困难子集提升 7.8 个百分点，弃答 F1 提升 20.0 个百分点。增益可迁移至 Video-MME、LongVideoBench 以及 LLaVA-Video-7B；消融证实增益来自视图集而非 rollout 次数。

Conclusion: BPO 以包级视角重构视频多模态后训练目标，使模型行为与问题所需的时序证据对齐，提升鲁棒性与弃答能力且具可迁移性。作者希望该包级视角为视频 MLLM 与多模态后训练社区迈向证据落地（evidence-grounded）的视频推理提供有用起点。

Abstract: Video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) keep climbing video question answering benchmarks, yet shuffling the frames, masking the segment that supports the answer, or occluding the target object barely changes their predictions. The accuracy rests on appearance and language priors, not on the temporal evidence the question asks for. We trace this to the unit of post-training: rewards are computed on a single response to the original clip, so the model is never asked to behave consistently across views. We propose Behavior Pack Optimization (BPO), which replaces the single response with a behavior pack of outputs across counterfactual views chosen by question type, scored jointly. The pack reward asks for stability when the intervention is irrelevant, sensitivity when key evidence is removed, and abstention when no evidence remains. To keep this objective stable at small pack sizes, BPO uses an anchor-relative advantage: the response on the original view serves as a per-prompt reference instead of a group mean over mixed views. On TempCompass, MVBench, and NExT-QA, BPO improves the macro accuracy of Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct by 4.7 pp, the temporal-hard subset by 7.8 pp, and abstention F1 by 20.0 pp over a budget-matched vanilla GRPO baseline from the same SFT checkpoint. The gains transfer to Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and to LLaVA-Video-7B; ablations confirm they follow the view sets, not the rollout count. We hope this pack-level perspective offers a useful starting point for the video MLLM and multimodal post-training community as the field moves toward evidence-grounded video reasoning.

</details>


### [53] [CalCErt: Bin-wise Certification of Confidence Calibration in Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03142)
*Leo Fillioux,Stergios Christodoulidis,Stergios Christodoulidis,Maria Vakalopoulou,Jose Dolz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CalCErt：一种事后认证方法，用经验校准估计、统计集中界与置信函数局部Lipschitz估计，为任意预训练可微分类器提供ℓ2半径R内逐bin置信校准的最坏情况误校准上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗鲁棒性认证方法只保证预测类别不变，忽视了攻击下置信度/不确定性校准行为的变化，而校准被破坏会直接误导下游决策。

Method: CalCErt为post-hoc策略：结合经验校准估计、统计集中不等式（置信区间）与置信函数的局部Lipschitz常数，推导数据依赖的、R-ℓ2球内最坏情况误校准上界；逐bin认证校准，无需重训练。

Result: 在11个医学图像分类任务及多种对抗扰动上评测，认证覆盖率显著高于基线策略，紧致度保持有竞争力；开源代码。

Conclusion: 将对抗认证从"类别保持"扩展到"校准保持"，提供了可扩展的事后校准认证工具，主要验证场景为医学图像分类。

Abstract: Deep neural networks remain vulnerable to adversarial perturbations, which can distort not only predictions but also confidence scores, undermining uncertainty calibration. While existing certification methods focus on preserving the predicted category, providing guarantees on how calibration behaves under adversarial attacks remains overlooked. In this work, we introduce CalCErt, a simple and efficient post-hoc strategy that certifies bin-wise confidence calibration for any pretrained differentiable classifier. Our approach combines empirical calibration estimates, statistical concentration bounds, and local Lipschitz estimates of the confidence function to derive data-dependent upper bounds on worst-case miscalibration within an $ell_2$-ball of radius R. We evaluate CalCErt across 11 medical image classification tasks and multiple adversarial perturbations, demonstrating substantially higher certified coverage than baseline strategies while maintaining competitive tightness. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [54] [Does Physics Live in the Activations? Localizing Physical Quantities in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03154)
*Jonas Kneifl,Jakub Skalski,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Kamil Deja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过线性探针研究视频扩散 Transformer（DiT）内部表征是否编码仿真器导出的物理量（运动学与刚体动力学），发现物理量在去噪早期即可高精度线性解码，且信息定位在物体 token 上、跨帧量可从单帧隐变量读出，探针还具部分外推能力并可转为调控向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型日益被当作世界模型使用，但基准显示其物理推理能力存在明显缺陷。核心疑问是：这些模型是否真正内化了物理规律，还是仅复现了熟悉的运动模式。作者希望通过探测内部表征来区分"内化物理原理"与"模仿运动模式"这两种可能。

Method: 以视频 Diffusion Transformer 为对象，用仿真器生成的运动学与刚体动力学（重力、接触）真值物理量作为标签，对去噪过程各步的激活做线性探针解码；设置从模型自身含噪隐变量直接解码的基线；分析激活在 token 序列上的空间定位（物体 token、单帧隐变量读取多帧物理量）；在场景变化与物体配置变化上测试探针外推；并在全分辨率激活空间重新拟合探针方向作为调控向量。

Result: 这些物理量在去噪早期即可被高精度线性解码，显著优于直接从含噪隐变量解码的基线，说明物理信息是在去噪过程中被主动构造而非输入自带；关键信息集中在物体 token 上，且多帧定义的物理量可从单帧隐变量读出，即"全局计算、局部存储"；探针表现出部分外推能力，可迁移到训练分布之外的场景与物体配置；全分辨率激活空间拟合的探针方向可作为调控向量改变模型输出。

Conclusion: 视频 DiT 内部确实编码了可线性解码的物理量，且是在去噪中主动构造并局部存储的，并非仅拟合场景相关性；这些方向还可用于干预生成结果，为理解与改进视频世界模型的物理一致性提供了可解释性与调控工具。

Abstract: Video generation models produce strikingly realistic sequences and are increasingly proposed as world models, yet recent benchmarks reveal pronounced deficits in their physical reasoning. This raises the question of whether these models internalize physical principles or merely reproduce familiar motion patterns. We address this by probing internal representations of video Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for simulator-derived ground-truth physical quantities spanning kinematic motion and rigid-body dynamics under gravity and contact. We find that these quantities are linearly decodable with high accuracy early in the denoising process, substantially outperforming a baseline decoded directly from the model's own noised latents, indicating that the relevant physical information is actively constructed during denoising rather than already present in the input. Additionally, we show that activations at on-object tokens carry the relevant physical information and that quantities defined over multiple frames are readable from single latent frames. Hence, information is sharply localized within the token sequence and is computed globally but stored locally. The probes further show partial extrapolation, transferring to scene variations and object configurations outside their training regime, so what they read is not simply a correlate of the scenes they were fit on. When fitted directly in the full-resolution activation space, the probing directions can serve as steering vectors to change the model's output.

</details>


### [55] [Geometry-Aligned Semantic Matching for Cross-Modal Planar Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03167)
*Zhiwei Wang,Defeng He,Yuxing Li,Meilu Zhu,Edmund Y. Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CDPM 跨模态图像匹配框架：先通过几何一致的跨模态 patch 对微调 DINOv3 使语义相似度反映真实几何对应，再构建 DINO-Centric 特征金字塔，以多尺度 DINO 主导对应估计、轻量 CNN 分支辅助局部细节精化。在三个跨模态数据集上优于 RoMa 与 RoMa v2，且 FLOPs 减少 45.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态（如可见光-红外）图像匹配需兼顾跨模态一致性与几何精度：纯语义表征虽跨模态一致，但语义相似不等价于几何对应；细粒度 CNN 特征虽定位精确，却缺乏全局跨模态语义引导，难以稳定精化。因此需要让几何一致的语义表征在对应估计中保持主导地位。

Method: 1) 用几何一致的跨模态 patch 对渐进式适配 DINOv3，使特征相似度更好反映真实跨模态空间对应；2) 构建 DINO-Centric 特征金字塔：多尺度 DINO 表征维持稳定的跨模态对应，轻量 CNN 分支提供辅助结构细节用于局部精化；3) 在整个细粒度定位过程中保持 DINO 语义表征在对应估计中的主导作用。为稠密匹配器（dense matcher）范式。

Result: 在三个跨模态数据集上验证。VIS-IR 上相较 RoMa：AUC@3/5/10/20 分别提升 7.36、13.40、13.75、10.42 个百分点，mACE 由 5.83 降至 2.78 像素；在所有指标上超过 RoMa v2，同时 FLOPs 减少 45.6%。已提供在线 demo 与数据集，代码将开源。

Conclusion: 跨模态匹配的关键在于让几何一致的语义表征主导对应估计，而非仅依赖语义相似或局部 CNN 细节；该思路在精度与效率上均优于现有稠密匹配器，并附带数据集与开源计划。

Abstract: Cross-modal image matching establishes stable and accurate geometric correspondences across modalities for planar registration. Existing semantic representations provide cross-modal consistency, but semantic similarity does not necessarily imply geometric correspondence. Meanwhile, fine-grained CNN features provide accurate local details but lack global cross-modal semantic guidance for stable refinement. To address these issues, we propose CDPM, which first establishes geometrically consistent semantic representations and then preserves their dominant role in correspondence estimation during fine-grained localization. Specifically, we progressively adapt DINOv3 using geometrically consistent cross-modal patch pairs, enabling feature similarity to better reflect true cross-modal spatial correspondences. We then construct a DINO-Centric Feature Pyramid, where multi-scale DINO representations maintain stable cross-modal correspondences, while a lightweight CNN branch provides auxiliary structural details for precise local refinement. Extensive experiments on three cross-modal datasets demonstrate the superior performance of CDPM. On VIS-IR, compared with the dense matcher RoMa, CDPM improves AUC@3/5/10/20 by 7.36, 13.40, 13.75, and 10.42 percentage points, respectively, and reduces mACE from 5.83 to 2.78 pixels. It also outperforms RoMa v2 across all metrics while requiring 45.6% fewer FLOPs. The online demo and dataset are available, and the code will be released on our project page at this https URL .

</details>


### [56] [PocketSplat: Mobile Gaussian Reconstruction via World-Space Latent Allocatio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03192)
*Wenzhi Guo,Xianda Chen,Dongxuan Chen,Guangchi Fang,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PocketSplat：面向移动端预算约束的前馈高斯资产生成框架，按整数容量分配在世界空间组织潜变量候选，仅解码保留候选的完整高斯属性，在 iPhone 上实时生成紧凑高质量资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动高斯重建需同时满足两个条件：模型须在设备资源包络内运行；产出的高斯资产表示大小须适配下游移动端使用。现有前馈重建器通常解码稠密、与图像对齐的候选，最终基数由输入分辨率与视角数隐式决定，无法控制资产规模。

Method: 提出 PocketSplat：给定输出预算后，在预测世界空间组织稠密的几何感知潜变量候选，在局部潜变量单元上分配精确整数容量，仅对保留候选解码完整高斯属性；采用单元条件潜变量融合在解码前聚合多视角重复证据，并用空间责任解码在局部稀疏化后自适应调整高斯支撑范围。

Result: 在 DL3DV 与分布外基准上，相对前馈高斯重建基线取得更优的质量—预算折衷。在 Mip-NeRF 360 上可直接运行于目标 iPhone，构建的紧凑高斯资产质量更高、速度显著快于可部署的流式 MVSplat 变体；原生 MVSplat 与 DepthSplat 则超出设备内存预算。

Conclusion: PocketSplat 通过预算化候选分配与按需解码，实现了资源受限设备上可控规模的前馈高斯资产构建，兼顾质量与效率，为移动端 3D 重建提供可行路径。

Abstract: Mobile Gaussian reconstruction must satisfy two requirements: the reconstruction model must execute within a device resource envelope, and the resulting Gaussian asset must expose a representation size suited to downstream mobile use. Existing feed-forward Gaussian reconstructors commonly decode dense, image-aligned candidates whose final cardinality is implicitly determined by the input resolution and number of views. We present PocketSplat, a feed-forward framework for budgeted mobile Gaussian asset construction. Given a prescribed output budget, PocketSplat organizes dense geometry-aware latent candidates in predicted world space, allocates exact integer capacity across local latent cells, and decodes complete Gaussian attributes only for retained candidates. Cell-conditioned latent fusion aggregates repeated multi-view evidence before decoding, while spatial responsibility decoding adapts Gaussian support after local sparsification. Experiments on DL3DV and out-of-distribution benchmarks establish a strong quality--budget trade-off against feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction baselines. On Mip-NeRF 360, PocketSplat executes directly on a target iPhone and constructs compact, higher-quality Gaussian assets substantially faster than a deployable streamed MVSplat variant; native MVSplat and DepthSplat exceed the device memory budget.

</details>


### [57] [Bridging Research and Practice: A Systematic Evaluation of Generalist and Dermatology-Specific Models in Clinical Skin Lesion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03193)
*Emanoel dos Santos,Kelvin Cunha,Rodrigo Mota,Fabio Papais,Thales Bezerra,Natalia Lopes,Erico Medeiros,Shirley Cruz,Jessica Araujo,Paulo Borba,Tsang Ing Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作对近期皮肤科数据集上的多种架构进行基准评测，涵盖皮肤镜与智能手机临床照片，比较通用/医学视觉语言模型、基础模型与任务专用皮肤科分类器在分布偏移、模态变化和人群差异下的鲁棒性，量化当前最优模型与临床部署需求之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤科机器学习已从概念验证走向潜在应用，但临床诊断模糊、皮损变异性高，且成像模态、设备质量与患者人群差异大，阻碍了稳健、安全且公平的临床模型开发。因此亟需系统评估当代模型在异构数据源上的泛化能力，以支撑临床转化。

Method: 在近期皮肤科数据集上对一组多样化架构进行基准评测，数据跨越皮肤镜图像与智能手机临床照片；评估近期通用与医学视觉语言模型、基础模型的鲁棒性，并与任务专用皮肤科分类器（含基于嵌入的方法与卷积神经网络）进行对比。

Result: 给出了模型在分布偏移、模态变化和人群差异条件下的性能评估，量化了当前最先进模型与临床部署要求之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该研究有助于推动可靠、可及且临床可用的皮肤科AI系统的开发，为临床落地提供评测依据。

Abstract: The application of machine learning to dermatology has grown substantially in recent years, moving beyond proof-of-concept studies toward potential applications. However, clinical dermatology remains a challenging and still open problem. Diagnostic assessment is often ambiguous, and skin lesions exhibit high variability, compounded by differences in acquisition modality, device quality, and patient demographics. These factors hinder the development of robust models suitable for safe and equitable clinical use. To support translation into practice, it is essential to systematically evaluate how contemporary models generalize across heterogeneous data sources. In this work, we benchmark a diverse set of architectures on recent dermatology datasets, spanning dermoscopic images and smartphone-based clinical photographs. We assess the robustness of recent general-purpose and medical vision-language models, as well as foundation models, and compare them against task-specific dermatology classifiers, including embedding-based approaches and convolutional neural networks. Our study provides an evaluation of model performance under distribution shifts, modality changes, and demographic variability. By quantifying the gap between current state-of-the-art models and the requirements of clinical deployment, we aim to contribute to the development of reliable, accessible, and clinically applicable AI systems for dermatology.

</details>


### [58] [Contextual Flow Matching: Adaptive Step Selection in Flow Models for Efficient Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03202)
*Divya Jyoti Bajpai,Arun Verma,Manjesh Kumar Hanawal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出COFLOW：一种推理时、上下文感知的自适应步数选择方法，无需重训练生成模型即可加速Flow Matching的图像/视频生成，并给出O(1/K)前向欧拉离散化误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching能高质量生成，但推理需多次顺序函数评估，成本高。现有加速方法要么引入额外训练开销，要么牺牲质量，且未考虑输入相关的可变性。

Method: 基于prompt特征的上下文感知自适应步数选择；在线训练一个无监督奖励，平衡推理效率与生成保真度；即插即用，不重训练底层生成模型；并给出前向欧拉离散化的O(1/K)误差界理论分析。

Result: 在图像与视频生成上实现超过2.5倍加速，同时保持感知与语义质量。

Conclusion: COFLOW展示了推理时自适应步数选择的可行性，兼顾效率与质量，且具备理论支撑，可泛化到图像和视频生成任务。

Abstract: Flow Matching enables high-quality visual generation via continuous-time dynamics, but inference remains costly due to multiple sequential function evaluations. Existing acceleration methods reduce the number of function evaluations but often introduce additional training overhead, degrade quality, or fail to account for input-dependent variability. We propose COFLOW, an inference-time method that adaptively selects the step counts each generation based on the prompt features. Our context-aware COFLOW is trained online with an unsupervised reward that balances inference efficiency and generation fidelity. Our method is plug-and-play, requiring no retraining of the underlying generative model. It generalizes to image and video generation, achieving over 2.5x speedup while preserving perceptual and semantic quality. We further provide a theoretical analysis establishing an O(1/K) forward-Euler discretization error bound under standard regularity conditions.

</details>


### [59] [VisionMX: Unlocking Microscaling Post-Training Quantization for Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03218)
*Elad Dror Cohen,Ofir Gordon,Lior Dikstein,Idan Achituve,Hai Victor Habi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究视觉模型的后训练Microscaling (MX)量化，识别出三类误差来源（块缩放表征、小卷积权张量与元素网格错配、非负激活未充分利用有符号码），并提出VisionMX方法：有界权重量化+可折叠仿射激活校正，在分类、检测、分割、低光增强任务上超越直接转换与已有PTQ基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MX格式已获硬件支持用于高效训练与推理，但其对视觉模型的影响尚缺乏系统研究；直接转换存在明显性能损失，需要针对视觉任务的量化方法。

Method: 分析直接MX转换的三类误差来源；提出VisionMX后训练量化方法，包含有界权重舍入优化（bounded weight rounding）与可折叠的仿射激活校正（foldable affine correction），在多种MX格式下评估。

Result: 在图像分类、目标检测、语义分割与低光图像增强任务上，VisionMX优于直接转换与所评估的PTQ基线，在对MX转换最敏感的架构上取得最大性能恢复。

Conclusion: VisionMX为视觉模型提供了有效的后训练MX量化方案，验证了误差分析与针对性校正的有效性。

Abstract: Microscaling (MX) formats are emerging as a hardware-supported approach to efficient training and inference. They combine low-precision elements with shared block scales, but their impact on vision models remains underexplored. We systematically investigate post-training MX quantization across vision models and tasks. An analysis of direct conversion identifies three sources of error: block-scale representation, the poor alignment of some small convolutional weight tensors with nonuniform element grids, and the underuse of signed codes by nonnegative activations. These findings motivate VisionMX, a post-training MX quantization method that optimizes bounded weight rounding and applies a foldable affine correction to activations. We evaluate VisionMX across image classification, object detection, semantic segmentation, and low-light image enhancement using several MX-style formats. It improves on direct conversion and the evaluated post-training quantization baselines, with the largest performance recoveries in architectures most sensitive to MX conversion

</details>


### [60] [VDOT++: Unified Few-Step Video Generation via Unbalanced Optimal Transport Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03221)
*Yutong Wang,Xingtong Ge,Enhuai Liu,Yunke Wang,Tianfan Xue,Yu Qiao,Yaohui Wang,Xinyuan Chen,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VDOT++：统一视频扩散蒸馏框架，用非对称非平衡最优传输+ℓ1加权中位数，对T2V/I2V/条件生成分别蒸馏出四步生成器，性能逼近多步教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流匹配视频生成模型采样需反复前向大骨干，成本高。分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）虽降本，但其反向KL在师生分布重叠有限时指导不稳定、不完整。已有VDOT引入最优传输蒸馏（OTD）提供几何方向，但平衡OTD对每对师生帧的空间token做全质量匹配，这一假设在T2V和I2V中不成立：同一条件允许多种合法输出，空间内容跨不同实现无需对齐。

Method: 1）非对称非平衡OTD：允许不可靠的学生token承载更少质量，同时保持对教师token的覆盖，从而对输出多样性鲁棒；2）ℓ1地面代价替换均值式聚合，采用更保模态的加权中位数，抑制远端传输目标影响；二者分别决定"匹配谁"与"如何聚合选中目标"。3）通过顺序反向传播结合分布匹配与对抗精修；4）利用解耦的score网络做跨尺度蒸馏，用更大score网络提升紧凑生成器。同一训练配方分别应用于三类任务的生成器。

Result: 在UVCBench、VBench、VBench-I2V、VACE四个基准上，所得四步生成器在T2V、I2V、条件生成三个任务族中均与多步教师及强少步基线具竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过对OTD的"非平衡匹配+模态保持聚合"改造，配合对抗精修与跨尺度蒸馏，可在三类视频生成任务上统一地把扩散采样压缩到四步且保持质量。

Abstract: Video creation spans text-to-video (T2V), image-to-video (I2V), and condition-based generation, yet video diffusion models remain costly because they repeatedly evaluate large backbones during sampling. Distribution matching distillation (DMD) reduces this cost, but its reverse Kullback--Leibler (KL) objective can provide unstable or incomplete guidance when the student and teacher distributions have limited overlap. VDOT addressed this issue by adding optimal transport distillation (OTD), whose explicit coupling supplies geometric directions for condition-based generation. Balanced OTD, however, performs full-mass matching between the spatial tokens of each corresponding student--teacher frame pair. This assumption weakens for T2V and I2V, where one condition admits many valid outputs and spatial content need not align across different realizations. We present VDOT++, a unified distillation framework that applies the same training recipe separately to generators for the three task families. It makes OTD robust to output diversity through an asymmetric unbalanced formulation that allows unreliable student tokens to carry less mass while maintaining coverage of the teacher tokens. An $\ell_1$ ground cost further replaces mean-based aggregation with a more mode-preserving weighted median that limits the influence of distant transport targets. The two changes respectively determine whom to match and how the selected targets should be aggregated. We additionally combine distribution matching and adversarial refinement through sequential backward passes, and exploit the decoupled score networks for cross-scale distillation, where larger score networks improve a compact generator. Experiments on UVCBench, VBench, VBench-I2V, and the VACE benchmark show that the resulting four-step generators are competitive with many-step teachers and strong few-step baselines across all three task families.

</details>


### [61] [Uncertainty as a Proxy for Semantic Correctness in Diffusion-Based Medical Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03224)
*Yuxuan Ou,Konstantinos Kamnitsas,OxAAA Study,AICT Consortium,Regent Lee,Vicente Grau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究扩散模型NCCT→CECT合成中，不确定性能否作为解剖/语义正确性的代理指标。以AortaDiff（联合生成CECT+管腔分割）为平台，比较6种不确定性方法，在像素/区域/图像三级尺度及OOD检测上评估。MCDropout表现最佳且零额外训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型可从无对比剂CT合成增强CT，避免对比剂注射的成本与风险，但视觉逼真的图像未必解剖正确，而PSNR/SSIM等像素与特征相似度指标并不直接度量解剖正确性，因此需要一种能反映语义正确性的质量信号。

Method: 采用AortaDiff多任务扩散框架，联合生成CECT与管腔分割，用分割导出的误差作为生成正确性的量化指标。对比覆盖权重空间（Ensemble、HyperDiff、BayesDiff）、架构扰动（MCDropout）、生成随机性（RDS）与输入扰动（TTA）六类不确定性方法，在像素、区域、图像三级尺度及临床相关OOD病例检测上评估，并在外部多中心数据集上验证分布偏移下的表现。

Result: 不确定性在三个空间尺度上均具信息量，在外部多中心数据集分布偏移下仍保持有效性，并支持OOD检测。MCDropout在各尺度均位居前列、外部泛化好，且对任何已用dropout训练的模型可在推理时启用，无额外训练成本。不确定性可可靠标记严重失败，但在已高质量图像间区分能力弱。

Conclusion: 不确定性可作为NCCT→CECT合成中质量筛选、可靠性评估与OOD检测的实用且计算经济的信号，但主要用于筛除严重失败而非细粒度质量排序。

Abstract: Diffusion models can synthesise contrast-enhanced CT (CECT) from non-contrast CT (NCCT), avoiding contrast administration and its environmental and patient-access costs. However, visually realistic images are not necessarily anatomically correct, and the pixel-intensity and feature-space similarity metrics used to assess generation quality do not directly measure anatomical correctness. In this work, we investigate whether uncertainty can serve as a proxy for semantic correctness in diffusion-based medical image synthesis. We study NCCT-to-CECT synthesis using AortaDiff, a multitask diffusion framework that jointly generates CECT images and lumen segmentations. The segmentation output provides an explicit representation of the generated vascular anatomy, enabling segmentation-derived errors to be used as a quantitative measure of generation correctness. Six methods spanning weight (Ensemble, HyperDiff, BayesDiff), architecture-perturbation (MCDropout), generative-stochasticity (RDS) and input-perturbation (TTA) uncertainty are compared at the pixel, region and image levels, and for detection of clinically relevant out-of-distribution (OOD) cases. Uncertainty proves informative at all three spatial scales, remains informative on an external multi-centre dataset under distribution shift, and supports OOD detection. MCDropout stands out among the six: it ranks among the leading methods at every scale, generalizes well on the external dataset, and can be enabled at inference on any model already trained with dropout, so reliable uncertainty comes at no extra training cost. Uncertainty reliably flags severe failures but discriminates poorly among already high-quality images. These findings support uncertainty as a practical and computationally economical signal for quality filtering, reliability assessment and OOD detection in NCCT-to CECT synthesis.

</details>


### [62] [EmbPASS: Towards Cross-Embodiment Open Panoramic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03248)
*Pujun Guo,Yuanfan Zheng,Fei Teng,Mengfei Duan,Guoqiang Zhao,Yuheng Zhang,Kai Luo,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨具身开放全景分割新任务Cross-Embodiment Open Panoramic Segmentation，构建覆盖车、无人机、可穿戴、四足的跨平台全景语义分割基准EmbPASS，并提出EPONet网络（RAMA+CAST）实现平台均衡的开放词汇全景分割，mIoU 35.82%，超最强基线1.10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景图像提供360°完整视场，有利于具身感知的场景理解；但不同具身平台的观测视角与空间布局差异显著，产生跨具身观测偏移，威胁全景感知的一致性与可靠性，而该问题的系统性研究仍很有限。

Method: 1) 定义新任务Cross-Embodiment Open Panoramic Segmentation；2) 构建EmbPASS基准，涵盖Vehicle、Drone、Wearable、Quadruped四类平台，统一语义分类体系；3) 提出EPONet开放词汇全景语义分割网络，包含Relation-Aware Metric Adapter (RAMA) 增强异构观测下的空间建模，以及Content-Adaptive Semantic Transfer (CAST) 增强语义迁移。

Result: 在EmbPASS上取得最佳平台均衡性能35.82% mIoU，超最强基线1.10%；在现有全景分割基准上保持竞争力。代码与基准将公开。

Conclusion: 该工作将跨具身差异引入全景开放词汇分割，通过基准与适配方法推动一致、可靠的多平台具身全景感知研究。

Abstract: Panoramic images provide a complete 360-degree field of view, enabling comprehensive scene understanding for embodied perception. However, heterogeneous embodied platforms exhibit substantial differences in observation viewpoints and spatial layouts, giving rise to cross-embodiment observation shifts that pose additional challenges to consistent and reliable panoramic perception, while systematic studies of this problem remain limited. To bridge this gap, we introduce a new task, termed Cross-Embodiment Open Panoramic Segmentation. Meanwhile, we establish EmbPASS, a multi-platform panoramic semantic segmentation benchmark spanning Vehicle, Drone, Wearable, and Quadruped platforms under a unified semantic taxonomy, providing a testbed for systematically studying cross-embodiment panoramic perception. We further propose EPONet, an open-vocabulary panoramic semantic segmentation network that integrates Relation-Aware Metric Adapter (RAMA) and Content-Adaptive Semantic Transfer (CAST) to enhance spatial modeling and semantic transfer under heterogeneous embodied observations. Extensive experiments show that EPONet achieves the best platform-balanced performance on EmbPASS with 35.82% mIoU, outperforming the strongest baseline by 1.10%, while remaining competitive on existing panoramic segmentation benchmarks. The source code and EmbPASS benchmark will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [63] [COSMI: COmpositional Synthesis of Multi-object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03252)
*Daniel Eskandar,Ilya A. Petrov,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 COSMI 数据集与方法：通过组合单物体交互片段（镜像平衡手别、跨身体迁移、语言模型+几何校验筛选合理配对），以组合方式扩充多物体人-物交互数据，构建 222k 序列/275 小时、最多五物体的数据集，并训练文本到交互的扩散 Transformer 生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多物体人-物交互数据稀缺：日常活动涉及多个物体，但现有采集数据集多只记录单个物体，多物体同步采集成本随组合数呈指数增长。作者观察到交互具有局部性，因此单物体采集片段中实际已包含多物体活动所需的部分，可通过组合方式构造数据。

Method: 数据侧：将接触一致的单一交互片段进行镜像以平衡左右手、跨身体迁移，并用语言模型与几何检查筛选语义与物理上合理的配对，使数据量随片段组合而非记录时长组合式增长。模型侧：训练 COSMI——一个文本到交互的扩散 Transformer，遵循数据构造方式，采用权重共享的物体槽生成可变数量物体，物体运动相对驱动其运动的身体部位进行预测。

Result: COSMI 数据集含 222k 序列、275 小时、最多五个物体，规模约为最大多物体采集数据集的近 30 倍，并可通过新增数据集甚至手-物录制继续扩展。在含未见物体与未见交互组合的基准上，基于该数据训练的模型能泛化到未见组合；COSMI 在文本对齐与接触准确性上优于基线，且在未见物体上优势最大。

Conclusion: 将局部交互片段组合生成多物体交互数据是可行且可扩展的路径，可显著扩大数据规模并支撑生成模型对未见物体与未见交互组合的泛化；代码、模型与数据管线将公开。

Abstract: Generative models of human-object interaction are bounded by the data that exists: everyday activities involve several objects, but most captured datasets record one at a time, as multi-object capture is combinatorially expensive. Our observation is that interactions are local, so single-object captures already contain the parts of multi-object activities. We compose them: contact-consistent clips of single interactions, mirrored to balance the hands, transfer between bodies, and a language model and geometric checks admit only the pairings that are plausible, semantically and physically. Therefore, the dataset grows combinatorially with the clips rather than recording time. The COSMI dataset holds 222k sequences and 275 hours with up to five objects, nearly thirty times the largest multi-object capture, and can be extended by adding datasets or even hand-object recordings. On this data we train the COSMI method, a text-to-interaction diffusion transformer that follows how the data is built: weight-shared object slots generate a variable number of objects, predicted relative to the body parts that move them. On a benchmark with an unseen object and unseen interaction combinations, models trained on the dataset generalize to the unseen combinations. COSMI outperforms baselines in text alignment and contact accuracy, where its margin is largest on the unseen object. Code, models, and the dataset pipeline will be released on the project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [64] [Consecutive Posterior Fusion for Diffusive Recovery of Unobservable Image Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03261)
*Elena Morotti,Davide Evangelista,Elena Loli Piccolomini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CPF-DDNM，一种免训练的扩散后验采样推理策略，通过融合相邻两步的测量感知估计来改善严重病态逆问题中不可观测结构的恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散后验采样器仅依赖瞬时测量感知估计，未显式利用先前后验校正所携带的信息；严重病态成像逆问题中不可观测/弱约束结构难以恢复。

Method: 在 DDNM 的 range/null-space 分解框架下融合连续两步的测量感知估计：保持测量决定分量不变，仅作用于先验驱动的 null-space 估计；给出几何解释与局部误差分析，推导最优时变融合系数（含外推区间），无需重训练或额外去噪器评估。

Result: 在稀疏视角 CT、模拟低剂量 CT 及医学图像超分辨率任务上，相较 DDNM 取得一致提升，并与扩散类逆问题求解器性能相当。

Conclusion: 连续后验融合可作为即插即用的推理时增强，为扩散后验采样提供理论刻画与实用改进。

Abstract: Solving severely ill-posed imaging inverse problems requires recovering image structures that are unobservable or weakly constrained by the measurements. Diffusion models provide expressive learned priors for inferring such missing information, while posterior sampling incorporates measurement consistency along the reverse process. Standard diffusion posterior samplers, however, rely on instantaneous measurement-aware estimates, without explicitly exploiting information carried by previous posterior corrections. We introduce Consecutive Posterior Fusion Denoising Diffusion Null-Space Models (CPF-DDNM), an inference-time strategy that fuses consecutive measurement-aware estimates to improve the diffusive recovery of unobservable image structures, without requiring retraining or additional denoiser evaluations. We instantiate this principle within DDNM, whose range/null-space decomposition reveals that consecutive fusion preserves the measurement-determined component while acting exclusively on the prior-driven null-space estimate. We thus provide a geometric interpretation of CPF-DDNM and a local error analysis that characterizes the optimal time-dependent fusion coefficient, including the extrapolative regime. Experiments on sparse-view and simulated low-dose computed tomography, as well as medical image super-resolution, show consistent improvements over DDNM and competitive performance against diffusion-based inverse solvers.

</details>


### [65] [Moving Forward with Video Saliency: A New Dataset and Benchmark where Motion Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03276)
*Susmit Agrawal,Rebecca Wanner,Juliane Verwiebe,Matthias Tangemann,Matthias Bethge,Matthias Kümmerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文验证了"视频显著性预测是否真正需要时序信息"这一质疑仍成立，并提出新基准 SalTempto（224 个高动态片段、最多 16 名被试、含训练划分）。在 SalTempto 上静态基线仅恢复约 13% 的超出 centerbias 的 headroom（LEDOV 上超过一半），而微调后的时序模型显著优于静态基线，说明该基准能测出真正的时间信息，但最强模型仍留下近一半 headroom 未解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频显著性预测通常被认为必须利用跨帧的时序活动，但先前工作表明在 LEDOV 上静态基线即可恢复大部分可解释注视信息，且视频模型与静态基线失败位置一致。作者想弄清：这种边际增益究竟源于当前时序架构的能力局限，还是基准本身缺乏时间动态性。

Method: 首先在更可靠的金标准（gold standard）与更新的近期架构面板下复现并验证原有诊断；随后构建 SalTempto 基准：从 HACS-Segments 数据集选取 224 个高动态视频片段，每个片段包含一个事件及其前因与后续，采集最多 16 名被试的注视数据，并提供训练划分以适配预训练模型；最后对比 centerbias、微调静态基线与微调时序架构的表现，并分析模型未能捕捉的人类注视倾向。

Result: 在原分析框架下，即使使用更强的金标准和更新的模型面板，面板中最强的时序架构相对微调静态基线仍无实质性提升，说明原诊断依旧成立。在 SalTempto 上，静态基线仅恢复约 13% 的超出 centerbias 的 headroom，而 LEDOV 上超过一半；微调时序架构相对静态基线取得显著提升，证明其确实捕获了更多时序信息，而 LEDOV 无法度量这一点。但即便该 SoTA 模型仍留下近一半 SalTempto headroom 未被解释。此外，通过对 SalTempto 的分析，还刻画了模型未能复现的人类注视倾向。

Conclusion: 现有流行视频显著性基准（如 LEDOV）因动态性不足而无法有效度量时序建模能力，导致静态基线看似已足够；SalTempto 通过引入高动态内容弥补了这一缺陷，验证了时序架构的价值，同时揭示视频显著性建模仍有较大提升空间，并提供了可分析人类注视偏好与模型差距的新平台。

Abstract: Video saliency prediction is inherently harder to model than static image saliency due to the additional temporal dimension. Video saliency benchmarks rest on the premise that predicting gaze on video requires utilizing temporal activity distributed across frames. Prior work has challenged this, showing that static baselines recover a significant fraction of the explainable gaze information on LEDOV, a popular video saliency dataset, and that video saliency models fail in the same places as this static baseline. We verify that this diagnosis still stands: under a more capable gold standard than the original analysis, and an updated panel of recent architectures, the strongest temporal architecture in the panel still does not substantially improve over a fine-tuned static baseline. However, it remains unclear whether the marginal gain reflects limitations of current temporal architectures or a lack of temporal patterns in the benchmark itself. We introduce SalTempto, a video saliency benchmark with greater dynamism: 224 clips of highly dynamic content, sourced from the HACS-Segments dataset so that each clip contains an event together with its lead-up and aftermath, with gaze recordings from up to 16 subjects and a training split for adapting pretrained models. On SalTempto, the static baseline recovers only about 13\% of the headroom above the centerbias, against more than half on LEDOV. A fine-tuned temporal architecture shows a substantial gain in performance over the static baseline, indicating that it does capture meaningfully more temporal information, which LEDOV fails to measure. Yet, even this SoTA model still leaves nearly half of SalTempto's headroom unexplained, indicating room for improvement in video saliency modelling. Examination of SalTempto also lets us describe human tendencies that models miss. SalTempto link: this https URL .

</details>


### [66] [T3lescope: Arbitrary-Resolution High-Fidelity Generative Surface Reconstruction from Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03308)
*Atsuhiro Noguchi,Tianhan Xu,Yiming Liang,Yuta Kikuchi,Masahiro Ishiyama,Shintaro Takagi,Hitoshi Murai,Eiichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T3lescope 提出无需逐场景优化的前馈式大尺度三维网格重建框架，用单一固定分辨率的生成器在推理时做由粗到细的级联，实现从单物体到城市级场景的高保真几何重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐场景优化方法在观测稀疏或表面为镜面/透明时缺少学到的三维先验；现有前馈生成方法虽有先验，但受限于固定分辨率与有限空间范围，扩展到大场景需切分重叠局部区域独立处理，难以保持全局几何一致性。作者希望用一套权重统一跨尺度重建，兼顾覆盖与细节。

Method: 提出 T3lescope：在推理阶段进行由粗到细的级联，采用单一固定分辨率的生成器跨场景尺度复用；粗层级确立场景布局，细层级在逐级细化的空间单元内对继承自粗层的几何做扰动与去噪以恢复表面细节。模型在多个尺度的单个单元上训练并跨所有层级共享权重，因此训练时不固定层级结构，层级数、单元尺度与单元位置均在推理时确定。

Result: 在室内、室外及城市级场景上，T3lescope 优于前馈与生成式基线，达到或超过逐场景优化方法，并能恢复精细结构以及镜面与透明表面，展现出对多样场景、视点数量与图像分辨率的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 权重共享的固定分辨率生成器配合推理期由粗到细级联，可在不重新训练、不逐场景优化的情况下统一处理跨尺度大场景重建，兼顾全局一致性与局部细节，是前馈三维重建面向大尺度泛化的一条可行路线。

Abstract: We reconstruct high-fidelity 3D scene meshes from posed multi-view images without per-scene optimization, across scales ranging from single objects to large outdoor scenes. Per-scene optimization methods lack the learned 3D prior needed when observations are sparse or surfaces are glossy or transparent. Existing generative methods leverage such priors to complete geometry in sparsely observed regions, but typically operate at a fixed resolution over a limited spatial extent, trading spatial coverage against detail. Reconstructing a large scene therefore often requires partitioning it into independently processed overlapping local regions, making it difficult to maintain global geometric consistency. To address these issues, we propose T3lescope, which applies a single fixed-resolution generator across scene scales in an inference-time coarse-to-fine cascade. A coarse level establishes the scene layout, and finer levels perturb and denoise geometry inherited from the coarser level within progressively finer spatial cells to recover surface detail. The model is trained on individual cells at multiple scales and shares its weights across all levels, so no hierarchy is fixed during training, and the number of levels, cell scales, and cell locations are determined at inference time. On indoor, outdoor, and city-scale scenes, T3lescope outperforms feed-forward and generative baselines, matches or surpasses per-scene optimization, and recovers fine structures as well as glossy and transparent surfaces. These results show that our method generalizes across diverse scenes, view counts, and image resolutions. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [67] [A Fully Automatic Pipeline for 3D Dendrite Instance Segmentation in SBF-SEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03332)
*Zewen Zhuo,Ilya Belevich,Eija Jokitalo,Alejandra Sierra,Jussi Tohka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出全自动3D树突实例分割流水线，结合YOLOv6引导SAM提示、二维掩码迭代精修、随机森林3D实例连接与nnU-Net实例感知高分辨率精修，在SBF-SEM海马CA1数据上实现高质量树突重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SBF-SEM中单体树突的精确3D重建对量化脑结构可塑性至关重要，但规模化人工标注不可行，需要无需人工提示的全自动分割方案。

Method: 统一流水线：在降采样切片上用YOLOv6引导SAM提示，迭代二维掩码精修，随机森林做3D实例连接，再用nnU-Net在原始分辨率做实例感知高分辨率精修；推理阶段无需人工提示。

Result: 在对照大鼠与匹罗卡品诱导癫痫大鼠的海马CA1 SBF-SEM数据上，重建出连贯且分离良好的树突，语义精度Dice为0.93与0.91；对照组织实例级表现强，癫痫组织的密集区域实例识别仍为主要瓶颈；高分辨率精修可恢复细小树突突起。

Conclusion: 该流水线实现免提示的全自动3D树突实例分割，为下游树突棘级分析提供基础；主要剩余局限在于密集区域的实例识别。代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate three-dimensional (3D) reconstruction of individual dendrites in serial block-face scanning electron microscopy (SBF-SEM) is essential for quantifying structural plasticity in the brain, yet manual annotation at scale is infeasible. We present a fully automatic pipeline for 3D dendrite instance segmentation that unifies YOLOv6-guided Segment Anything Model (SAM) prompting on downsampled slices, iterative two-dimensional mask refinement, random forest 3D instance linking, and instance-aware high-resolution refinement using nnU-Net at native resolution into a single system requiring no manual prompting at inference. Applied to hippocampal CA1 SBF-SEM datasets from a control rat and a pilocarpine- induced epileptic rat, our pipeline reconstructs coherent, well- separated dendrites with high semantic accuracy (Dice 0.93 and 0.91) and strong instance-level performance on control tissue, while analysis of the more challenging epileptic tissue identifies instance recognition in dense regions as the principal remaining limitation. The high-resolution refinement stage recovers thin dendritic protrusions, providing a basis for downstream spine- level analysis. Code is available at this https URL ZE-WEN/dendrite-3d-instance-seg.

</details>


### [68] [LAS-CLIP: A Lightweight Adapter Steering Approach for CLIP's Visual Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03370)
*Anh-Khoa Dinh-Duc,Duc-Tai Dinh,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAS-CLIP：冻结 CLIP 全部参数，用轻量 MaskAdapter 从输入掩码生成逐头逐层注意力偏置注入自注意力，实现区域级视觉引导，同时保留零样本能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 视觉编码器只输出全局表征，难以支持区域级任务；现有适配方案（视觉提示、输入掩码、编码器微调）都会损害预训练表征的完整性。

Method: 提出 LAS-CLIP 轻量适配器引导方案：保持 CLIP 参数完全冻结；紧凑的 MaskAdapter 由输入掩码生成 per-head、per-layer 的注意力偏置，注入冻结的自注意力层，将注意力导向目标区域；无掩码输入时可无损退化为原生 CLIP。

Result: 仅 11.6万–14.5万可训练参数、10万训练样本、2 张 T4 GPU，在 ImageNet-S 零样本分类与 RefCOCO 指代表达理解上达到与 Alpha-CLIP 相当或更优的效果，而后者需在数百万样本上微调整个编码器；定性分析显示在错误掩码下及下游生成任务中表征保真度更强。

Conclusion: 通过外部注意力偏置注入而非改动骨干，可在极低训练成本下为 CLIP 赋予区域级能力，并保持基础零样本性能，为区域级视觉-语言任务提供高效适配范式。

Abstract: CLIP's visual encoder produces only global image representations, limiting its use in region-level tasks. Existing adaptations rely on visual prompting, input masking, or encoder fine-tuning, each compromising pre-trained representations. We propose LAS-CLIP, a Lightweight Adapter Steering approach that keeps every CLIP parameter frozen. A compact MaskAdapter generates per-head, per-layer attention biases from an input mask and injects them into the frozen self-attention layers, steering attention toward the target region. Crucially, because the backbone remains strictly untouched, LAS-CLIP seamlessly reverts to vanilla CLIP when no mask is provided, preserving its foundational zero-shot capabilities. With approximately 116K to 145K trainable parameters and 100K training samples on two T4 GPUs, LAS-CLIP achieves competitive or superior results compared to Alpha-CLIP on ImageNet-S zero-shot classification and RefCOCO referring expression comprehension, despite the latter fine-tuning its entire encoder on millions of samples. Qualitative analysis further confirms stronger representational fidelity under incorrect masks and in downstream generation. Our project page is link to this https URL

</details>


### [69] [EVEWorld: Physical Evolution Supervision for Embodied World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03374)
*Kaiqi Wang,Songxin Zhang,Zejian Xie,Xiao Xiong,Zhuoyang Song,Ziwei Wu,Jun Yu Lu,Yitan Teng,Ziying Song,Jiaxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EVEWorld 框架，通过实例引导修复(IGR)与时间实例对齐(TIA)对具身世界模型施加物理演化监督，并引入 Model Laziness Rate(MLR) 指标，在多个基准上显著降低实例一致性违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身世界模型存在"模型惰性"(Model Laziness)：过度追求视觉保真度而牺牲物理推理能力，且缺乏对操作物体时序动态的过程级监督，导致生成轨迹中目标实例持续出现不一致。

Method: EVEWorld 由两个组件构成：(1) Instance-Guided Restoration (IGR)，通过修复式监督提升实例一致性；(2) Temporal Instance Alignment (TIA)，通过相邻帧间目标实例对齐提升跨帧一致性。同时提出 MLR 指标量化生成轨迹中实例一致性的持续违背程度。

Result: 在 DreamGenBench、EWMBench、PBench 上验证有效性，MLR 相比 GigaWorld-0 降低 87.5%；在 WorldArena 2.0 Track 1 榜单上 JEPA Similarity 排名第 6、总排名第 17。

Conclusion: 过程级物理演化监督（实例修复+时序对齐）可有效缓解具身世界模型的模型惰性，提升物理一致性，是具身世界模型训练的一种可行监督策略。

Abstract: Embodied world models enable scalable simulation of embodied interactions for robot learning. However, existing models are prone to Model Laziness, as they focus on visual fidelity at the expense of physical reasoning and lack process-level supervision over the temporal dynamics of manipulated objects. In this work, we propose EVEWorld, a physical evolution-supervision framework for physically consistent target evolution. EVEWorld consists of two components: Instance-Guided Restoration (IGR) and Temporal Instance Alignment (TIA). First, IGR promotes instance consistency through restoration supervision.
