Second, TIA promotes cross-frame consistency by aligning target instances across adjacent frames. We further introduce the Model Laziness Rate (MLR), a metric that measures persistent violations of instance consistency in generated trajectories. Extensive experiments on DreamGenBench, EWMBench, and PBench demonstrate the effectiveness of EVEWorld, notably achieving an 87.5% reduction in MLR compared with GigaWorld-0. On the WorldArena 2.0 Track 1 leaderboard, our model ranks 6th in JEPA Similarity and 17th overall, which further validates the performance of our evolution supervision strategy.

</details>


### [70] [Interpretable Deepfake Detection in Videos via Explicit Forensic Features and Temporal Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03380)
*Chahira Benhama,Mohand Saïd Allili,Assia Hamadene*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可解释的深度伪造检测框架：将视频转为身份一致的面部轨迹，划分为定长时序窗口，用68个跨光度/纹理/几何/压缩域的描述子表征每帧，再由LSTM建模时序依赖，在四个基准上取得98.0%/91.0%/97.6%/96.2%的F1并具良好跨数据集泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频深度伪造在帧级视觉上可能高度一致，仅在时序上存在细微不一致，端到端深度模型依赖隐式表征、缺乏可解释性且跨数据集泛化不足，因此需要显式编码有物理依据的取证线索。

Method: 构建身份一致的面部轨迹，切分为固定长度时序窗口；每帧用68个结构化描述子表征，覆盖光度、纹理、几何与压缩四类互补域；用LSTM处理该紧凑多域表征以捕获时序依赖与细微异常，整个流程为显式特征驱动的可解释流水线。

Result: 在FaceForensics++、Celeb-DF v2、DFDC精选子集与DeeperForensics上分别取得98.0%、91.0%、97.6%、96.2%的F1，且跨数据集泛化表现良好。

Conclusion: 显式、物理可解释的多域取证描述子配合时序建模，可同时实现高精度与跨数据集鲁棒性，为视频深度伪造检测提供可解释方案。

Abstract: Deepfake detection in videos remains challenging, as manipulated content may appear visually consistent at the frame level while exhibiting subtle temporal inconsistencies. This paper introduces an interpretable deepfake detection framework that models spatially and temporally coherent facial features in video sequences. Unlike end-to-end deep models relying on implicit representations, the proposed approach explicitly encodes physically grounded forensic cues, enabling transparent analysis and improved multi-dataset generalization. The pipeline transforms videos into identity-consistent facial trajectories, segments them into fixed-length temporal windows, and represents each frame using 68 structured descriptors spanning four complementary domains: photometric, textural, geometric, and compression-based features. These descriptors provide a compact multi-domain representation of manipulation artifacts and are processed by a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network to capture temporal dependencies and subtle irregularities. Evaluation on four benchmark datasets, FaceForensics++, Celeb-DF v2, a curated subset of the DeepFake Detection Challenge (DFDC), and DeeperForensics, yields strong and consistent F1-scores of 98.0%, 91.0%, 97.6%, and 96.2%, respectively. The approach also demonstrated a good cross-dataset generalization, providing a robust and interpretable solution for video deepfake detection.

</details>


### [71] [From Patching to Pruning Visual Computation in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03389)
*Rahul Chowdhury,Timothy A Rupprecht,Xuan Shen,Shaoyi Huang,Pu Zhao,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: P2P 是一个免训练推理加速框架，将激活修补（activation patching）从诊断工具转化为计算旁路：通过验证引导的前向/反向逐层扫描，在容差内用固定中性代理激活向量替换若干解码层的视觉 token 投影输出，从而在不删除 token、不改权重的前提下降低 VLM 视觉计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 推理成本高昂：每个视觉 token 都要经过所有解码层的注意力与 MLP 投影，即便许多深度上的 token 级视觉计算并无必要。现有 token 剪枝会删 token、改变序列长度与位置信息，难以保持预训练模型结构与残差通路。作者希望找到一种既能省算力又不破坏原模型语义结构的推理期旁路机制。

Method: 提出 Patch-to-Prune (P2P)，受机制可解释性启发，免训练。用互不重叠的校准/验证/测试划分，执行验证引导的逐层前向与反向扫描，定位那些视觉 token 投影输出可被固定中性代理激活替换的解码区域，满足用户指定的精度容差。与常规剪枝不同，它完整保留序列长度、token 顺序、位置信息、注意力掩码与残差通路，仅旁路计算，不删 token、不改权重。在 Qwen2.5-VL 与 LLaVA 两个系列共 4 个模型、7 个多模态基准上评测。

Result: 在 3% 容差下，P2P 保留约 94% 的稠密精度，同时将 FLOPs 降低 55%。逐层分析显示 VLM 的视觉处理在解码深度上分布不均：早期与晚期层几乎不需要 token 级视觉计算，中间层承担大部分任务相关的视觉整合，后续推理主要依赖已嵌入共享残差与文本表征中的视觉信息。

Conclusion: P2P 既是一个高效推理框架，也是理解 VLM 视觉信息处理的因果透镜，表明视觉计算可被非均匀地稀疏化而不损失结构完整性。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) incur substantial inference cost because every visual token is processed by the attention and MLP projections of every decoder layer, even when token-specific visual computation is unnecessary at many depths. We introduce Patch-to-Prune (P2P), inspired by Mechanistic Interpretability, a training-free framework that converts activation patching from a diagnostic tool into an inference-time computation bypass. P2P performs validation-guided forward and backward layer sweeps to identify decoder regions whose visual-token projection outputs can be replaced by fixed neutral proxy activation vectors within a user-specified accuracy tolerance. Unlike conventional token-pruning methods, P2P preserves the sequence length, token order, positional information, attention mask, and residual pathways, thereby pruning computation without removing tokens or modifying the pretrained model weights. We evaluate P2P on four VLMs from the Qwen2.5-VL and LLaVA families across seven multi-modal benchmarks using mutually disjoint calibration, validation, and test partitions. P2P at a 3% tolerance retains around 94% of dense accuracy while reducing FLOPs by 55%. Beyond these efficiency gains, our layer-wise analysis suggests that visual processing in VLMs is non-uniformly distributed across decoder depth: early and late layers often require little token-specific visual computation, whereas intermediate layers appear to perform most task-relevant visual integration, enabling later reasoning to rely largely on visual information already embedded in shared residual and textual representations. This makes P2P both an efficient inference framework and a causal lens into visual information processing in VLMs.

</details>


### [72] [Native Action-Prior Learning from Videos for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03391)
*Zhaochong An,Fei Zhang,Menglin Jia,Duncan Frost,Zijian Zhou,Yikai Wang,Xudong Wang,Aditya Patel,Belinda Zeng,Tao Xiang,Serge Belongie,Amir Bar,Sen He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NAVA-WAM 提出"原生动作先验学习"，直接用纯观测视频预训练动作策略，避免表征到控制的间接迁移或单独的潜在动作模型；两阶段训练在分布内外均优于先前方法，并展示动作标签效率与真机泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）将未来视觉动态与机器人动作预测结合，但其扩展性受限于需要动作标注的机器人轨迹。纯观测视频蕴含丰富的交互动态证据，但既有做法要么只用其预训练视觉表征、后续再适配控制，要么推断潜在动作再落地到机器人指令，存在间接性与额外建模开销。

Method: 两阶段训练。第一阶段：仅在观测视频上预训练，通过对视觉转移施加未来视频 flow-matching 监督，经"转移结构化联合注意力"反向传播，优化 Action-DiT 以学习动作相关先验。第二阶段：用带动作标注的示范对 Action-DiT 后训练，采用联合视频-动作 flow matching；同时用非对称注意力将视觉分支与迭代式动作去噪解耦，从而实现高效的仅动作推理。

Result: 大量实验表明 NAVA-WAM 在分布内与分布外设置下均持续优于先前方法，并展现出较强的动作标签效率与有效的真机泛化能力。

Conclusion: 原生动作先验学习是一条从纯观测视频直接预训练动作策略的有效路径，为超越动作标注机器人数据提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: World action models integrate future visual dynamics with robot action prediction, but their scalability remains limited by the need for action-annotated robot trajectories. Observation-only videos contain rich evidence about interaction dynamics, but existing approaches typically use them either to pretrain visual representations that must later be adapted for control, or to infer latent actions that are subsequently grounded to robot commands. We present NAVA-WAM, which introduces native action-prior learning by directly pretraining the action policy from observation-only videos, avoiding indirect representation-to-control transfer or a separate latent-action model. Our training consists of two stages. First, we pretrain on observation-only videos, where future-video flow-matching supervision over visual transitions is propagated through transition-structured joint attention to optimize the Action-DiT and learn action-relevant priors. Second, we use action-labeled demonstrations to post-train the Action-DiT for robot control through joint video--action flow matching, while asymmetric attention decouples the visual branch from iterative action denoising and enables efficient action-only inference. Extensive experiments show that NAVA-WAM consistently outperforms prior approaches under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, while demonstrating strong action-label efficiency and effective real-robot generalization. These results establish native action-prior learning as an effective approach to directly pretrain action policies from observation-only videos, providing a scalable path beyond action-labeled robot data.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond Entropy: Self-Diagnostic Multi-Role Token Optimization for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03400)
*Yudong Han,Yong Wang,Zaiquan Yang,Liang Lin,Chongyang Tao,Xiangxiang Chu,Liyuan Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DyCPO共演化框架，通过多角色依赖度量与自适应反事实干预联合优化token级信用分配，提升多模态视频推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推理的可验证奖励强化学习受限于模糊的token级信用分配：高熵token启发式易导致冗长推理，反事实视觉token定位又过度偏重视觉探索、忽视决定性推理线索，且静态反事实策略无法随策略共同演化。

Method: DyCPO框架联合优化可靠token选择与自适应反事实干预：构建多角色依赖度量，在token级对比学习中平衡视觉探索与答案相关性挖掘，抑制纯探索填充token与虚假视觉噪声；反事实信号由模型自身成功/失败采样动态导出，实现自诊断与目标—策略共演化。

Result: 在复杂视频推理与通用视频理解基准上取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: DyCPO可作为多模态强化学习中稳健的token级信用分配范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has substantially advanced multimodal reasoning, yet it remains fundamentally limited by ambiguous token-level credit assignment. While high-entropy token heuristics encourage possibility exploration, naively extending them to video reasoning tends to induce lengthy reasoning, as the model becomes overly reliant on high-entropy visual activations. Alternative approaches that rely on counterfactual-based visual token localization for credit assignment also tend to over-prioritize visual exploration at the expense of decisive reasoning cues for answer derivation, thereby exacerbating the interference from spurious visual nuances. Moreover, these methods employ static counterfactual strategies that fail to co-evolve with the policy during training. In this paper, we introduce DyCPO, a co-evolutionary framework that jointly optimizes reliable token selection and adaptive counterfactual intervention. It constructs a multi-role dependence metric to balance visual exploration and answer-relevance mining in token-wise contrastive learning, while suppressing exploration-only filler tokens and spurious visual noise. Rather than relying on static counterfactual priors, DyCPO dynamically derives counterfactual signals from the model's own successful and failed rollouts, enabling self-diagnostic analysis and co-evolution of the optimization objective with the policy. Extensive experiments on complex video reasoning and general video understanding benchmarks demonstrate consistent performance improvements, establishing DyCPO as a robust token-level credit assignment paradigm for multimodal reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [74] [ForestQuery: Boundary-Aware and Spatially Anchored Query Learning for Unified Forest Point Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03403)
*Zhihao Zhan,Le Tao,Yifei Tian,Xin Liu,Jie Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForestQuery：面向森林点云统一语义/实例分割的边界感知与空间锚定查询学习框架，用边界不确定性建模指导实例查询构建与自适应损失重加权，用可学习3D锚点编码森林垂直分层先验增强语义查询，在多个公开基准与自采真实数据集上取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林点云分割是细粒度3D场景理解的基础，但受树木结构不规则、遮挡严重、密度变化和实例边界模糊等问题困扰。现有基于查询的森林分割方法虽能统一预测语义与实例，却未充分利用森林特有空间结构，也未考虑边界不确定性。

Method: 提出ForestQuery统一分割框架：(1) 显式建模边界不确定性，用于指导可靠的实例查询构建，并通过自适应损失重加权调制查询优化；(2) 空间锚定语义查询增强（SA-SQE），引入可学习3D锚点编码森林垂直分层先验，为语义查询提供显式空间参照。

Result: 在多个公开森林点云基准及一个自采集标注的真实世界数据集上进行评估，实验显示在单木分割与语义分割两类任务上跨多样森林场景均取得一致性提升；代码与数据已公开。

Conclusion: 面向森林点云领域特定任务的查询式分割改进，边界不确定性与垂直分层空间先验是有效设计；属领域专用3D感知工作，未涉及具身导航、物理仿真或机器人操作等核心方向。

Abstract: Forest point cloud segmentation is fundamental for fine-grained 3D forest scene understanding, yet remains challenging due to irregular tree structures, severe occlusions, density variations, and ambiguous instance boundaries. Recent query-based forest segmentation methods have shown promise for unified semantic and instance prediction, but they still insufficiently exploit forest-specific spatial structure and account for boundary uncertainty. In this paper, we propose ForestQuery, a boundary-aware and spatially anchored query learning framework for unified forest point cloud segmentation. ForestQuery enhances instance and semantic query learning through two complementary designs. Specifically, boundary uncertainty is explicitly modeled to guide reliable instance query construction and modulate query optimization through adaptive loss reweighting. Meanwhile, spatially anchored semantic query enhancement (SA-SQE) introduces learnable 3D anchors encoding forest vertical stratification priors to enrich semantic queries with explicit spatial references. We evaluate ForestQuery on multiple public forest point cloud benchmarks and a self-collected annotated real-world dataset. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in both individual-tree segmentation and semantic segmentation across diverse forest scenes. Code and data are publicly available at this https URL

</details>


### [75] [OuroReward: Sequential Reward Scheduling for Reinforcement Learning in Text-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03423)
*Bingyang Cui,Yujie Zhang,Yiling Xu,Yunfeng Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 OuroReward——面向 Text-to-3D 强化学习的"干扰感知"奖励调度策略：先估计各质量维度两两依赖，构造累积干扰最小的循环优化路径并转为一趟序列，从聚合干扰最低维度起步，按当前维度的剩余优化余量自适应切换推进；并引入 AdaSelect 自适应提示筛选提升训练稳定性，在多模型/多 RL 算法上一致改善生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 T23D RL 方法把语义对齐、纹理清晰度等多质量维度的奖励同时聚合优化，未显式建模维度间依赖关系，导致优化不均衡、冲突维度间持续相互干扰，从而难以在多维度上同时达到良好表现。

Method: 1) 估计维度间成对依赖强度，构建使累积干扰最小的循环优化路径，并利用尾—首依赖使环闭合以捕捉全局兼容性；2) 将循环展开为单趟序列，从聚合干扰最低的维度开始优化；3) 自适应预算调度：不固定各维度奖励的优化步数，而依据当前奖励的剩余优化余量决定何时切换下一奖励；4) AdaSelect：依据模型当前能力筛选可靠且信息量高的提示，将策略更新集中于这些提示。

Result: 在不同 T23D 模型与不同 RL 算法上的大量实验表明，该框架在语义对齐、纹理清晰度等多个质量维度上一致提升生成质量，并改善训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 多维度奖励优化应按依赖结构排序并自适应切换，而非等权聚合并行优化；干扰感知的顺序调度与提示筛选可作为 T23D RL 的通用改进模块，但论文未在摘要中给出与三维重建、机器人或具身任务相关的证据。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for Text-to-3D (T23D) generation requires optimization across multiple quality dimensions such as semantic alignment and texture clarity. Existing methods typically optimize these dimensions simultaneously through multiple reward aggregation, without explicitly modeling inter-dimension dependencies. This can cause imbalanced optimization and persistent interference among conflicting dimensions. To address this limitation, we propose OuroReward, an interference-aware sequential reward scheduling strategy for T23D RL. OuroReward first estimates pairwise dependencies among dimensions and constructs a cyclic optimization path that minimizes cumulative interference. By incorporating the tail-to-head dependency, the cycle captures global compatibility across the entire schedule. Then, OuroReward converts the cycle into a one-pass sequence, and starts optimization from the dimension with the lowest aggregate interference. Rather than assigning a fixed optimization budget to each dimension-wise reward, training adaptively determines when to advance to the next reward according to the remaining optimization headroom of the current one. We further introduce AdaSelect, an adaptive prompt selection strategy that identifies reliable and informative prompts aligned with the model's current capability. By focusing policy updates on these prompts, AdaSelect effectively improves training stability. Extensive experiments across different T23D models and RL algorithms demonstrate that our framework consistently improves generation quality across multiple dimensions.

</details>


### [76] [Depth Hypothesis Guided Iterative Refinement for Event-Image Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03439)
*Daikun Liu,Teng Wang,Changyin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HypoDepth，首个事件相机-图像单目深度迭代细化框架，将深度回归转化为基于离散深度假设体（DHV）的约束搜索任务，通过 DHV 特征与上下文特征构建 3D cost volume 并进行多尺度相关性搜索，指导稳定的残差优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机单目深度估计方法主要通过优化上下文特征提升性能，但直接进行全深度回归存在严重的病态性与非线性问题，难以稳定求解。作者希望用显式的离散深度假设与搜索式细化替代直接回归，以提升稳定性与精度。

Method: 核心是 Depth Hypothesis Volume（DHV）：将连续深度离散化为假设空间，把回归问题转成受约束的深度搜索。在 DHV 特征与图像上下文特征之间构建 3D cost volume，并做多尺度相关性搜索以引导残差式迭代细化。该 cost volume 轻量，支持全局到局部的多分辨率高效细化。

Result: 在 DSEC 与 MVSEC 数据集上取得 SOTA，并具备较强零样本泛化能力；tiny 版本在精度与效率间取得良好平衡，可在资源受限设备上实时运行。

Conclusion: 事件相机单目深度估计中，以离散深度假设搜索与轻量 cost volume 迭代细化替代直接回归是有效方向，兼顾精度、泛化与实时性。未来可进一步探索假设空间设计与更多传感器组合。

Abstract: Event cameras hold excellent dynamic properties, showing great potential for monocular depth estimation (MDE). However, existing methods mainly improve performance by optimizing contextual features, but still struggle with the ill-posed and nonlinear nature of direct full-depth regression. In this paper, we propose HypoDepth, the first event-image monocular depth iterative refinement framework. By introducing a discrete Depth Hypothesis Volume (DHV), we transform the depth regression problem into a constrained depth search task. Specifically, we construct a 3D cost volume between the DHV features and contextual features and perform a multi-scale correlation search to guide stable residual optimization. This lightweight cost volume enables efficient global-to-local refinement across multi-resolution. Our method outperforms existing approaches on DSEC and MVSEC with state-of-the-art results and strong zero-shot generalization. Meanwhile, our tiny model achieves an excellent balance between accuracy and efficiency, enabling real-time performance on resource-limited devices.

</details>


### [77] [ChromaGS: Text-Driven Semantic Editing of 4D Gaussian Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03441)
*Antonio Canela,Jordi Sànchez-Riera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChromaGS: 语言引导的可动画3D高斯头像实时颜色编辑，通过语义软分配与颜色分解实现免重训练、确定性的区域级换色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可动画3D高斯头像已能高质量重建与驱动，但外观编辑（尤其颜色）通常需要重训练或依赖生成式编辑，易引入非预期的全局改动，缺乏精确的语义局部控制与实时交互性。

Method: 为每个高斯原语附加学习到的语义区域软分配，并将颜色解耦为区域级基色与高斯级残差；编辑时仅修改区域基色，经软分配传播至相关高斯，残差保留细节。两阶段语言管线将文本指令解析为目标颜色，支持绝对指定与相对调整；编辑在渲染时生效，无需重训练。

Result: 实验显示在多样主体上保持忠实外观，实现确定、精确定位的语义控制与直观交互；相比生成式编辑方法避免非预期修改。

Conclusion: 该工作为可动画高斯头像提供了一种实时、语言驱动、免重训练的颜色编辑方案，以确定性语义控制替代生成式改动的不可控性，适合交互式内容创作与虚拟人编辑。

Abstract: We present ChromaGS, a method for real-time, language-guided color editing of animatable 3D Gaussian head avatars. Given a trained animatable avatar, users can instantly modify the color of semantic regions through natural language, with edits applied at render time and no retraining required. Our key insight is to augment each Gaussian primitive with learned soft assignments to semantic regions and decompose colors into region-level base colors and Gaussian-level residuals. This decomposition enables coherent color transfer: modifying a region's base color propagates naturally through all associated Gaussians while preserving fine appearance details encoded in residuals. A two-stage language pipeline translates text instructions into target colors, supporting both absolute specifications and relative adjustments. Unlike generative editing methods that may introduce unintended modifications, our approach provides deterministic, precisely localized semantic control. Experiments demonstrate faithful appearance preservation and intuitive interaction across diverse subjects. Project page and code are available at: this https URL

</details>


### [78] [Corrupted but Correct: Why Vision-Language Models Lie to Themselves Internally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03445)
*Arun Josephraj Arokiaraj,Zekun Wu,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 上揭示并机制性解释了 VLM 的"训练/推理鸿沟"：对抗扰动可使 teacher-forcing 损失趋零，但自由生成仍输出正确描述；通过 logit lens 将该鸿沟定位到单个自回归步（目标 token 排名恒为 3488/152064），并证明鲁棒性主要由语言解码器的先验决定而非视觉编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 的对抗鲁棒性评估长期依赖 teacher-forcing 损失等代理指标，但作者观察到一种解离现象：使训练损失趋近于零的目标对抗扰动，在自由生成时完全失效，模型仍输出正确描述。若代理指标与真实生成行为脱节，则现有鲁棒性评估与防御的定位可能根本错误。作者希望为此现象给出精确的机制性解释，并回答"鲁棒性究竟属于视觉编码器还是语言解码器"这一关键问题。

Method: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 上，对 200 张 held-out COCO 图像实施受控的两阶段 PGD 攻击（固定目标 caption）。分析分三层：(1) 检验图像级像素统计量与复现的纹理类 attackability 度量对"哪些图像被攻破"的预测力（相关性与 ridge 回归）；(2) 使用 logit lens 逐层追踪目标 token 在词表中的排名，定位鸿沟发生的自回归步；(3) 扫描全部 28 层 LLM decoder，比较易感与抗性图像的表征演化轨迹，并用 merger 隐状态上的线性探针区分两种结果。

Result: (1) 图像级像素统计（含复现的纹理 attackability 度量）对攻破结果几乎无预测力：最佳预测子 r=-0.050（p=0.484），ridge 回归 R²=0.069。(2) 鸿沟精确定位于单个自回归步：在正确首 token 已生成的条件上，目标 token 排名对所有图像、所有条件恒为第 3488 位（词表 152064），方差为零。(3) 视觉编码器对所有图像施加的表示破坏幅度相当，与最终结果无关；语言解码器随后差异化仲裁——对易感图像放大该信号，对抗性图像则主动抑制至低于干净基线（p<0.001，rank-biserial r=0.579）。merger 隐状态线性探针分离两类结果的 AUC=0.858，但作者标注了该估计存在的循环性问题。

Conclusion: 自回归 VLM 的对抗鲁棒性在很大程度上是语言解码器先验的属性，而非视觉编码器的属性。这意味着基于 teacher-forcing 损失的信实性评估可能系统性地误导结论，防御与评估的重点应转向语言解码器端；同时作者对探针结果的循环性保持审慎反思。

Abstract: A targeted adversarial perturbation can drive a vision-language model's (VLM's) teacher-forced training loss for a fixed target caption to near zero, yet the same model, allowed to generate freely, produces the original, correct description with no trace of the target. We call this dissociation the train/inference gap, and give it a precise mechanistic account on Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct using a controlled two-stage PGD attack on 200 held-out COCO images. First, we show that image-level pixel statistics, including a correctly re-implemented, texture-based attackability measure from the CNN robustness literature, have essentially no predictive power over which images are corrupted (best predictor r=-0.050, p=0.484; ridge regression R^2=0.069). Second, using the logit lens, we localise the gap to a single autoregressive step: the rank of the target token, conditioned on the correct first token already being generated, is fixed at exactly 3,488 out of 152,064 vocabulary entries for every image and every condition, with zero variance. Third, tracking target-token rank across all 28 LLM decoder layers reveals that the visual encoder corrupts every image's representation by a comparable margin regardless of eventual outcome, but the language model decoder then differentially arbitrates: amplifying the corrupted signal for susceptible images and actively suppressing it, past its clean-image baseline, for resistant ones (p<0.001, rank-biserial r=0.579). A linear probe on the merger hidden state separates these two outcomes with AUC=0.858, though we flag a circularity concern in this estimate. Together these results argue that adversarial robustness in autoregressive VLMs is substantially a property of the language decoder's prior, not the visual encoder, with direct implications for where faithfulness evaluations and defenses for deployed VLM systems should be targeted.

</details>


### [79] [Preserving Anatomical Continuity: Three-Stage Pipeline for Colon Segmentation in 3D Abdominal CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03467)
*Deshan Kalupahana,Sonit Singh,Praveen Ravindran,Arcot Sowmya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段拓扑保持的结肠CT分割流程：深度学习初分割 + 中心线桥接重连 + 重建细化，改善结构连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习结肠分割因解剖结构复杂常产生不连通的预测结果，破坏拓扑完整性，影响后续临床与科研分析。

Method: 三阶段流程：第一阶段深度学习初分割；第二阶段基于中心线（centreline）桥接，重新连接断开的区域；第三阶段重建细化，改善分割的连续性。

Result: 在TotalSegmentator与RAOS数据集上，使用重叠度、距离与拓扑类指标评估，结构一致性提升，同时保持分割精度。

Conclusion: 该方法增强了拓扑完整性，为临床与科研提供更可靠的结肠分割。

Abstract: Accurate colon segmentation from CT images is essential for colorectal disease analysis, yet deep learning based methods often produce disconnected predictions due to complex anatomy. This study introduces a three-stage, topology-preserving segmentation pipeline to address this issue. The first stage performs initial deep learning-based segmentation, followed by centreline bridging to reconnect disjoint regions and a reconstruction stage to refine continuity. Evaluations on TotalSegmentator and RAOS datasets using overlap, distance and topology-based metrics demonstrate improved structural consistency while maintaining segmentation accuracy. The proposed method enhances topological integrity, enabling more reliable colon segmentation for clinical and research applications.

</details>


### [80] [A Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based Pipeline for End-to-End Procedural Modeling of Field-Grown Maize from Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03468)
*Mozhgan Hadadi,Talukder Z. Jubery,Adarsh Krishnamurthy,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出全自动流水线，用VLM在正交渲染视图中标注玉米叶片中线，结合几何回投影、跨视图一致性合并与可微NURBS拟合，从原始3D点云重建可编辑procedural玉米模型，无需人工调参或物种专用训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 田间作物的可编辑3D模型对高通量表型与in silico育种有价值，但现有方法要么需每株数小时人工调参，要么依赖物种专用标注训练分割模型，难以规模化。

Method: 多模态VLM标注正交渲染视图中的叶片中线；确定性几何算法将标注回投影到点云，通过跨视图共识合并为3D叶片，并在方向加权表面图上将中线生长为完整叶片；测量器官参数生成植株描述符，驱动基于NURBS的procedural模型生成器；最后用可微NURBS拟合将每片叶面精配到扫描点。

Result: 在MaizeField3D的100株基因型多样田间玉米上，整株Chamfer距离中位数5.4 mm，优于基于人工标注的半自动流水线；在不使用参考标签作为输入的前提下，以IoU≥0.5恢复1,023片人工curated参考叶中的1,017片（99.4%）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当后续几何阶段能纠正误差时，VLM标注可作为可用的器官级测量，使可编辑3D植物资产的自动化生成在现代表型实验规模上成为可行。

Abstract: Editable 3D models of field-grown crops support high-throughput phenotyping and in silico breeding trials, but building them from scanned point clouds requires organ-level segmentation and fitting. Procedural generators can turn an organ-level parameter set into an analysis-suitable 3D model, but obtaining that set requires hours of manual tuning per plant or segmentation models trained on species-specific labels. We present an automated pipeline that reconstructs procedural maize models from raw 3D point clouds without manual tuning or species-specific training data. A multimodal vision-language model (VLM) annotates leaf midlines in rendered orthographic views. Deterministic geometric algorithms back-project the annotations onto the point cloud, merge them into 3D leaves by cross-view consensus, and grow the midlines to full blades on an orientation-weighted surface graph. Measured organ parameters populate a plant descriptor for a Non-Uniform Rational B-Spline (NURBS)-based procedural model generator. Each leaf surface is then refined against its scan points by differentiable NURBS fitting. The pipeline reached a median whole-plant Chamfer distance of 5.4 mm on 100 genotypically diverse field-grown maize plants from the MaizeField3D dataset. The reconstructions were closer to the scans than those of an earlier semi-automated pipeline based on manual annotations. The pipeline recovered 1,017 of 1,023 (99.4%) curated reference leaves at an intersection-over-union of at least 0.5 without using those labels as input. These results show that VLM annotations become usable organ-level measurements when downstream geometric stages can correct them. This makes automated generation of editable 3D plant assets feasible at the scale of modern phenotyping experiments.

</details>


### [81] [UniDynamics: Event-RGB Fusion for Unified Future 4D Dynamic Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03473)
*Daikun Liu,Xin Zhan,Teng Wang,Xiaoping Wang,Changyin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniDynamics 提出基于扩散的单次事件-RGB 对驱动的 4D 动态场景生成框架，通过事件潜变量增强与感知动力学空间显式建模未来运动场，在 VKitti2 与 DSEC 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 4D 动态场景生成方法依赖长历史序列或控制先验，单帧 RGB 外推缺乏可靠运动先验；事件相机提供高时间分辨率运动信息，可弥补该缺口。

Method: 以单次 event-RGB 对为输入，无需长历史与控制先验。设计 Event Latent Enhancement (ELE) 模块，对齐并增强事件潜变量为可注入扩散的条件特征，提供初始运动先验与纹理/结构线索；引入嵌入多尺度 U-Net 的 Perceptual Dynamics Space (PDS)，解耦并自适应交互深度与光流，同时持续向外观特征反馈几何-运动约束。

Result: 在 VKitti2 与 DSEC 数据集上达到 state-of-the-art，生成高质量、时间一致、4D 一致的未来预测，并在高速运动模糊等挑战场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 事件流作为运动先验可有效支撑单 RGB 条件下的 4D 动态场景生成，多模态几何-运动约束建模提升了物理合理性与时空一致性。

Abstract: We propose UniDynamics, a diffusion-based framework for future 4D dynamic scenes (RGB, depth, and optical flow) generation from a single event-RGB pair, without requiring long histories or control priors as in existing methods, while explicitly modeling future motion fields. The core idea is to leverage event streams to offer an alternative motion prior for single-RGB extrapolation, and to enforce geometric and motion constraints throughout generation via multimodal modeling. Specifically, we design an Event Latent Enhancement (ELE) module to align and enhance event latents into diffusion-injectable conditioning features, providing robust initial motion priors and reliable texture/structure cues. We further introduce a Perceptual Dynamics Space (PDS) embedded in the multi-scale U-Net, which decouples and adaptively interacts depth and flow while continuously feeding back constraints to appearance features, improving geometric-motion consistency for physically plausible and spatiotemporally coherent prediction. Experiments on VKitti2 and DSEC demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, producing high-quality, temporally coherent, and 4D-consistent future predictions, especially under challenging high-speed motion blur.

</details>


### [82] [Fed-ADApt: Federated Anytime Depth Adaptation for Resource-Aware Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03474)
*Abhijeet Parida,Zhifan Jiang,Pooneh Roshanitabrizi,Austin Tapp,Maria J. Ledesma-Carbayo,Syed Muhammad Anwar,Ziyue Xu,Marius George Linguraru,Holger R. Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Fed-ADApt，一种深度自适应联邦学习框架，用多深度监督与分层深度聚合让不同算力的机构协同训练 UNet 分割模型，并支持部署时动态深度选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦医学图像分割假设各机构算力同质，低资源站点难以参与；同时训练与推理的算力约束未被联合考虑。

Method: 基于 UNet 的深度自适应联邦框架：多深度（multi-depth）监督 + 分层深度聚合（hierarchical depth-wise aggregation），各站点按本地算力预算训练不同深度的子网络，聚合为支持动态深度选择的全局模型。

Result: 在 2D 视网膜眼底视盘分割与 3D 脑肿瘤分割上验证：联邦协作显著提升域偏移下的鲁棒性；3D 上匹配全资源 FedAvg，2D 平均 Dice 仅降 4.7%；推理成本 3D 降 19.5%、2D 降 34.5%，最受限站点训练成本降低 98%。

Conclusion: 联合考虑训练与推理算力预算，使低资源机构可参与联邦并保持有竞争力的全局性能，为异构临床与边缘环境下的联邦医学图像分割提供实用的公平方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative training of medical image segmentation models without sharing raw patient data, yet existing approaches assume a homogeneous compute budget across institutions, limiting participation of low-resource sites. We propose Fed-ADApt, a depth-adaptive federated framework for UNet-based segmentation that jointly addresses low-compute training and inference. Fed-ADApt integrates multi-depth supervision with hierarchical depth-wise aggregation, allowing each site to train according to its local compute budget while contributing to a global model that supports dynamic depth selection at deployment. We evaluated Fed-ADApt on multi-site 2D retinal fundus disc segmentation and 3D brain tumor segmentation. Across both tasks, federated collaboration substantially improves robustness under domain shift. Fed-ADApt matched the full-resource FedAvg performance in 3D and achieved competitive 2D performance with a 4.7% average Dice reduction, while reducing average inference cost by 19.5% in 3D and 34.5% in 2D and substantially reducing training cost by 98% at the most constrained sites. Importantly, Fed-ADApt enables low-resource institutions that cannot train full-capacity models to participate in federations while maintaining competitive global performance under a favorable accuracy to efficiency trade-off. By considering training and inference compute budgets, Fed-ADApt provides a practical and equitable solution for federated medical image segmentation across heterogeneous clinical and edge-enabled imaging environments.

</details>


### [83] [Weave Forcing: Compositional Memory Routing for Interactive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03510)
*Ziyi Wang,Junchi Yao,Heqian Qiu,Wenbo Shi,Chengjiu Wang,Jinyang He,Binkai Hong,Hongliang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 Weave Forcing，通过 LLM 语义槽路由、掩码记忆编织与覆盖自适应 RoPE，实现交互式长视频生成中的组合式记忆复用，提升跨镜头主体与背景一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成虽提升了长时程时序一致性，但交互式叙事需要新镜头组合不同历史镜头的角色与背景。整体提示检索忽略各组件差异化的参考需求，而直接拼接全部历史记忆会引入无关视觉内容。

Method: 1) LLM 语义槽路由：将用户提示分解为角色与背景描述，并为每个组件显式选择合适的历史参考；2) 掩码记忆编织：以语义槽为条件的对比注意力图构造精细语义掩码，选择性暴露压缩历史 KV 记忆中的相关 token；3) 覆盖自适应 RoPE：按无/部分/全覆盖参考调整时间偏移与记忆保留，缓解不完整参考靠近当前生成时的视觉伪影。

Result: 大量实验表明 Weave Forcing 提升了跨镜头主体与背景一致性，同时保持有竞争力的视觉质量与文本对齐。

Conclusion: 通过将历史记忆按语义组件解耦并按覆盖度自适应注入，可在免训练条件下有效支持交互式长视频生成中的组合式记忆复用。

Abstract: Recent advances in autoregressive video generation have improved temporal consistency over extended durations, yet interactive storytelling requires more than continuous scene extension: a new shot may combine characters and backgrounds from different historical shots. Whole prompt retrieval can overlook the distinct reference needs of individual components, while directly combining all historical memories may introduce unrelated visual content. To address these problems, we present Weave Forcing, a training-free framework for compositional memory reuse in interactive long video generation. First, we use an LLM for semantic slot routing to decompose user prompts into character and background descriptions and explicitly select suitable historical references for each component. To isolate the required content, masked memory weaving uses contrasting attention maps conditioned on semantic slots to construct refined semantic masks, selectively exposing relevant tokens from compressed historical KV memories to guide the generation of the current shot. We further introduce coverage adaptive RoPE to adjust temporal offsets and memory retention according to no, partial, or full reference coverage, addressing visual artifacts observed when incomplete historical references are positioned close to the current generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Weave Forcing improves cross-shot subject and background consistency while maintaining competitive visual quality and text alignment.

</details>


### [84] [ProgressNet: Sketching and Prompting with a Frozen Text-to-Image Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03512)
*Arkaprabha Basu,Chaitat Utintu,Yi-Zhe Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 ProgressNet，让冻结的文生图模型以约1秒/轮的交互速度跟随人类逐步作画（增删笔画、修改提示词），通过三种推理期机制复用模型内部记忆、层间外观传递与未完成草图信任信号，在三个草图域保持保真度与渐进一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成器通常接收已完成草图并一次性出图，每次编辑都重新生成；而少数跨轮保持状态的模型由文本驱动、无法接收笔画且速度太慢，无法支持人类逐步绘制的交互过程。作者希望在不重新训练的前提下，让冻结的文生图模型支持增量、可撤销、可改提示的持续绘制会话。

Method: ProgressNet 是免训练框架，复用冻结文生图模型已有的三方面能力：上一轮的记忆通路、可携带外观而不冻结结构的层，以及对未完成草图信任程度的内部信号。对应设计三种推理期机制——Previous-Concept Memory（前轮概念记忆）、Layer-Selective K/V Injection（层选择性 K/V 注入）、Banded Adaptive Control（分带自适应控制）。笔画增加/擦除与提示修改均转化为推理期条件更新，无需新参数。

Result: 在 FS-COCO 上，随草图完成度从 10% 提升到 100%，FLUX+ControlNet 基线的 FID 翻倍，而 ProgressNet 几乎不变；在三个草图域上保持较强保真度与渐进一致性；用户研究中相对五个竞争方法更受偏好，尤其在擦除操作上优势最明显。

Conclusion: 结果表明，冻结文生图模型内部已具备渐进式生成所需的条件信息，通过纯推理期机制即可实现约 1 秒/轮的可交互逐步绘制，且在草图越完整、基线退化越严重的场景下优势越显著，为免训练交互式图像生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Humans draw progressively: a few strokes, a look at the result, a stroke erased, a prompt revised. Image generators do not work this way. They typically take a finished sketch and produce the image in a single pass, so every edit starts the picture again, and the models that do keep state across turns are driven by text, cannot take a stroke, and are too slow to draw with. We present ProgressNet, a training-free framework that lets a frozen text-to-image model follow a drawing session as it unfolds: strokes are added and erased, the prompt is revised, and the image keeps up at about a second per turn. It needs no new parameters because the frozen model already has what a progressive generator needs, a pathway through which the previous turn can be remembered, layers that can carry appearance forward without freezing structure, and an internal signal of how far to trust an unfinished sketch; three inference-time mechanisms (Previous-Concept Memory, Layer-Selective K/V Injection and Banded Adaptive Control) use each in turn. As a sketch fills in, every existing method degrades, the FID of the FLUX+ControlNet baseline doubling between 10% and 100% completion on FS-COCO, while ProgressNet's barely moves; it maintains strong fidelity and progressive coherence across three sketch domains and is preferred by users over five competitors, most widely on erasure.

</details>


### [85] [Feedforward Novel View Synthesis for Heterogeneous Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03522)
*Meng Wei,Cheng Zhang,Boying Li,Yihang Chen,Jianmin Zheng,Hamid Rezatofighi,Jianfei Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究跨异构中心相机（透视、鱼眼、全景）的前馈新视角合成，指出tokenization带来的"token聚合投影相关像素射线束"歧义，提出结合token中心相对相机位置编码（CPE）、局部raymap与投影感知2D RoPE，将多样相机统一为共享射线空间的校准采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈NVS方法普遍假设context与target视角共享固定相机族（同构相机假设），但实际多传感器系统中透视、鱼眼、全景相机常共存，且目标投影可能在训练中未见。作者识别出关键歧义：一个视觉token聚合的是投影相关的像素射线束，而现有相机编码仅暴露绝对射线或token中心关系，无法表达patch内射线分布。

Method: 三项组件：(1) token中心相对Camera Positional Encodings（CPE）表达token间相对几何；(2) 提出local raymaps——token级表示，显式描述每个token所概括的patch内射线分布；(3) 投影感知2D RoPE，用射线诱导的角度坐标替代原始图像网格坐标，使相对位置推理在不同相机投影下对齐。整体框架将异构相机视为共享射线空间的校准采样而非独立视觉域。

Result: 在ScanNet++异构相机系统上，混合相机评估下优于相机条件基线；并展示了对全景视角的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模token级射线分布与投影对齐的相对位置编码，可有效解除同构相机假设，实现跨异构中心相机的前馈新视角合成与零样本全景泛化。

Abstract: Feed-forward novel view synthesis has recently shown promising results from sparse posed images, but most existing methods assume that context and target views share a fixed camera family. This homogeneous-camera assumption breaks in practical multi-sensor systems, where perspective, fisheye, and panoramic cameras may coexist and where the target projection may be unseen during training. We study feed-forward NVS across heterogeneous central cameras and identify a key ambiguity introduced by tokenization: a visual token aggregates a projection-dependent bundle of pixel rays, while existing camera encodings mainly expose absolute rays or token-center relations. To address this, we combine token-center relative Camera Positional Encodings and proposed local raymaps, a token-level representation that explicitly describes the intra-patch ray distribution summarized by each token. We further propose projection-aware 2D RoPE, which replaces raw image-grid coordinates with ray-induced angular coordinates so that relative positional reasoning is aligned across camera projections. Together, these components treat diverse cameras as calibrated samplings of a shared ray space rather than separate visual domains. On ScanNet++ with heterogeneous-camera system, our method improves over camera-conditioned baselines under mixed-camera evaluation and demonstrates zero-shot generalization to panoramic views.

</details>


### [86] [DuoMatching: Joint-Marginal Distribution Matching for Few-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03543)
*Jiahao Zhan,Yan Wang,Yongrui Ma,Qunliang Xing,Ruchang Yao,Runtao Liu,Shijie Zhao,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DuoMatching分布匹配框架，在联合分布匹配基础上增加边缘（帧级）匹配目标，并设计LatentBridge与Latent Variation Sampling解决视频学生模型与图像教师模型的隐空间不匹配问题，用于流式视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成借助DMD匹配视频帧联合分布，虽能缓解自回归漂移，但在视觉质量与语义对齐上仍有不足，需引入图像生成器的帧级先验进行补充监督。

Method: 统一联合-边缘分布匹配框架：在联合匹配目标上叠加帧级边缘匹配目标，从图像生成器迁移视觉与语义先验；LatentBridge对齐视频学生与图像教师的隐表示差异；Latent Variation Sampling将帧级监督分散到不同时间段以减少冗余。

Result: 实验显示视觉质量、构图与语义对齐提升，同时较好保持运动动态；人类评估对全部基线偏好在80%以上。

Conclusion: 联合+边缘的分布匹配可有效融合图像教师先验，是提升流式视频生成质量的可扩展方向。

Abstract: Streaming video generation has benefited from distribution matching distillation (DMD), which matches the joint distribution of video frames to a video teacher's approximation of the real video distribution. Although this joint matching mitigates drift during autoregressive rollouts, limitations remain in visual quality and semantic alignment. To address these limitations, we propose DuoMatching, a distribution matching framework that approximates the real video distribution through a unified joint-marginal formulation. On top of existing joint matching formulations, the additional marginal matching objective provides dedicated frame-level supervision from an image generator, transferring complementary visual and semantic priors from it. To apply this frame-level supervision in video generation, we introduce LatentBridge to resolve the latent representation mismatch between the video student and the image teacher. Latent Variation Sampling further distributes such frame-level supervision across distinct temporal segments, reducing redundancy. Experiments demonstrate that DuoMatching improves visual quality, composition, and semantic alignment while largely preserving motion dynamics. Human evaluations show overall preference rates above 80% against all evaluated baselines. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [87] [Rethinking What to Cache in Few-Step Diffusion Transformers: Solver-Aware Target Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03577)
*Shuo Yang,Lihao Fang,Yi Zhang,Haixiang Wang,Xincheng Ye,Shufan Chen,Jipeng Guo,Youqing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoTarget：为蒸馏后的 Diffusion Transformer 采样自动选择缓存张量（cache target），在给定模型、求解器与复用调度下先用少量无缓存运行测量各候选张量复用的误差，选取误差最低者，从而减少 DiT 前向次数与缓存存储，同时保持接近无缓存的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT 生成需多次昂贵前向，加速手段主要有步数蒸馏（减少采样步数）与缓存（跨步复用张量）。多数缓存方法预先固定复用哪个张量；而蒸馏后相邻采样步间隔变大，跨大间隔复用误差更大，因此"缓存什么"变得尤为关键，需要按模型/求解器/复用调度自适应决策。

Method: AutoTarget 使用一小组无缓存复用的运行来定量测量复用每个候选张量所导致的误差，然后选择误差最低的候选张量。同时分析单步复用误差如何传播到最终样本；针对 Euler 采样，识别出能产生相同轨迹的缓存目标，并说明为何"存储的求解器更新量"不一定具备该性质。

Result: 在蒸馏后的图像与视频 DiT 上，最佳缓存目标随模型、图像分辨率和求解器而变；AutoTarget 在降低 DiT 评估次数与缓存存储的同时，生成质量与对应无缓存运行接近。在 PixArt-LCM 与 FLUX.1-schnell 测试设置上，其校准排序与留出缓存的运行排序一致，并提供 GitHub 核心实现便于复现。

Conclusion: 缓存目标的选取应由模型、求解器、复用调度和数据共同决定，而非预先固定；基于误差测量的轻量校准即可选出最优目标，是与步蒸馏正交的实用 DiT 采样加速组件。其价值主要在生成效率与缓存设计层面，而非具身/机器人或物理仿真方向。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) can generate high-quality images and videos, but generating each sample requires multiple costly DiT forward passes. Two common ways to accelerate DiT sampling are step distillation, which reduces the number of sampling steps, and caching, which skips some DiT evaluations by reusing a tensor computed at an earlier step. Most caching methods decide in advance which tensor to reuse. After distillation, adjacent sampling steps are farther apart. Reusing a tensor across this larger gap introduces more error, so choosing what to cache becomes especially important. We therefore introduce AutoTarget, a method that chooses the cached tensor for a given model, solver, and reuse schedule. AutoTarget uses a small set of runs without cache reuse to measure the error caused by reusing each candidate tensor, then selects the candidate with the lowest error. We also analyze how an error at one reuse step affects the final sample. For Euler sampling, we identify cache targets that produce the same trajectory and show why a stored solver update may not. Experiments on distilled image and video DiTs show that the best cache target changes with the model, image resolution, and solver. AutoTarget reduces DiT evaluations and retained cache storage. Generation quality remains close to the corresponding uncached run. On the tested PixArt-LCM and FLUX.1-schnell settings, its calibration ranking matches the ranking from held-out cached runs. To help others reproduce the method, we provide its core implementation on GitHub at this https URL .

</details>


### [88] [ManifoldSplat: Language-Guided Semantic Shape Editing of 3D Gaussian Head Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03599)
*Antonio Canela,Jordi Sànchez-Riera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ManifoldSplat 是首个端到端、语言引导的可动画 3D 高斯化身语义形状编辑框架，在结构化 FLAME 流形内进行编辑以严格保持身份与动画一致性，约 90 秒完成重建与编辑，渲染约 800 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动 3D 头部化身编辑难以提供细粒度局部控制，存在特征纠缠、几何一致性差的问题；通过自然语言修改几何往往依赖逐提示的慢速优化，或牺牲身份与绑定（rigging）质量。

Method: 以单目视频重建的可动画 3D Gaussian Splatting 化身为对象，编辑在结构化 FLAME 流形中执行而非直接优化非结构化高斯点云；提出 DeltaRegion——按区域解耦的条件变分自编码器（CVAE）前馈式输出形状增量，并配合精修阶段恢复视角一致细节。

Result: 在消费级 GPU 上约 90 秒完成化身重建与编辑，渲染约 800 FPS；大量评测表明在局部提示对齐、几何连贯性与身份保持方面达到新的 state-of-the-art。

Conclusion: 将语义形状编辑约束在参数化流形（FLAME）内并结合解耦的区域级 CVAE，可同时实现前馈式语言编辑、身份/动画保持与高保真实时渲染，为可动画头部化身提供端到端编辑方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D head avatars have reached near-photorealistic quality. While recent methods enable text-driven manipulation, they struggle to provide fine-grained localized control, often entangling features or lacking geometric consistency. Modifying geometry through natural language currently requires slow per-prompt optimization or compromises identity and rigging. We present ManifoldSplat, the first end-toend framework for language-guided semantic shape editing of animatable 3D Gaussian Splatting avatars reconstructed from monocular videos. By performing edits within the structured FLAME manifold rather than directly optimizing an unstructured Gaussian cloud, we strictly preserve identity and animation. We introduce DeltaRegion, a per-region disentangled Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE) delivering feedforward shape deltas, alongside a refining stage to recover view-consistent details. ManifoldSplat reconstructs and edits an avatar in ~90 seconds on a consumer GPU, rendering at ~800 FPS. Extensive evaluations demonstrate our approach sets a new state-of-the-art in localized prompt alignment, geometric coherence, and identity preservation. Project page and code: this https URL

</details>


### [89] [DEPICT: Scoring Text-to-Image Alignment by Answer Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03617)
*Vasco Ramos,Sandra Godinho Silva,Joao Magalhaes,Ricardo Rei,Pedro Henrique Martins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DEPICT：一种免训练的图文对齐度量，用"图像答案与纯文本答案的期望一致性"替代固定参考答案，并按文本决定性加权，再与整体（holistic）分数融合，在5个基准、11个骨干上超越所有免训练指标，并在3个相关性基准中的2个超过微调评估器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图文对齐是caption评测、幻觉检测、数据筛选与T2I基准测试的核心问题。随T2I模型能力提升，评测需发现缺失物体、属性错位、计数错误、忽略否定等问题。现有方案存在三类缺陷：微调评估器被绑定在单一骨干与训练分布上；整体式指标丢失细粒度信息；分解式指标依赖"固定YES"假设，当假设不成立时会误罚忠实图像（如否定句）。

Method: 提出DEPICT，一种免训练指标。核心是"一致性规则"：不再用固定参考答案，而是让图像侧答案与仅文本侧答案求期望一致性，并以caption对该问题的决定性程度对问题加权；同时为弥补分解造成的上下文丢失，将一致性分数与一个整体式分数融合。

Result: 一致性规则使否定句准确率从19%提升至88%；在5个基准、来自三个模型家族共11个骨干上评测，超越所有免训练指标，并在3个人工相关性基准中的2个上超过微调评估器。

Conclusion: 免训练、跨骨干的评估器可同时兼顾细粒度与整体判断，通过期望一致性替代固定参考答案显著改善否定等难题，为T2I/图文对齐评测提供了可推广的替代方案。

Abstract: Image-text alignment is a core problem in computer vision with applications in caption evaluation, hallucination detection, data curation, and the benchmarking of text-to-image (T2I) generators. As T2I models improve, benchmarking has become demanding, requiring metrics capable of finding a series of issues like missing objects, swapped attributes, miscounts, and ignored negations. Recent work addresses this by fine-tuning evaluators on preference data or by prompting a vision-language model, either holistically with the caption or with decomposed verification questions. However, existing approaches fall short: fine-tuned metrics remain bound to one backbone and training distribution; holistic metrics miss fine-grained details; and decomposed metrics rely on a fixed-YES assumption that penalizes faithful images whenever that assumption fails. In contrast, we propose DEPICT, a training-free metric that replaces fixed reference answers with expected agreement between image-based and caption-only answers, weighting questions by how decisively the caption determines them. By replacing fixed references, our agreement rule increases negation accuracy from 19% to 88%. To recover the context lost during decomposition, DEPICT merges this agreement score with a holistic score. We evaluate DEPICT on five benchmarks and eleven backbones from three model families and find that it surpasses all training-free metrics and exceeds fine-tuned evaluators on two out of three human-correlation benchmarks.

</details>


### [90] [World Embedding Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03632)
*Yiqi Liu,Ruifeng Yuan,Yang Wang,Long Li,Fengyu Cai,Hou Pong Chan,Jialin Yu,Hao Zhang,Chenghua Lin,Chenghao Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出World Embedding Benchmark（8,000个受控仿真案例、80个族、覆盖流体/固体/动力学/光学电磁），用检索、物理属性回归、视频-描述配对分类三项任务区分"跨模态物理对齐"与"定量物理信息可恢复性"；发现对齐训练提升检索/分类但损害回归，且检索增强可提升生成视频的物理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型与视频生成越来越强调物理保真度，但视频表征究竟如何编码物理信息仍不清楚；且现有评估往往把"跨模态对齐"与"定量物理信息保留"混为一谈，缺少可诊断二者的受控基准。

Method: 构建World Embedding Benchmark：80个物理族、8,000个受控仿真案例，每个案例含渲染视频与仿真导出的物理标注。设计三类任务——文本-视频检索、物理属性回归、多选视频-描述配对分类。评测预训练全模态嵌入模型，并用轻量探针从冻结视频嵌入中恢复物理量；再做物理专用视频-文本对的持续对比训练；最后用嵌入检索参考视频做RAG，接入MiniMax-H3生成。

Result: 预训练全模态嵌入模型检索弱、族内配对分类接近随机；轻量探针可从冻结视频嵌入中恢复有用物理信息。物理专用对比训练提升检索与配对分类，但使物理属性回归性能下降，体现出"对齐—定量可恢复性"的权衡。检索增强的参考视频提升生成视频的物理保真度，且检索模型越强增益越大（MiniMax-H3实验）。

Conclusion: 物理对齐与属性可恢复性必须联合评估，二者不可互推；物理表征对改进视频生成的物理保真度具有实用价值，检索增强是一条有效路径。

Abstract: Physical fidelity has received increasing attention in world models and video generation, yet how video representations encode physical information remains less understood. We introduce the World Embedding Benchmark, comprising 8,000 controlled simulation cases from 80 families spanning fluid mechanics, solid mechanics, dynamics, and optics & electromagnetism. Each case pairs a rendered video with simulation-derived physical annotations, supporting three complementary tasks: text-video retrieval, physical-property regression, and multiple-choice video-description pair classification. We use these tasks to distinguish cross-modal physical alignment from the recoverability of quantitative physical information. Evaluated pre-trained omnimodal embedding models show weak retrieval and near-chance within-family pair classification, while lightweight probes recover useful physical information from frozen video embeddings. Continual contrastive training with physics-specific video-text pairs improves retrieval and pair classification but degrades physical-property regression, revealing a trade-off between alignment and quantitative information recoverability. Finally, we use the embeddings to retrieve reference videos for retrieval-augmented generation with MiniMax-H3. Retrieved references improve the physical fidelity of generated videos, with stronger retrieval models yielding larger gains in our experiments. Together, these findings highlight the need to evaluate physical alignment and property recoverability jointly, and demonstrate the utility of physical representations for improving video generation.

</details>


### [91] [LoGo: Local-Global Rewards for Consistent Long-Horizon Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03636)
*Ziqi Ma,Shreya Sharma,Mohamed El Banani,Katja Schwarz,Chongjie Ye,Chao-Yuan Wu,Li Fei-Fei,Ben Mildenhall,Georgia Gkioxari,Justin Johnson,Gowthami Somepalli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoGo 提出对相机控制视频生成模型进行后训练时混合全局与局部空间奖励，通过细粒度信用分配提升长时程相机运动下的 3D 一致性，并发布 TrajectoryBench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机控制的视频模型正朝长生成时程与复杂相机控制发展，但其关键失效模式是 3D 不一致：相机移动时物体失去恒常性、场景结构漂移。现有后训练方法对整个生成序列只给一个标量奖励，难以在长时程上定位并纠正局部不一致。

Method: LoGo 在相机控制视频模型的后训练中混合两类奖励：全局奖励维持相机跟随与视频质量，局部（空间定位）奖励提供细粒度信用分配。并在三个基础模型上验证，同时提出面向长时程复杂相机控制生成的 TrajectoryBench 基准，配合 DL3DV 进行评估。

Result: 在三个基础模型上，LoGo 在 DL3DV 与 TrajectoryBench 上均取得明显优势，有效减少局部物体漂移、伪影与全局场景变化，同时保持相机跟随与视频质量。

Conclusion: 细粒度信用分配是长时程相机控制视频模型后训练的关键；混合全局—局部奖励是改善 3D 一致性的有效方案，并为长时程复杂相机控制生成提供了新基准。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video models are rapidly advancing toward long generation horizons and complex camera control. A key failure mode is 3D inconsistency: as the camera moves, objects lose permanence and scene structures shift. Existing post-training techniques, which assign a single scalar reward to the entire generation, are poorly suited to correcting these inconsistencies over long horizons. We introduce LoGo, which blends global and spatially localized rewards for camera-controlled video models. The local reward provides fine-grained credit assignment, which substantially improves 3D consistency, while the global reward preserves camera following and video quality. Across three base models, LoGo shows a clear advantage on DL3DV and TrajectoryBench, a new benchmark for long-horizon, complex-camera-control generation that current evaluations lack. LoGo effectively reduces local object shifts, artifacts, and global scene changes, illustrating the importance of credit assignment in post-training video models. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [92] [On-Board Anomaly Detection for Efficient Marine Environmental Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03649)
*Thomas Goudemant,Clotilde Szywala,Benjamin Francesconi,Michelle Aubrun,Yves Bobichon,Marjorie Bellizzi,Adrien Girard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向多/高光谱地球观测卫星的在轨海洋事件检测流水线：自监督编码器压缩图像至低维潜空间，机器学习异常检测模型识别海面异常，并与Isolation Forest、One-Class SVM、LOF对比，已集成于ESA PhiSat-2与IMAGIN-e任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋生态系统受溢油、藻华、泥沙洪等威胁，需早期探测；星上算力与下行带宽有限，需要轻量、资源高效的星载检测与数据缩减方案。

Method: 自监督神经网络编码器将多/高光谱卫星图像压缩为低维潜空间以支持星上高效处理；在此基础上用机器学习异常检测模型识别偏离正常海面模式的环境异常；与Isolation Forest、One-Class SVM、LOF等传统算法对比；流水线针对从嵌入CPU到AI加速器的星载平台进行资源优化，并优先传输关键信息。

Result: 未在摘要中给出定量结果；以与ESA PhiSat-2、Microsoft/Thales Alenia Space IMAGIN-e等多个任务的集成演示作为验证，强调及时告警与数据缩减能力。

Conclusion: 该轻量流水线可提升海洋环境监测的时效性与星地通信带宽利用效率，具备多任务星载部署可行性。

Abstract: Marine ecosystems are impacted by various threats such as oil spills, algal blooms, and sediment floods, which disrupt habitats, wildlife, and human activities. Advances in satellite imagery and Artificial Intelligence (AI) have enhanced our capabilities for early detection and mitigation of such hazards. In this paper, we propose a marine event detection pipeline for Earth observation satellites equipped with multi- or hyperspectral sensors. Our approach includes a self-supervised neural network encoder that compresses satellite images into a reduced latent space, enabling efficient onboard processing. A machine learning anomaly detection model identifies deviations from normal sea patterns to detect environmental anomalies. We compare its performance against traditional algorithms such as Isolation Forest, One-Class Support Vector Machine and Local Outlier Factors. Our lightweight, resource-efficient pipeline is optimized for deployment on satellites with limited computational resources, ranging from embedded CPUs to AI hardware accelerators. By prioritizing the transmission of critical information, our solution enhances system responsiveness and optimizes satellite communication bandwidth. Demonstrated through current integration across multiple missions, including European Space Agency's (ESA) Phisat-2 mission and Microsoft/Thales Alenia Space IMAGIN-e mission, our pipeline aims to improve marine environmental monitoring by providing timely alerts and efficient data reduction.

</details>


### [93] [ProAR: Learning Prospective Reasoning with Autoregressive Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03664)
*Linghui Shen,Tinghui Zhu,Sheng Zhang,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProAR 将自回归视频生成改造为目标导向的推理过程，通过目标帧预测的非对称注意力掩码与未来表征自对齐两种机制，为生成提供显式稀疏目标监督与隐式稠密逐步引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频模型擅长因果生成，但"预测下一块"的范式使其短视、被动，缺乏长程目标约束；而在以推理为导向的生成任务中，通过合法的中间状态达成目标结果比局部视觉合理性更重要。

Method: ProAR 引入两个组件：(1) 在自回归循环中通过非对称注意力掩码集成目标帧预测，使预测的目标帧引导中间状态生成而不被其干扰，实现长程结果锚定；(2) 提出未来表征自对齐，借助 AR 训练中的 teacher-forcing 单次前向提取干净的未来表征，用轻量的、仅训练时启用的预测器将当前隐状态与其对齐，从而引导短程转移。

Result: 在多个视觉推理基准上，ProAR 的两个互补组件均带来一致的性能提升；训练效率显著，仅用 25% 的训练步数即超越完全训练的标准 AR 基线；在具身推理任务上展现出应用潜力。

Conclusion: 显式稀疏目标监督与隐式稠密逐步引导相结合，可在适度计算开销下促进连贯、目标导向的推理过程，为自回归视频模型提供了一种目标导向推理的新范式。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video models excel at causal generation, but their reliance on next-chunk prediction confines them to a short-sighted, reactive paradigm. This limitation is particularly consequential for reasoning-oriented generation, where achieving a target outcome through valid intermediate states matters more than local visual plausibility. To address this challenge, we propose Learning Prospective Reasoning with Autoregressive Video Models (ProAR), a novel framework that transforms autoregressive video generation into a goal-oriented reasoning process. ProAR introduces two key components: (1) To anchor generation to the long-range outcome, we integrate goal-frame prediction into the autoregressive loop via an asymmetric attention mask, enabling the predicted goal frame to guide the generation of intermediate states without being disrupted by them. (2) To guide short-range transitions, we introduce future representation self-alignment to encourage current hidden states to anticipate upcoming temporal dynamics. By leveraging teacher-forcing in AR training, we extract clean future representations in a single forward pass and align current representations with them using a lightweight, training-only predictor. Together, these two mechanisms seamlessly combine explicit, sparse target supervision with implicit, dense step-wise guidance, promoting coherent, goal-directed reasoning progress with modest computational cost. Experiments show that ProAR's complementary components consistently improve performance across diverse visual reasoning benchmarks. The framework proves highly training-efficient, surpassing fully trained standard AR baselines using only 25% of the training steps. This paradigm also demonstrates promising applicability to embodied reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [94] [SigLIP2 for aerial fire risk classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03689)
*Yunus Serhat Bıçakçı*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将预训练的SigLIP2图像编码器迁移至航拍图像上的七类火灾风险分类任务，比较冻结编码器探测与全模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大规模视觉-语言预训练编码器（SigLIP2）在航拍火灾风险分类这一特定遥感应用上的迁移能力，并建立可复现的数据划分与实验框架。

Method: 构建公开FireRisk训练镜像的可复现划分，记录数据来源、预处理与模型选择流程；对比两种设置：冻结编码器线性探测（probe）与全模型微调（full adaptation），均使用单一训练种子并基于同一验证划分选择checkpoint。

Result: 验证集上全模型微调达到63.05%准确率与58.94%宏F1；冻结探测为55.95%准确率与50.19%宏F1，全微调明显优于探测。

Conclusion: 该初步结果为SigLIP2在该任务上的进一步评估提供了支持，但未在独立测试集或未见区域验证性能；配套代码为重复实验与更多视觉编码器对比提供统一框架。

Abstract: We examine the transfer of a pretrained SigLIP2 image encoder to seven class fire risk classification from aerial imagery. We introduce a reproducible partition of the public FireRisk training mirror and an implementation that records data provenance, preprocessing and model selection. Two initial runs compare a frozen encoder probe with full model adaptation. On the validation partition, full adaptation reaches 63.05% accuracy and 58.94% macro F1, compared with 55.95% and 50.19% for the probe. Both runs use one training seed and select their checkpoint on the same validation partition. These development results support further evaluation of SigLIP2 but do not establish performance on an independent test set or unseen regions. The accompanying code provides a common framework for repeated experiments and comparisons with additional visual encoders.

</details>


### [95] [FlowHMR: Physically Plausible Motion Capture from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03691)
*Zhanke Wang,Chengfeng Zhao,Qing Shuai,Jingzhong Lin,Heng Li,Zeyu Ling,Yuxin Wen,Jing Li,Di Kang,Chunchao Guo,Linchao Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowHMR 将单目视频人体运动捕捉重新表述为"视频条件运动生成"问题：先预训练流匹配模型生成多样运动候选，再用 GRPO 与保真度、物理可跟踪性双重奖励进行后训练，并发布 Wild-4K 数据集，物理跟踪成功率达 82.47%（基线 GVHMR 62.82%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频恢复全局 3D 人体运动存在深度歧义，直接回归易塌缩到"平均解"；且生成的运动不保证物理合理，导致基于物理的跟踪器经常失败。

Method: 回归->生成：以视频为条件预训练流匹配 (flow matching) 运动生成模型产生多样候选；随后用 Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 后训练，奖励包含（1）保真度奖励（与输入视频一致）与（2）跟踪奖励（物理控制器可成功跟踪），从而迁移输出偏好。

Result: 在自建 Wild-4K（约 4K 网络视频）上，定性+定量实验均优于 SOTA，整体运动保真度更佳；物理跟踪成功率 82.47%，最强基线 GVHMR 为 62.82%。

Conclusion: 将视频动捕转为条件生成 + 强化学习后训练，可同时提升视频保真度与物理可跟踪性，为野外单目人体运动恢复提供可行范式。

Abstract: We present FlowHMR, a framework for recovering physically plausible global 3D human motion from monocular video. Previous learning-based methods typically regress human motion directly from video and train the network with geometric supervision. However, recovering human motion from monocular video is inherently ambiguous in depth, and direct regression tends to collapse toward an averaged solution. Moreover, the recovered motions are not guaranteed to be physically plausible, so physics-based tracking of them often fails. To address these challenges, we formulate video motion capture as a video-conditioned motion generation problem and first pretrain a flow matching model for this task. Given an input video, the pretrained model generates diverse motion candidates, but not all of them are faithful to the video or physically trackable. We therefore post-train the model using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with two rewards. A fidelity reward encourages consistency with the input video. A tracking reward favors motions that a physics-based controller can track successfully. Together, these rewards shift the model's output preference, so the post-trained model stays faithful to the input video while producing more physically plausible motion. We further introduce Wild-4K, a large and diverse dataset of about 4K internet videos, for evaluating human motion recovery in the wild. Qualitative and quantitative experiments on Wild-4K show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods in overall motion fidelity and achieves a physical tracking success rate of 82.47%, compared with 62.82% for the strongest baseline, GVHMR.

</details>


### [96] [Decoding the Functional Roles of Register and High-Norm Patch Tokens in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03698)
*Neel Varma,Andrew Rufail,Dipika Khullar,Vasu Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文用稀疏自编码器（SAE）解读 DINOv2 中 register token 与高范数 outlier patch token 的语义与功能差异：register 特征偏高层语义，outlier 特征偏低层纹理/背景结构，且因果消融显示两者功能极不对称（48.17% vs 0.31% 表征相似度下降），支持自监督 ViT 的 token 专门化假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督 ViT（如 DINOv2）的表征很强，但内部 token 的功能长期缺乏解释。近期架构引入专用 register token 以抑制背景区域出现的高范数 outlier patch token，但这两种 token 的语义与功能角色尚未被系统界定。

Method: 在 DINOv2 的 register-token 与 outlier-token 激活上分别训练稀疏自编码器（SAE），并结合自动化可解释性流水线、UMAP 聚类与 CLIP 空间交叉校验进行特征语义分析，最后通过因果消融对比两类特征的功能重要性。

Result: register-token 特征与高层语义概念关联更强，outlier-token 特征更多对应低层结构性、背景与纹理主导模式；因果消融中破坏 register 派生高分激活特征导致表征余弦相似度下降 48.17%，而破坏 outlier 派生特征仅下降 0.31%，呈现显著功能不对称。

Conclusion: 结果提供了自监督 ViT 中存在 token 专门化的证据：register token 承载语义信息，outlier token 主要吸收低层背景/纹理成分。该工作属机制可解释性分析，可为表征理解提供参考，但未给出物理 AI/机器人等核心方向的可直接落地方法。

Abstract: Self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs), such as DINOv2, learn rich visual representations, but the functions of their internal tokens remain poorly understood. Recent architectures introduce dedicated register tokens to reduce high-norm out- lier patch tokens that emerge in background re- gions, yet the semantic and functional roles of both token types have not been fully established. In this paper, we analyze these roles by training sparse autoencoders (SAEs) on register-token and outlier-token activations in DINOv2. Using an automated interpretability pipeline, UMAP clus- tering, and CLIP-space cross-checks, we find that register-token features are more strongly associ- ated with high-level semantic concepts. Outlier- token features, by contrast, are more often associ- ated with lower-level structural, background, and texture-dominant patterns. Causal ablations fur- ther reveal a substantial functional asymmetry: disrupting top-activating register-derived features produces a 48.17% drop in representation cosine similarity, whereas disrupting outlier-derived fea- tures produces only a 0.31% drop. Together, our results provide evidence for token specialization in self-supervised ViTs.

</details>


### [97] [4DCodeBench: Benchmarking Agents on Inverse Graphics of Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03715)
*Ruihong Shen,Žiga Kovačič,Peter Kulits,Xingrui Wang,Zizhang Li,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Alan Yuille,Jieneng Chen,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 4DCodeBench 基准：要求智能体从视频中通过代码生成重建动态场景（4D 逆向图形），覆盖形变、流体、断裂等物理现象，构建可执行图形程序；评测发现前沿模型静态重建强但动态重建不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-代码生成与静态重建能力尚不清楚能否支撑对世界动态（物理仿真、复杂行为）的抽象建模，需要一个可量化、可追踪的测试平台来评估智能体是否真正理解并复现动态场景。

Method: 构建 4DCodeBench 基准：采集真实世界视频并合成覆盖多种物理现象（形变、流体流动、断裂）的场景；任务定义为将视觉观测翻译为紧凑的场景结构与动力学表示，并通过代码实现物理仿真等抽象以复现行为；对多个前沿模型进行大规模基准评测。

Result: 前沿模型在静态重建上表现较强，但这一能力未能迁移到复杂动力学的可靠重建；动态场景重建仍是明显短板。

Conclusion: 4DCodeBench 为“用代码解读世界动态”的智能体提供了可追踪进展的评测平台，明确了当前模型在 4D 逆向图形上的能力边界。

Abstract: We introduce 4DCodeBench, a benchmark for 4D inverse graphics through code generation, in which agents reconstruct dynamic scenes from video as executable graphics programs. To accomplish this, agents must translate visual observations into compact representations of scene structure and dynamics, by implementing abstractions such as physical simulations to reproduce complex behavior. To evaluate this capability, we curate a set of real-world videos and construct synthetic scenes spanning diverse physical phenomena, including deformation, fluid flow, and fracture. We perform extensive benchmarking of frontier models, finding that strong static reconstruction capabilities do not yet translate into reliable reconstruction of complex dynamics. 4DCodeBench provides a testbed for tracking progress toward agents that can interpret the dynamics of the world through code. Our benchmark is available at this https URL

</details>


### [98] [MoSE3: Learning World-Space SE(3) at Every Pixel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03716)
*Jiahuan Cheng,Zhiyi Li,Tian Xia,Ruojin Cai,Yilun Du,Qianqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MoSE3，首个前馈模型从单目 RGB 视频预测逐像素稠密 SE(3) 运动（世界坐标系下 6-DoF 刚体变换），并通过引入 Art-Kubric 合成数据集实现训练，在 SE(3) 估计与 3D 点跟踪上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密 3D 点跟踪只用每像素 3-DoF 平移曲线来建模动态场景运动，无法表达部件旋转，也无法判断哪些像素属于同一刚体。逐像素 SE(3) 能同时给出旋转、平移与运动分组，信息更丰富，但直接回归 SE(3) 困难：旋转位于弯曲流形上不适合欧氏回归，且 SE(3) 标注极难获取。

Method: MoSE3 不直接回归 SE(3)，而是联合学习两个中间表征——3D 点轨迹与刚性嵌入（rigidity embedding），在每个软刚体簇内可微拟合变换以恢复 SE(3)，从而支持端到端预测与监督。同时构建 Art-Kubric：面向关节物体、含丰富物理交互的大规模合成数据集，提供稠密 SE(3) 与刚性标签。

Result: 在刚体与关节物体的像素级、部件级、物体级 SE(3) 估计上达到 SOTA；在三个数据集上取得平均 3D 点跟踪精度 SOTA；尽管仅在合成运动数据上训练，对真实视频仍表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 逐像素 SE(3) 运动为动态场景建模提供了旋转、平移与分组统一的表征；通过点轨迹+刚性嵌入的可微拟合绕开流形回归与标注难题，MoSE3 在合成与真实动态场景中验证了其有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: Dense 3D point tracking has been a prominent paradigm for modeling motion in dynamic scenes, but a point track is just a 3-DoF translation curve per pixel: it captures where pixels go, not the rotation of the underlying part, nor which pixels move together as one body. We propose MoSE3, the first feed-forward model that predicts dense SE(3) motion from monocular RGB video, producing full 6-DoF rigid transforms at every pixel in world space. Per-pixel SE(3) motion offers a richer view of how a scene moves: rotation, translation, and grouping all at once. Directly predicting SE(3) is challenging: rotations lie on a curved manifold that is ill-suited to Euclidean regression, and annotations for SE(3) are particularly difficult to acquire. To address these challenges, MoSE3 predicts per-pixel SE(3) through two jointly learned intermediates, 3D point tracks and rigidity embeddings, and recovers SE(3) by differentiably fitting transforms within each soft rigid cluster, enabling end-to-end prediction and supervision. To close the data gap, we introduce Art-Kubric, a large-scale synthetic dataset with dense SE(3) and rigidity labels for articulated objects with rich physical interactions. MoSE3 achieves state-of-the-art SE(3) estimation at pixel, part, and object levels on both rigid and articulated benchmarks, and state-of-the-art average 3D point tracking accuracy across three datasets, while showing strong generalization to real-world videos despite being trained solely on synthetic motion data.

</details>


### [99] [Less Decoder is More Encoder: Geometric Representation Learning from Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03717)
*Keerthi Kaashyap,Dennis Anthony,Akshay Krishnan,Nhi Ngoc Nguyen,Jeremy Collins,James Hays,Shreyas Kousik,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SNAP 提出一种姿态条件局部解码器 + 潜空间重建目标的自监督编码器-解码器 Transformer，用于 Novel View Synthesis 驱动的几何表征学习，在五项任务上媲美专用几何监督方法，并展现涌现的视角不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于编码器的 NVS 方法表征质量差。作者认为问题不在监督信号不足，而在于两个不显眼的架构选择：空间表达能力过强的解码器稀释了场景编码器的表征能力；低层像素空间重建目标阻碍了特征学习。因此需要重新设计解码器表达性与重建目标空间。

Method: 提出 SNAP，自监督编码器-解码器 Transformer。两个关键设计：(1) pose-conditioned local decoder（姿态条件局部解码器），限制解码器的空间表达性以保护编码器表征；(2) latent-space reconstruction objective（潜空间重建目标），替代像素级监督。方法任务无关（task agnostic），仅用自监督训练。

Result: 在五项任务（视觉定位、位姿估计、点对应、深度估计、机器人操作）上与自监督表征方法竞争力相当，并与专用几何监督方法可比。其 patch 特征展现涌现的视角不变性，接近重度监督模型，且计算与数据预算更低；在相机位移下，标准 2D 表征崩溃时 SNAP 退化更平缓。

Conclusion: 限制解码器表达性可主动防止可迁移几何结构被抑制；NVS 若架构设计得当，能学到可迁移的多视图几何表征，无需几何监督标签，为任务无关的自监督几何表征提供了新范式。

Abstract: This paper examines the role of Novel View Synthesis (NVS) in geometric representation learning. In principle, NVS should reason about 3D scene structure, thereby enabling transferable multi-view geometric representations. Yet, existing encoder-based NVS methods yield poor representations. This is not because of a lack of supervisory signal, but rather due to inconspicuous architectural choices: \textit{spatially expressive decoders} that dilute representational capabilities of the scene encoder, and \textit{low-level pixel-space targets} that hinder feature learning. We present SNAP, a self-supervised encoder-decoder transformer that addresses both through a pose-conditioned local decoder and a latent-space reconstruction objective. SNAP is task agnostic, and we show that it is competitive with special-purpose geometry-supervised methods. SNAP also performs competitively against self-supervised representations across five tasks: visual localization, pose estimation, point correspondence, depth estimation, and robot manipulation. Remarkably, SNAP's patch features exhibit emergent viewpoint invariance that approaches heavily supervised models despite lower compute and data budgets. Under camera shifts where standard 2D representations collapse, SNAP degrades more gracefully, revealing that restricting decoder expressivity actively prevents the suppression of transferable geometric structure. this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Awomo-SimDataEngine: Agentic Simulation-ReadyWorld Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02274)
*Awomo-PhysicalRSI Team,Danjiao Ma,Enhui Ma,Haohan Liu,Heng Jia,Hui Shan,Jianhua Xu,Jiahuan Zhang,Jiangdi Xu,Kaiwen Guo,Kaicheng Yu,Linwei Zhang,Liyang Jin,Maochun Luo,Pengyao Niu,Shiwen Li,Shuangyu Feng,Tong Zhang,Tianheng Wang,Xin Wang,Xiangru Huang,Yongqiang Huang,Zhaozhi Wang,Zijian Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Awomo-SimDataEngine 是一个 agentic 系统，将资产/场景生成与机器人示教数据合成串联，通过图原生 harness 协调构建、验证与有界修复，并在 LIBERO-Plus 上验证了跨仿真器策略训练的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人训练数据不仅需要视觉上逼真的场景，还要求物体可交互、布局物理有效、任务可重复执行。现有生成流程往往只关注视觉合理性，割裂了资产生成、场景构建与示教合成的链路。

Method: 系统包含：共享资产服务（刚性/铰接物体，含 ISArt 的结构化部件与关节生成）；双路径场景生成（Unravel 从图像重建可编辑场景，SimForge 从文本构建单/多房间环境）；图原生 harness 协调构建、验证与有界修复，失败路由到责任模块并保留未受影响的场景状态；PolicyForge 将验证后的世界绑定到任务与机器人本体以生成可重放示教。

Result: 评估覆盖资产几何、场景质量与下游策略学习。在 MuJoCo 的 LIBERO-Plus 上，与 Isaac Sim 示教协同训练将 World-Action Model 的总体成功率从 77.17% 提升至 89.43%，目标与空间子任务成功率分别提升 31.66 和 6.25 个百分点；长时程任务增益较有限。

Conclusion: 生成数据对跨仿真器策略训练具有实用价值，但长时程任务仍是瓶颈。整体属于仿真数据引擎与 VLA/WAM 数据增强方向的工作，方法较完整但真实验证仅限于仿真环境。

Abstract: Generating useful robot-training data requires more than visually plausiblescenes: objects must support interaction, placements must remain physicallyvalid, and tasks must admit repeatable execution. We present\textbf{Awomo-SimDataEngine}, an agentic system that connects asset and scenegeneration to robot demonstration synthesis. Shared asset services providerigid and articulated objects, including structure-grounded part and jointgeneration with ISArt. Scene generation supports two complementary routes:Unravel reconstructs editable scenes from images, while SimForge buildssingle-room and multi-room environments from text. A graph-native harnesscoordinates construction, validation, andbounded repair, routing failures to the responsible module while retainingunaffected scene state. PolicyForge binds validated worlds to tasks and robotembodiments to produce replayable demonstrations. Evaluations cover assetgeometry, scene quality, and downstream policy learning. On MuJoCo-basedLIBERO-Plus, co-training with Isaac Sim demonstrations improves the overallsuccess rate of a World-Action Model (WAM) from $77.17\%$ to $89.43\%$. Goal and spatialsuccess improve by $31.66$ and $6.25$ percentage points, this http URL results support the utility of the generated data for cross-simulatorpolicy training, with more limited gains on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [101] [World-Calibrated Proposal-to-Action Flow for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02323)
*Jie He,Wei Li,Junwen Tong,Rui Shao,Wei-Shi Zheng,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProAct：世界模型校准的 proposal-to-action 框架，用提案专家与非各向同性源分布改进流匹配 VLA 策略，性能优于 π0.5 且去噪步数减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配 VLA 策略的生成源是与任务无关的各向同性高斯，既不条件于近期执行历史（丢失局部运动连续性），世界表示也往往只条件化传输动力学，而非决定生成的起点、允许偏差范围与可扩展的动作方向。

Method: 三部分：(1) Proposal Expert 用一步运动锚定的端点流匹配把近期动作转为场景感知假设，将生成初值置于示范动作流形附近；(2) World Expert 以该假设为软运动先验，预测任务一致的潜在未来；(3) 基于兼容性校准以提案为中心的各向同性/各向异性源，用有界每步范围控制偏差，并在条件数预算下用迹归一化低秩几何在平移、旋转、夹爪方向上分配细化。

Result: 相比 π0.5，在仿真与真实世界任务上性能提升，同时去噪步数减少 50%，推理延迟最多降低 25.8%，吞吐最多提升 34.8%。

Conclusion: 将生成源本身变为可预测的、由世界模型校准的各向异性提案分布，是提升流式 VLA 策略连续性、效率与性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Flow-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies generate action chunks by transporting samples from a task-agnostic isotropic Gaussian source. As this source is conditioned on neither recent execution nor predicted future evolution, (i) it discards the local continuity established by recently executed motion. (ii) Even when predictive world representations are introduced, they often only condition the transport dynamics rather than determine where generation starts, how far it may deviate, or along which action directions it may expand. Building on this observation, we introduce ProAct, a world-calibrated proposal-to-action framework that makes the generative source itself predictable. (i) To preserve motion continuity, a lightweight Proposal Expert converts recent actions into a scene-aware hypothesis via one motion-anchored endpoint flow-matching step, initializing generation near the demonstrated action manifold. (ii) To jointly capture intended scene evolution and proposal-future compatibility, a prospective World Expert treats the hypothesis as a soft motion prior while predicting the task-consistent latent future. (iii) From this compatibility, the model calibrates a proposal-centered anisotropic source, where a bounded per-step extent controls the allowed deviation and a trace-normalized low-rank geometry under a condition-number budget allocates refinement over coupled translation, rotation, and gripper directions. Compared with $\pi_{0.5}$, ProAct improves performance across simulation and real-world tasks while reducing denoising steps by 50%, inference latency by up to 25.8%, and increasing throughput by up to 34.8%.

</details>


### [102] [SoTa: Soft Tactile Skins for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02338)
*Jingyun Yang,Baiyu Shi,Timothy Yu,Haitian Liu,Alberta Longhini,Weichen Wang,Rika Antonova,Zhenan Bao,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SoTa——低成本电容式触觉皮肤，实现人手与机器手全掌覆盖且共享202个taxel的统一布局，支持人手-机器人协同训练，在三个接触密集任务上将平均成功率从22.8%提升至45.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视触觉机器人数据稀缺，因为灵巧演示依赖遥操作，限制了数据规模。而人类演示采集成本低得多，但前提是人手与机器手需装配具有对应信号、跨本体共享布局的触觉传感器。现有传感器难以同时满足柔性贴合、全掌覆盖与跨本体布局一致这三个条件。

Method: 设计多层柔性电容式触觉皮肤：采用织物电极与多层结构，可在室内自制，材料成本低于10美元/片，几何形状可定制以适配人手与机器人手。人机对应手指与手掌区域共享202个taxel的布局，因此可用同一个触觉编码器进行人机协同训练，无需学习跨传感器映射。通过循环加载测试与握拳折叠测试评估耐久性，并在三个接触密集操作任务上做策略学习实验。

Result: 10,000次加载-卸载循环后仍保持超过97%的初始响应范围，1,280次紧握拳折叠循环后走线保持连续。共享taxel布局支持单一触觉编码器的人机协同训练。三个接触密集任务中，加入触觉观测较纯视觉策略提升分布内成功率；在固定机器人演示预算下，加入人类演示使八种评测条件的平均成功率从22.8%提升至45.9%，且五种分布外条件全部改善。

Conclusion: SoTa以低成本、可自制、跨本体共享布局的方式解决了视触觉数据规模化中的硬件瓶颈，验证了人类触觉演示对机器人操作策略的有效增益。作者计划开源制作与使用这些皮肤所需的资源。

Abstract: A growing body of work suggests that tactile sensing gives robot policies contact information that complements vision in dexterous manipulation. However, visuo-tactile robot data remains scarce: dexterous demonstrations require teleoperating robots, which limits dataset scale. Human demonstrations are far cheaper to collect and offer a path to scale this data, but only if human and robot hands carry tactile sensors with corresponding signals. This requires sensors that conform to different hand geometries, cover the full hand, and share a common layout across embodiments. We present SoTa, a low-cost capacitive tactile skin that provides full-hand coverage on humans and robots while preserving a shared layout of 202 taxels across corresponding finger and palm regions. Our multilayer design with fabric electrodes enables in-house fabrication of thin, soft skins with customizable geometry for under $10 in materials per skin. The sensor retains over 97% of its initial response span after 10,000 loading-unloading cycles with traces retaining continuity through 1,280 tight-fist folding cycles. The shared taxel layout supports human-robot co-training with a common tactile encoder and no learned cross-sensor mapping. Across three contact-rich manipulation tasks, tactile observations improve in-distribution success over vision-only policies. With a fixed robot demonstration budget, adding human demonstrations more than doubles mean success across eight evaluation conditions, from 22.8% to 45.9%, improving success in all five out-of-distribution conditions. We plan to open-source the resources needed to fabricate and operate these skins.

</details>


### [103] [NEEDLEWORK: Offline Rewriting of Robot Data with Verified Local Stitches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02339)
*Juntao Ren,Yifan Hou,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NEEDLE：一种离线数据集增强算法，在高维机器人演示的观测之间插入短的、经过验证的动作桥（action bridges），仅用RGB图像、本体感知与回合级结果，无需新环境交互或特权状态，即可拼接轨迹、纳入失败轨迹，并通过专门采样技术将桥接动作纳入策略训练。真机任务上成功率平均提升21个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示中单条轨迹常低效或失败，但其中仍含可用行为。轨迹拼接（trajectory stitching）有望把这些行为组合成更好的训练数据，但在高维机器人数据中识别有效连接并验证其可行性很困难，已有方法多依赖低维状态表示。因此需要一种能在高维观测空间下自动建立并验证连接的数据增强方法。

Method: 1) 在记录的观测之间识别并生成连接，绕过次优绕路、扩大动作覆盖，并把失败轨迹并入原始数据集；2) 只使用RGB图像、本体感知和回合级结果，不进行新环境交互、不使用特权物体状态；3) 对候选桥接进行可行性验证（verified action bridges）；4) 提出采样技术，把被接受的桥接纳入策略训练，不合成中间图像、不丢弃原始演示，使策略在保持原数据覆盖的同时学习替代动作。

Result: 在真实机器人任务上，NEEDLE 相比每个任务上最强的基线方法，成功率平均提升约21个百分点。

Conclusion: NEEDLE 表明无需新环境交互或特权状态，仅凭高维视觉与本体感知即可完成有效的轨迹拼接式数据增强，并给出真实机器人上的显著性能提升，为VLA/模仿学习的数据增强提供了可直接复用的思路。局限在于仅验证于若干真机任务、桥接长度受限，且摘要未给出开源与规模细节。

Abstract: Robot demonstrations may contain useful behavior even when individual episodes are inefficient or unsuccessful. Trajectory stitching offers a way to compose these behaviors into improved training data, but identifying useful connections and verifying their feasibility is difficult in high-dimensional robot data, where many prior methods rely on low-dimensional state representations. We introduce NEEDLE, an offline dataset-augmentation algorithm that addresses these challenges by adding short, verified action bridges between recorded observations in high-dimensional robot demonstrations. First, NEEDLE identifies and creates connections that bypass suboptimal detours, broaden action coverage, and augment the original dataset with failed trajectories, using only RGB images, proprioception, and episode-level outcomes, without new environment interaction or privileged object state. Next, we present a sampling technique that incorporates accepted bridges into policy training without synthesizing intermediate images or discarding the original demonstrations, allowing policies to learn alternative actions while retaining the original dataset's coverage. On real-robot tasks, NEEDLE improves success rate over the strongest baseline on each task by an average of 21 percentage points. Videos and supplementary materials are on this https URL .

</details>


### [104] [Filter-Aware Fine-Tuning for Safe Humanoid Whole-Body Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02341)
*Pranit Mohnot,Christian Helten,Daniele Gammelli,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示人形机器人中"跟踪策略—安全滤波器"独立设计的根本性失配，并提出滤波器感知微调方法 CoFiT，大幅降低约束违反时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身安全控制通常采用"参考生成 + RL 跟踪策略"的分层架构，运行时用 CBF 等安全滤波器对跟踪器输出做干预。作者指出，把跟踪策略与安全滤波器视为相互独立会引入本质失配：滤波不仅改变执行动作，还改变诱导出的状态分布，从而使跟踪目标与安全修正相互冲突。
