Method: 通过案例研究系统化地分离并剖析三类失配：动力学失配、目标失配与信息失配，定位其成因；据此提出 CoFiT（Constrained Filter-aware Tuning），一种面向预训练跟踪器的滤波器感知微调方法，在训练中显式纳入滤波器的干预行为，使策略适应被滤波后的动作与状态分布。

Result: 在多种约束场景下，CoFiT 相比仅使用滤波器的训练将违反时间在 TWIST2 上降低 91%、在 SONIC 上降低 21%，同时所需的安全滤波器修正量更小。在 Unitree G1 实机上，TWIST2 下违反时间降低 83%，所有试验均无需操作员介入完成；而基线有 50% 的试验需要操作员急停。

Conclusion: 研究给出了策略—滤波器交互的可操作洞见，并确立了将学习型跟踪器与运行时安全滤波器集成的设计原则：安全滤波不应作为外挂后处理，而应与策略联合或感知式地协同训练。

Abstract: Safe whole-body motion is essential for deploying humanoid robots in unstructured environments. Modern humanoid control commonly separates reference specification from execution, with a planner, teleoperator, or motion generator providing a reference that a reinforcement-learning policy tracks through dynamically feasible whole-body control. Runtime safety filters, such as control barrier functions (CBFs), offer a promising approach for enforcing newly introduced constraints via interventions on the tracker's outputs. We show, however, that treating the tracking policy and safety filter independently induces fundamental mismatches, as filtering alters both the executed actions and the induced state distribution. We study this policy-filter interface through case studies that isolate dynamics, objective, and information mismatches, highlight their root causes, and use these insights to develop CoFiT (Constrained Filter-aware Tuning), a filter-aware fine-tuning method for pretrained trackers. Across diverse constraint scenes, CoFiT reduces violation time relative to filter-only training by 91% on TWIST2 and 21% on SONIC, while requiring smaller safety filter corrections. On Unitree G1 hardware, CoFiT reduces violation time by 83% for TWIST2 and completes every trial without operator intervention, whereas 50% of baseline trials require an operator stop. Together, these results provide actionable insights into policy-filter interactions and establish design principles for integrating learned trackers with runtime safety filters.

</details>


### [105] [SocialVLA: A Social Perception Gateway for Human-Reaction-Based Failure Detection and Recovery in VLA Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02360)
*Sofya Konstantinova,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Artem Lykov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SocialVLA 是一个本地化、策略无关的社会感知网关，将人类旁观者的自发言语/面部/语音反应转化为 VLA 操作策略的运行时中断信号，并在 Unitree G1 真机上验证了从社会反应到物理停机与恢复的完整闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略在执行中往往无法自我识别错误，而人类观察者对机器人异常行为会自发产生快速声音、表情或语言反应，这些互补信号目前未被利用作为运行时干预手段。

Method: 系统融合四类信号：因果副语言音频检测、视觉反应识别、显式停止短语与机器人相关性估计；采用异步首事件融合机制，由最早达到置信度的信号触发 VLA hold，另有独立语音通道采集口头纠正以支持继续、重启或修改指令。

Result: 15 名参与者、238 个标注干预片段与 1.038 小时非干预数据上，冻结离线回放达 54.6% 召回/69.5% 精确率，未过滤音视频融合召回 64.3%；相关性估计将误停机片段从 100 降至 57，精确率由 60.5% 升至 69.8%。对未见过的第 16 名参与者前瞻部署达 59.5% 召回/91.7% 精确率。检测到融合中位延迟 47.9 ms，门控到物理停机 336 ms，反应起始到停机 1.021 s。

Conclusion: 该工作证明存在一条完全本地化的通路，可将自发社会反应转化为 VLA 的物理中断与人主导的恢复流程，为 VLA 安全监控提供了策略无关的补充层。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies enable diverse robotic manipulation but can fail during execution without recognizing their own errors. Human observers provide complementary signals, as unexpected robot behavior can trigger rapid vocal, facial, or verbal reactions before failure is completed. We introduce SocialVLA, a local, policy-agnostic social perception gateway that converts spontaneous human reactions into runtime intervention signals for VLA manipulation. SocialVLA combines causal paralinguistic audio detection, visual reaction recognition, explicit stop phrases, and robot-relevance estimation. An asynchronous first-event fusion mechanism triggers a VLA hold from the earliest sufficiently confident signal, while a separate speech channel captures verbal corrections for participant-directed continuation, restart, or instruction revision. We evaluate SocialVLA on physical Unitree G1 manipulation using 15 participants, with 238 annotated intervention-worthy episodes and 1.038 h of non-intervention behavior. Frozen offline replay achieves 54.6% recall and 69.5% precision, while unfiltered audio-video fusion reaches 64.3% recall. Relevance estimation reduces false-stop episodes from 100 to 57 and increases precision from 60.5% to 69.8%. In prospective deployment on an unseen 16th participant, the frozen system achieves 59.5% recall and 91.7% precision. Median detector-to-fusion latency is 47.9 ms, VLA-gate-to-physical-hold latency is 336 ms, and reaction-onset-to-hold latency is 1.021 s. These results demonstrate a complete local pathway from spontaneous social reaction to physical VLA interruption and participant-directed recovery.

</details>


### [106] [Rethinking World-Action Model for Compositional and In-Context Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02368)
*Shukai Gong,Xuanran Zhai,Yintianrun Zhang,Ruopeng Cui,Ye Huang,Yiyang Fu,Dexuan Lyu,Chaojie Li,Xinyi Song,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,Mingyuan Jia,Yufan Deng,Jiaxin Fang,Bo Liang,Jiaxin Li,Yuxiang Gao,Hao Liu,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViGAR 提出分层框架，将长程组合操作分解为视觉子目标规划器与子目标执行器，二者共享预训练世界模型表征，并支持无需参数更新的上下文学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 world-action models 虽能联合预测短时程视觉未来与动作，但缺乏显式的子任务级推理，难以胜任长时程组合操作任务。

Method: 分层框架：子目标规划器依据当前观测与全局指令预测下一子任务的视觉子目标；执行器以该子目标为条件联合生成未来视觉轨迹与动作；两模块共享预训练世界模型表征；以全局目标图像作为上下文实现 in-context 子任务分解与行为调整。

Result: RoboTwin Clean2Random 基准上 Clean 82.00%、Random 67.02% 成功率，平均成功率超出最强基线 12.86 个百分点；五个组合任务与两个上下文学习任务的真实机器人实验验证有效。

Conclusion: 显式子任务级视觉目标推理结合共享世界模型表征，可显著提升长程组合操作性能，并赋予无需微调的上下文学习能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon compositional manipulation has become increasingly important for real-world robot deployment, where a single task involves multiple coordinated subtasks. Existing world-action models (WAMs) jointly predict short-horizon visual futures and actions, but typically lack explicit subtask-level reasoning. We propose Visual Goal-conditioned Action Reasoning (ViGAR), a hierarchical framework that factorizes manipulation into a visual subgoal planner and a subgoal executor. Given the current observation and global instruction, the subgoal planner predicts a visual subgoal for the next subtask. The subgoal executor then jointly generates future visual trajectories and actions conditioned on the predicted subgoal. Both components share a pretrained world-model representation, enabling task-level planning and action generation to benefit from common physical knowledge. Moreover, our framework naturally supports in-context learning: using a global goal image as context can induce different subtask decompositions and behaviors without parameter updates. On the RoboTwin Clean2Random benchmark, ViGAR achieves 82.00% and 67.02% success rates under the Clean and Random settings, respectively, surpassing the strongest baseline by 12.86 percentage points in average success rate. Real-world robot experiments on five compositional and two in-context learning tasks further confirm the effectiveness of ViGAR.

</details>


### [107] [Keep the Effect, Drop the Actor: Programmable Effect-to-Execution World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02398)
*Junyi Hu,Zhewen He,Zhenhua Li,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将演示编译为"效果程序"(物体3D关键点轨迹+抓取点+终止状态)，用四流独立流匹配的71.5M世界-动作模型PEWAM生成执行，使推理变为可编辑的程序化闭环重解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从演示中同时记录了"物体发生了什么"与"某条特定机械臂如何做到"，但机器人策略通常需要迁移到不同臂、不同任务、甚至单一人类视频上。需要一种与执行体解耦、只以任务效果为条件、可由人直接编辑的中间表征。

Method: 将单条演示编译为effect program：被移动物体的3D关键点轨迹、标记抓取位置的两点、去除演示者后的场景终止配置。PEWAM(71.5M参数)以流匹配同时生成effect、robot execution、action、terminal state四个流，各流有独立时间步，因此钳制program并采样execution即把推理变为"编程"，可从实时场景闭环重解。程序为坐标集合，故可通过平移终止状态、旋转接触点来编辑放置与抓取。

Result: LIBERO-Goal留出任务上，单条演示程序完成90个episode中的40个，而同骨干以目标图像或语言为条件、以及已发表的演示条件方法最多完成19个；在Meta-World三个留出类上超过最佳已发表结果(五类均值未超)。同一程序无需重训即可运行于四种机械臂；受推动扰动后重解完成60个中的23个，而回放演示仅6个。Franka上以其他任务真实演示微调后，由单条人类视频编译的程序完成40次试验中的36次，同骨干以视频末帧为目标图像仅22次。

Conclusion: 以"效果"而非"动作"为条件的程序化世界-动作模型，实现了跨机械臂、跨具身、跨来源(真机演示/人类视频)的演示复用，并赋予用户对抓取与放置的可编辑控制与扰动后的闭环恢复能力。

Abstract: A robot demonstration records two things in the same frames: what happened to the objects, and how one particular arm made it happen. We condition on the first. A demonstration is compiled into an effect program: the 3D keypoint trajectories of the objects that moved, two points marking where each was held, and the configuration the scene ends in, with the demonstrator removed. PEWAM, a 71.5M-parameter world-action model, generates effect, robot execution, action and terminal state as four streams with independent flow-matching times, so clamping a program and sampling the execution turns inference into programming, re-solved closed loop from the live scene. On held-out LIBERO-Goal tasks, one demonstration's program completes 40 of 90 episodes, where the same backbone given a goal image or language, and published demonstration-conditioned methods, complete at most 19; on three of Meta-World's held-out classes it exceeds the best published results, though not on the five-class mean. Because a program is a set of coordinates, a person can edit it: the placement follows a shifted terminal state and the grasp turns with rotated contact points. The same program runs on four robot arms without retraining, and after a push, re-solving completes 23 of 60 episodes where replaying the demonstration completes 6. On a Franka arm, fine-tuned on real demonstrations of other tasks, programs compiled from single human videos complete 36 of 40 trials, against 22 for the same backbone conditioned on the video's last frame as a goal image.

</details>


### [108] [Autonomous mobile robot operations logistics: a dataset of jobs, dispatch events and robot states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02428)
*Jan-Felix Klein,Yongkuk Jeong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoRoOp：记录实验室套件准备与供应场景中AMR连续九个八小时班次的运行数据集，关联任务规范、操作、调度事件与机器人状态观测，含1382个任务、4815个操作、19352个调度事件与140386条机器人状态观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMR在生产物流中承担物料运输，其运行受任务生成、调度与机器人控制共同支配，但缺乏同时串联任务层、调度层与机器人状态层的真实运行数据，难以支撑决策评估与扰动检测等研究。

Method: 在实验室套件准备与供应场景中，让AMR执行随机生成的套件供应、空箱补给与充电任务，跨九个八小时班次采集数据；采用分层记录结构，将任务规范、构成任务的操作、记录操作状态转移与结果的调度事件，以及包含位置、朝向、速度、单轮荷电状态与诊断信息的机器人状态观测相互关联，并保留延迟、导航受阻、操作失败等技术上有效但异常的观测，同时提供原始与清洗后的状态表。

Result: 数据集包含1382个任务、4815个操作、19352个调度事件和140386条机器人状态观测，并附任务生成时所用的套件规范；数据在真实运行的实验室环境中采集，保留了延迟、导航受阻和失败操作等有效异常观测。

Conclusion: MoRoOp为AMR运行提供多层关联的真实数据，可用于AI智能体在运行决策记录上的评估、扰动检测、操作预测、数据驱动仿真与事件日志分析等方向。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots (AMRs) increasingly perform material transport in production logistics, where their operation is governed by job generation, dispatching and robot control. We present MoRoOp, a dataset of AMR operations recorded in a laboratory kit preparation and supply scenario over nine eight-hour shifts. During each shift, an AMR executed stochastically generated kit supply, empty-box refill and charging jobs. The dataset links job specifications, the operations constituting each job, dispatch events documenting operation state transitions and outcomes, and robot-state observations comprising position, orientation, velocity, per-wheel state of charge and diagnostics. It contains 1,382 jobs, 4,815 operations, 19,352 dispatch events and 140,386 robot-state observations together with the kit specifications used during job generation. The dataset was recorded in an operating laboratory environment, and technically valid observations of delays, obstructed navigation and unsuccessful operations were retained. Both raw and cleaned robot-state tables are provided. Documented reuse directions include the evaluation of AI agents on operational decision records, disturbance detection, operation prediction, data-driven simulation and event-log analysis.

</details>


### [109] [OpenRUA: Robot-Use Agents Are Zero-Shot Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02459)
*Zhaoyang Chu,Earl T. Barr,Claire Le Goues,Peter O'Hearn,Mark Harman,Federica Sarro,He Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenRUA 提出零抽象（zero-abstraction）harness，仅给现成编码 agent 开放机器人原生 ROS 2 终端的访问权，将感知视为文件 I/O、操作视为写代码，无需定制抽象层与任务专用训练。用 Claude Code (Opus 5) 在 CaP-Bench 达 99.0%、LIBERO-PRO 达 87.0%，并观察到 agent 自发形成度量感知、运动控制客户端与闭环控制的涌现行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人编码 agent 多依赖人工构建的复杂定制 harness：预设专用工作流、提供 bespoke 接口，工程开销大且妨碍通用性。作者质疑这类额外 harness 工程是否必要——若成熟的机器人软件栈（ROS 2）本身已足够，是否可让通用编码 agent 直接以零抽象方式驱动机器人。

Method: 提出 OpenRUA，一种"workspace-as-harness"的极简 harness：不给任何专用抽象或 agentic 工作流编排，仅提供 ROS 2 文档与基础终端工具，让 agent 自行组织工作。核心重述为：感知=文件 I/O，操作=编写代码。接入现成 Claude Code（Claude Opus 5），零样本、无 bespoke primitive、无任务专用训练，作为视觉运动策略直接调用机器人原生接口。

Result: CaP-Bench 成功率 99.0%，LIBERO-PRO 87.0%，表明通用编码 agent 可经原生接口充当零样本视觉运动策略。涌现行为统计：96.80% 回合自发编写程序处理原始传感输入并推导度量测量；95.87% 回合自发构建运动控制客户端（如夹爪控制）；50.13% 回合自发构建基于传感器反馈的闭环控制程序。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 结论是复杂定制 harness 工程并非必要：把编码 agent 直接置于机器人原生软件栈上、不施加抽象层与工作流编排，即可取得高成功率。该方法支持"感知即 I/O、控制即编码"的统一范式，并揭示了通用编码 agent 在机器人任务中自发产生度量感知、工具构建与闭环控制等涌现能力。也提示未来应评估其在真实硬件、更长时程与安全性约束下的可扩展性。

Abstract: Coding agents are extending their reach into the physical world by writing and executing robot control programs. One might expect the agents to use the existing mature software stack that engineers have developed over decades to access sensors and control motion. Yet prior work primarily engineers complex custom harnesses to orchestrate agents for robot use, particularly by prescribing specialized workflows and providing bespoke interfaces. This raises the question: "Is such additional harness engineering necessary?" We introduce OpenRUA, a zero-abstraction harness that bypasses bespoke abstraction layers by providing off-the-shelf coding agents with only terminal access to the robot's native software interface ROS 2. OpenRUA employs a minimalist workspace-as-harness design, only offering ROS 2 documentation and basic tools while leaving the coding agent to organize its own work without orchestrating any agentic workflow. Within this workspace, OpenRUA recasts perception as file I/O and manipulation as coding. With Claude Code powered by Claude Opus 5, OpenRUA achieves success rates of 99.0% on CaP-Bench and 87.0% on LIBERO-PRO, demonstrating that an off-the-shelf coding agent can serve as a zero-shot visuomotor policy through the robot's native interface, without bespoke primitives or task-specific training. Under this minimalist design, further analysis reveals striking emergent behaviors of coding agents: (1) For perception, the agent spontaneously writes programs that process raw sensory inputs and derive metric measurements in 96.80% of episodes. (2) For manipulation, the agent spontaneously builds motion-control clients (e.g., gripper control) in 95.87% of episodes and closed-loop control programs (e.g., adjusting motion based on sensor feedback) in 50.13% of episodes. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [110] [RUL-Aware RRT*: Degradation-Balanced Motion Planning for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02469)
*Haibo Li,Zhiguo Zeng,Xu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RUL-aware RRT*运动规划方法，将关节剩余使用寿命（RUL）信息嵌入规划过程，自适应调整关节使用以抑制退化不均衡，在三种退化场景下延缓瓶颈失效并提升长期可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械臂长期运行下关节退化不均，最弱执行器提前失效，造成非计划停机与运维损失；传统运动规划不考虑关节健康状态，反而加剧这种不平衡。

Method: 在RRT*框架中引入关节剩余使用寿命（RUL）信息作为规划代价/约束，使规划器随健康状态演化自适应分配各关节的使用程度，从而平衡退化速率。

Result: 在全健康（FHS）、异质退化（HDS）、局部退化（LDS）三类场景中评估，结果显示该方法有效抑制退化不均衡、推迟瓶颈失效出现、提升长期运行可靠性。

Conclusion: 将健康反馈融入运动规划是提升持续磨损下机械臂耐久性与运行韧性的可行且鲁棒的技术路径。

Abstract: Robotic manipulators operating over long durations often experience uneven joint degradation, which causes the weakest actuator to fail prematurely, leads to unplanned downtime, and results in significant operational losses. Traditional motion-planning algorithms do not account for joint health conditions and therefore tend to exacerbate this imbalance during extended operation. To address this challenge, this study introduces the RUL-aware RRT*, a motion-planning method that incorporates joint remaining useful life information into the planning process and adaptively adjusts joint usage in response to evolving health conditions. The method is evaluated across three representative scenarios, namely the Full Health Scenario (FHS), the Heterogeneous Degradation Scenario (HDS), and the Local Degradation Scenario (LDS). The results show that the RUL-aware RRT* effectively suppresses degradation imbalance, delays the emergence of bottleneck failures, and improves the long-term reliability of the robotic system during extended operation. These findings demonstrate that integrating health feedback into motion planning provides a practical and robust pathway for enhancing the durability and operational resilience of manipulators subject to continuous wear.

</details>


### [111] [CriticHack: Evaluating Visual Rewards Under Robot Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02527)
*Jiaxuan Luo,Xingguo Xu,Shanshan Wang,Yuhan Zhou,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文揭示：针对学习型视觉奖励模型（Robometer）微调扩散策略，会在任务成功率上升的同时放大“操作错误物体”的失败率，且传统监控指标无法察觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型视觉奖励模型被广泛用于优化机器人策略，但存在奖励误判风险：模型可能给“操作错误物体”的执行打高分。作者希望验证优化此类奖励是否会使错误物体失败被系统性放大，并解释其发生机制。

Method: 在抽屉任务上，对扩散策略的全部去噪器参数基于Robometer奖励进行微调（5次训练），与基于模拟器任务完成信号训练（5次）对照，评估512个种子；并复现于已有奖励优化策略、原生扩散采样器、匹配初始策略距离，以及两个critic、两个优化器的约束策略实验；提出“tilt模型”解释KL正则化优化下结果频率偏移；跨26个约束设置做Spearman相关验证，并用冻结的结果验证器重定向优化。

Result: 奖励优化使任务成功率提升10.2个百分点，同时错误物体失败率上升10.9个百分点；而任务完成信号训练仅提升成功率（差异9.2点，95% CI 5.6–13.0），不放大错误物体失败。Robometer整体区分成功/失败AUROC为.81，但对错误物体失败打分略高于成功（AUROC .37）。tilt模型在26个设置下预测结果偏移，Spearman .89，包括任务成功率下降的情形；其官方success-termination配方也继承该错误。冻结结果验证器可纠正优化方向。

Conclusion: 优化学习型视觉奖励模型会放大奖励误判对应的失败模式，且过程指标看似正常，属于隐性失败；tilt模型可预测该现象，冻结结果验证器是有效的缓解手段。

Abstract: Learned visual reward models are increasingly used to optimize robot policies, yet a reward model can score an execution that acts on the wrong object as highly as one that completes the task. We show that optimizing such a reward can amplify these wrong-object failures while reward and task success both rise, so the signals a practitioner would normally monitor look healthy. We fine-tune every denoiser parameter of a diffusion policy against Robometer on a drawer task. Starting from a supervised policy with no prior reward exposure, five training runs raise task success by 10.2 percentage points and wrong-object failures by 10.9 points on 512 evaluation seeds, whereas five runs trained on the simulator's task-completion signal raise success without amplifying wrong-object failures (difference 9.2 points, 95% CI 5.6 to 13.0). The amplification recurs from a policy previously optimized against learned rewards, under the policy's native diffusion sampler, at matched distance from the initial policy, and across constrained-policy experiments with two critics and two optimizers. A tilt model explains when it occurs: under KL-regularized optimization, an outcome becomes more frequent whenever its expected reward under the initial policy exceeds the population average. Robometer separates successes from failures well overall (AUROC .81) but scores wrong-object failures slightly above successes (AUROC .37), so optimization raises both. The same model predicts the outcome shifts across 26 constrained settings (Spearman .89), including those in which task success falls, and Robometer's own published success-termination recipe inherits the error. A frozen outcome verifier redirects the same optimization toward the requested task.

</details>


### [112] [Real-time Event-camera Stereo Visual Odometry via Keytime Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02601)
*Nikan Nobari,Jonathan D. Gammell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种连续时间事件相机双目视觉里程计流水线，通过WNOA先验将估计状态降至关键时间点，同时以插值保持异步测量的原时间分辨率，在MVSEC/DSEC上实现实时且优于ES-PTAM的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具备微秒级时间分辨率与高动态范围，但朴素地对异步事件流做连续时间GP回归/WNOA会导致估计状态维度爆炸，无法实时；作者希望在保留异步测量原时间戳的同时将状态规模与稠密测量数解耦。

Method: 连续时间事件相机双目VO：以关键时间点（keytimes）为状态节点，采用物理建模的白噪声加速度（WNOA）先验对异步测量插值到其精确时间戳，从而在不丢弃异步特性的前提下降低状态规模，实现实时连续时间估计。

Result: 在MVSEC与DSEC数据集上实时运行，分别在22 Hz和6 Hz输出估计；除一条序列外均优于最先进的离散估计器ES-PTAM；所有有效序列的RMS相对误差为0.46 cm和0.038度，分别比ES-PTAM好11倍和15倍。

Conclusion: 用关键时间点+WNOA插值的连续时间建模范式可兼顾事件流原生时间分辨率与实时性，显著提升事件相机VO精度，为高频异步视觉里程计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Event cameras have microsecond-level temporal resolution and high dynamic range which make them more resilient to motion blur and poor illumination than standard frame-based cameras. Event-camera visual odometry (VO) pipelines maximize these benefits when they process the asynchronous event stream at the native temporal resolution. Continuous-time Gaussian process (GP) regression and a white-noise-on-acceleration (WNOA) prior can handle asynchronous measurements but result in a prohibitively large estimation state when applied naively. This paper presents a continuous-time event-camera stereo VO pipeline that maintains the native measurement times of asynchronous events while also running in real time. It reduces the estimation states to keytimes while maintaining full temporal resolution by interpolating measurements to their exact timestamps with a physically founded WNOA prior. This decouples the state size from the dense number of measurements without discarding their asynchronous nature. The real-time continuous-time VO pipeline is evaluated on the MVSEC and DSEC datasets. It provides estimates in real time that are more accurate than ES-PTAM, a state-of-the-art discrete estimator, in all but one of the tested sequences. The pipeline respectively provides estimates at 22 Hz and 6 Hz on MVSEC and DSEC and RMS relative errors of 0.46 cm and 0.038 degrees across all valid sequences, which were 11 and 15 times better than ES-PTAM, respectively.

</details>


### [113] [A Passive AI System for Verifying Physical State on Automated Liquid Handlers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02668)
*Junqiong Joanne Qiu,Zeckria Kamrany,Ananya Anand,Emma Vidal,Yunjia Johanna Bai,Nathan S Chen,Sean Son,Varada Abhyankar,Taryn Jakub,Eleazar Eskin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Labware Setup Checker：以外部消费级网络摄像头加浏览器端软件，在不改动 Opentrons Flex 硬件与固件的前提下，被动校验实验板位准备，报告缺失/错位/多余耗材。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化液体处理机器人执行数字协议时，仍需人工确认物理台面与实验意图一致，现有校验依赖操作者肉眼检查且需中断或改动机器人系统。

Method: 消费级摄像头外置拍摄台面，浏览器应用将台面分割为槽位，依据已知台面几何校正槽位分配，对耗材进行分类，并与参考协议库比对；系统独立于机器人控制器运行，不干预或门控运行。

Result: 在 SwabSeq RVP 预 PCR 台面准备中，跟踪某槽位由空到底座再到完整板-底座装配，在机器人运动前 29 帧全部识别出完成态；帧内 348 次精确匹配分类中 95.1% 与协议参考一致；480 帧部分遮挡储液槽中 6,720 次槽位分配全部正确；三种相机位置与两种光照下槽位分配 100% 正确、分类匹配率 97.73%；中位验证时间笔记本纯 CPU 2.10 s，实验室 AWS 部署下从采集到结果 4.24 s。

Conclusion: 外部摄像头与独立软件可在数秒内依据协议要求校验台面准备，使操作者在启动运行前复核物理设置。

Abstract: Automated liquid handlers execute digital protocols, but operators must assemble the deck and confirm that the physical setup matches the intended experiment. We present the Labware Setup Checker, a passive vision system that verifies deck preparation on an Opentrons Flex without modifying its hardware or firmware. A consumer webcam mounted outside the robot supplies images to a browser application. The checker segments the deck into slots, corrects slot assignments using the known deck geometry, classifies the labware, and compares the observed setup with a reference protocol library to report missing, misplaced, or unexpected labware. It operates independently of the robot controller and does not interrupt or gate a run. During SwabSeq Respiratory Viral Panel (RVP) pre-PCR deck preparation, the checker tracked a slot from empty to a base and then to the completed plate-and-base assembly, identifying the completed assembly in all 29 frames before robot motion. Across these frames, 95.1% of the 348 labware classifications for slots requiring exact matches agreed with the protocol reference. In 480 frames with partially occluded reservoirs, all 6,720 slot assignments were correct. Across three camera positions and two lighting conditions, slot assignment was 100% correct, and 97.73% of labware classifications matched the protocol reference. Median verification time was 2.10 s on a laptop using only its CPU and 4.24 s from frame capture to receipt of results during AWS deployment in the laboratory. These results show that an external camera and independent software can provide verification of deck setup against protocol requirements within seconds, allowing operators to review the physical setup before starting a run.

</details>


### [114] [DeltaWorld: Physically Consistent Interactive World Simulators via Action-Conditioned Latent Increment Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02691)
*Boyuan Hou,Xiaoge Cao,Chaofan Zhang,Shuo Wang,Shaowei Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 DeltaWorld，一种面向机器人操作的高物理一致性交互式世界模拟器。其核心为 Delta-LTM（预测动作诱发的潜特征变化并叠加到当前状态，而非直接预测下一潜状态）与交互感知潜对齐（构建反事实交互区域以监督交互相关潜变化），从而抑制物体穿透与过度形变。在 IWS 基准与自采跨机器人数据集上，FVD 降低 46.6%、LPIPS 降低 31.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模拟器可为机器人规划、策略训练与评估提供可扩展环境，但现有世界模型通常直接预测完整下一潜状态，难以捕捉机器人动作引起的细微变化，导致物体相互穿透、过度形变等物理不合理结果，损害长时程动作条件预测的可信度。

Method: 提出 DeltaWorld：(1) Delta Latent Transition Model (Delta-LTM)，建模动作诱发的潜特征增量并将其加到当前潜状态以得到下一状态，以凸显动作可分辨的变化；(2) Interaction-aware Latent Alignment，构造反事实交互区域并对交互相关潜变化进行监督，以缓解穿透与过度形变。

Result: 在 IWS 操作基准与自采跨机器人数据集（多本体、多任务）上评估；跨机器人数据集上相对 IWS 基线 FVD 降低 46.6%、LPIPS 降低 31.1%。

Conclusion: 通过增量式潜转移预测与交互感知对齐，DeltaWorld 显著提升动作条件视频预测的物理一致性与长时程稳定性，显示出在机器人操作世界模拟中的潜力。

Abstract: Interactive world simulators can provide scalable environments for robot planning, policy training, and evaluation by predicting action consequences while reducing reliance on repeated physical rollouts. To serve these applications, they must generate future image sequences that respond faithfully to robot actions and preserve the dynamics of robot-object interactions over long horizons. However, existing world models typically predict the entire next latent state and often fail to capture subtle changes induced by robot actions. Such omissions can produce physically implausible outcomes, including object interpenetration and excessive deformation. To address this limitation, we propose DeltaWorld, a physically consistent interactive world simulator for robotic manipulation. Our method introduces the Delta Latent Transition Model (Delta-LTM), which predicts action-induced latent feature changes and adds them to the current latent state to obtain the next state, rather than predicting the next latent state directly. To mitigate object interpenetration and excessive deformation in predicted future frames, Interaction-aware Latent Alignment is introduced to construct counterfactual interaction regions and supervise interaction-related latent changes. DeltaWorld is evaluated on the IWS manipulation benchmark and a self-collected cross-robot dataset covering multiple robot embodiments and manipulation tasks. On the cross-robot dataset, DeltaWorld reduces FVD by 46.6% and LPIPS by 31.1% relative to the IWS baseline. These results highlight the potential of DeltaWorld for long-horizon action-conditioned video prediction in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [115] [Who Went Where When on the Lunar Surface: Forensic Trajectory Analysis to Identify Byzantine Rovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02694)
*Lachlan Holden,Feras Dayoub,Melissa de Zwart,David Harvey,Tat-Jun Chin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"非合作行星巡视器取证轨迹分析"问题：在多巡视器共享部署区、仅有稀疏遥测的条件下，BYZANTINE（失准或伪造测量）巡视器可构造内部自洽且数量占优的伪测量，使真实"有罪"测量被鲁棒优化当作外点剔除；为此提出以"智能体可信度"而非单条测量有效性为推理对象的归因感知轨迹估计方法，在候选可信子集上比较内部与边界相对观测的统计一致性，仅用归属可信者的测量做位姿图估计，在合成与真实行星类比数据上显著优于鲁棒PGO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来行星表面任务可能由多个独立运行的巡视器共享同一部署区域，需要事后核验其是否遵守"月球安全区"等运行约束；但连续原位可观测性几乎不可得，只能依赖稀疏遥测（里程计、位姿先验、巡视器间相对观测）事后重建轨迹。更棘手的是存在恶意或失准的非合作巡视器（Byzantine agents），它们能产生内部一致、数量占优的伪造测量，使标准外点鲁棒位姿图优化把真实的不利证据判为外点，导致违规行为被掩盖。

Method: 归因感知（attribution-aware）轨迹估计：将推理粒度从"单条测量是否有效"提升到"哪个巡视器可信"。枚举/筛选候选可信巡视器子集，用其内部相对观测与跨子集边界相对观测相对给定先验的统计一致性打分，据此识别Byzantine巡视器；随后只使用归属于可信智能体的测量执行轨迹估计（位姿图优化）。

Result: 在合成仿真以及真实行星类比（planetary-analogue）轨迹数据上，所提方法能够识别出Byzantine巡视器，并给出比现有鲁棒位姿图优化基线显著更准确的轨迹估计。

Conclusion: 表明在多智能体、非合作、测量可被伪造的事后轨迹核验场景中，按"智能体可信度"归因而非按"测量有效性"做鲁棒性处理是必要且有效的；该问题设定与归因感知方法可用于行星安全区合规核查等取证任务。

Abstract: Future planetary surface missions are likely to involve multiple independently operated rovers sharing the same deployment region, raising the need to verify compliance with operational constraints such as Lunar Safety Zones. Because continuous in-situ observability is rarely available, such verification requires post-hoc reconstruction of rover trajectories from sparse telemetry, including odometry, pose priors, and relative inter-rover detections. We introduce the problem of forensic trajectory analysis for non-cooperative planetary rovers in the presence of Byzantine agents: rovers that provide miscalibrated or deliberately falsified measurements to support an incorrect trajectory. We show that standard outlier-robust pose graph optimisation methods are vulnerable in this setting, because Byzantine rovers can generate measurements that are internally consistent and numerous enough to make truthful incriminating measurements appear as outliers. To address this, we propose an attribution-aware trajectory estimation method that reasons over rover credibility rather than individual measurement validity. The method evaluates candidate credible rover subsets by comparing the statistical consistency of their internal and boundary relative detections against provided priors, and then estimates trajectories using only measurements attributed to credible agents. Across synthetic simulations and real planetary-analogue trajectory data, the proposed method identifies Byzantine rovers and produces significantly more accurate trajectory estimates than existing robust pose graph optimisation baselines.

</details>


### [116] [GeoScaffold: Learning Compact Geometric Latents via Reconstruction for Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02697)
*Yixuan Jiang,Wentong Li,An Liu,Zihao Xin,Fulin Tang,Cong Leng,Yang Gao,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoScaffold 提出一种"训练时一次性注入几何"的监督框架：先用训练轨迹深度图训练并冻结紧凑深度分词器，再以少量可学习几何查询 token 微调 Video-LLM 导航策略，使其隐状态重建深度、连通性与可通行性，从而把几何内化进策略权重，推理时不增加任何传感器、3D 编码器或工具调用，训练后脚手架组件全部丢弃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式 Video-LLM 导航策略直接从自我中心 RGB 与指令映射到低层动作，但继承自 2D 预训练，缺乏 3D 几何先验；现有几何增强方案（深度传感器、3D 编码器、逐步感知工具调用）都带来持续的推理开销，不利于边缘部署。

Method: 两阶段：1) 在训练轨迹的深度图上学习紧凑深度分词器并冻结；2) 引入少量可学习几何查询 token 微调策略，用其隐状态重建导航关键几何（深度、连通性、可通行性），使查询状态成为动作解码的紧凑几何隐表征，并通过共享权重将几何内化进主干表示。训练后丢弃分词器、目标生成器与重建头，主干与动作接口不变。

Result: 在连续 VLN 基准上持续优于领先的纯视觉导航器，且不增加推理期开销（无深度传感器、无 3D 编码器、无逐步工具调用），适合轻量边缘部署。

Conclusion: 为空间感知的具身导航模型提供了"训练期付费、推理期零成本"的实用范式，几何监督可被内化为策略自身能力，兼顾性能与部署轻量性。

Abstract: Recent vision-and-language navigation (VLN) systems increasingly adopt streaming Video-LLM policies that map egocentric RGB observations and instructions directly to low-level actions. Yet these policies inherit weak 3D geometric priors from 2D pretraining. Existing geometry-aware extensions charge a persistent inference-time price: depth sensors, 3D encoders, or per-step perception tool calls. We propose GeoScaffold, a geometric supervision framework that pays this price once, at training time, by internalizing geometry into the policy itself. It first learns a compact depth tokenizer on depth maps from the training trajectories and freezes it. It then fine-tunes the policy with a handful of learnable geometry query tokens, training their hidden states to reconstruct navigation-critical geometry such as depth, connectivity, and traversability. This supervision turns the query states into compact geometric latents for action decoding, and through the shared weights also internalizes geometry into the backbone's own representations. Like a scaffold, the tokenizer, target generators, and reconstruction heads are discarded after training, leaving the backbone and action interface unchanged. Extensive experiments show that GeoScaffold consistently outperforms leading vision-only navigators on continuous VLN benchmarks, offering a practical paradigm for lightweight edge deployment of spatially aware embodied navigation models.

</details>


### [117] [AdaTempo: Learning Shared Relative Tempo from Demonstrations for Faster Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02706)
*Jiale Cao,Yike Niu,Zhengrong Xue,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaTempo：自监督加速模仿学习视觉运动策略，利用演示中的共享相对节奏结构建立相位对应、聚合共识节奏、映射为连续加速曲线并重采样轨迹，使ACT/Diffusion Policy训练后直接内化更快节奏，最高3.57倍加速且成功-速度权衡优于均匀加速基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作演示往往节奏过慢，策略继承该节奏导致执行效率低；但全轨迹均匀加速不可靠，因为操作任务不同阶段对加速的容忍度不同（如抓取阶段需慢、自由移动阶段可快）。需要一种能按阶段差异化加速的方法。

Method: 自监督方法AdaTempo：利用多条演示中共享的相对节奏（relative-tempo）结构，先做阶段对应（phase correspondence），将各演示对齐后的相对节奏聚合成共识节奏（consensus），再映射为连续加速曲线（speedup profile），据此重采样演示得到加速训练轨迹；用标准策略（ACT、Diffusion Policy）在这些重采样轨迹上训练，将目标节奏直接嵌入行为，无需运行时选速或在线重定时。

Result: 在广泛评估中实现最高3.57×加速，成功-速度权衡优于原始策略及代表性加速基线。

Conclusion: 按阶段/相位建模相对节奏是实现视觉运动策略加速的一种有效且无需额外标注的自监督思路；示范重采样可在不改变策略架构、不增加推理开销的前提下直接部署加速行为。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies trained via imitation learning often inherit the unnecessarily slow timing of teleoperated demonstrations. Yet uniform speedup is unreliable because different phases of a manipulation task tolerate acceleration differently. In this work, we introduce AdaTempo, a self-supervised method that accelerates visuomotor policies by exploiting shared relative-tempo structure in demonstrations. AdaTempo establishes phase correspondence, aggregates the aligned relative tempo into a consensus, and maps it to a continuous speedup profile used to resample demonstrations into accelerated training trajectories. Training standard policies such as ACT or Diffusion Policy on these resampled trajectories directly embeds the desired tempo in the learned behavior, without runtime tempo selection or online retiming. Extensive evaluations show that AdaTempo achieves up to a $3.57\times$ speedup and yields a stronger success--speed trade-off than the original policies and representative acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [118] [RoboChemGym: A Protocol-Driven Generative Simulation Framework for Long-Horizon Chemical Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02708)
*Chenxi Li,Haiyuan Wan,Rui Li,Jingyuan Li,Sha Zhang,Bohan Feng,Jianbao Cao,Zhangrui Zhao,Di Hu,Wangmeng Zuo,Shixiang Tang,Minting Pan,Dongzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 RoboChemGym 框架，用于自动生成符合真实化学实验规程的高保真操作演示数据，并配套分层基准评估具身智能体能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湿实验是科学假设验证的金标准，但成本高、危险性大。具身智能体有望自动化化学实验流程，但缺乏真实世界训练数据；现有仿真方法多面向短时程、弱约束任务，无法满足化学实验严格的流程约束和精细操作要求。

Method: RoboChemGym 包含两部分：（1）自主生成高保真操作演示的机制，通过自改进任务合成（self-improving task synthesis）迭代优化任务执行与场景配置，可可靠生成超过 10 步交互的复杂多物体专家轨迹；（2）分层基准，从原子操作到全周期实验流程，系统评估不同粒度的性能。

Result: 框架可自动合成与真实实验协议对齐的长时程（>10 交互步）专家轨迹，并提供跨粒度的评估体系，为化学任务中具身智能体的数据合成与能力评测建立可扩展范式。

Conclusion: RoboChemGym 为复杂化学任务中具身智能体的自动化数据合成与能力评估提供了可扩展范式，是迈向全智能实验室的关键一步。

Abstract: Wet-lab experimentation serves as the gold standard for hypothesis verification in scientific discovery; yet it is inherently labor-intensive, costly, and safety-critical. Embodied agents hold the promise of automating these tedious workflows, but their development is hindered by the scarcity of real-world training data. While simulation offers a scalable alternative for producing demonstrations, current methods primarily target relatively short-horizon tasks with loosely structured interactions, failing to meet the strict procedural constraints and fine-grained manipulation demands of chemical experiments. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{RoboChemGym}, a framework that autonomously generates high-fidelity manipulation demonstrations aligned with real-world experiment protocols, featuring a \textit{self-improving task synthesis} mechanism to iteratively refine task execution and scene configurations, enabling the reliable generation of expert trajectories for complex, multi-object protocols exceeding 10 interaction steps. Furthermore, we introduce a hierarchical benchmark that systematically assesses performance across varying granularities, spanning from atomic operations to full-cycle experimental workflows. RoboChemGym sets a scalable paradigm for the automated data synthesis and capability evaluation of embodied agents in intricate chemical tasks, serving as a critical stepping stone toward fully intelligent laboratories.

</details>


### [119] [RoboBridge: A Self-Evolving Embodied Agent Framework for Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02717)
*Chenxi Li,Zhangrui Zhao,Rui Li,Yuan Gao,Kehui Liu,Jiarui Li,Dong Wang,Tong Si,Minting Pan,Wanli Ouyang,Dongzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboBridge将sim-to-real迁移重新定义为可执行任务技能的持续适应：以程序化技能（任务意图—观测—工具操作—结果验证）表示任务知识，利用真实交互反馈生成候选技能修订并经验证后持久化或拒绝，同时把预训练VLA策略封装为可复用动作工具并施加推理期引导，从而在不重训底层策略的前提下实现跨仿真与现实的细粒度执行与持续适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端VLA策略迁移到物理环境通常依赖视觉/动力学条件标定、额外目标域演示与再训练，成本高且部署后难以持续适应；现有工具型具身智能体侧重单环境内任务执行与经验复用，缺乏跨仿真—现实迁移与持续演化的程序性知识支持。

Method: 提出RoboBridge框架：将任务知识表示为连接任务意图、观测、工具操作与结果验证的技能程序；由交互反馈生成候选技能修订，经评估后持久化或拒绝；把预训练VLA策略暴露为可复用动作工具，并辅以推理期引导以在不重训策略的情况下实现细粒度执行；迁移性技能锚定在仿真与现实共享的任务语义与交互接口上，环境相关操作可被选择性修订。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO基准及对应物理任务上评估，研究了技能演化与迁移后适应两方面表现，验证了框架从一次性策略部署走向跨环境持续程序性学习的可行性。

Conclusion: 将sim-to-real迁移视为可执行技能的持续适应，可在保留可复用任务结构的同时通过真实执行反馈选择性修订环境相关部分，为跨环境的持续程序性学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A key challenge in bringing embodied intelligence into the real world is transferring capabilities from simulation to reality and enabling agents to continually adapt after deployment. End-to-end vision-language-action policies provide strong manipulation capabilities, but their transfer to physical environments typically relies on calibrating simulated visual and dynamical conditions, collecting additional target-domain demonstrations, and optimizing the policy through further training. Tool-using embodied agents offer flexible task orchestration, yet existing systems primarily emphasize task execution and experience reuse within a given environment, with limited support for transferring procedural knowledge and continuously adapting it across simulation and reality. We propose RoboBridge, a framework that treats sim-to-real transfer as the continued adaptation of executable task skills. The agent represents task knowledge as procedures connecting task intent, observations, tool operations, and outcome verification. Interaction feedback is used to generate candidate skill revisions, which are evaluated before being persisted or rejected. A pretrained vision-language-action policy is exposed as a reusable action tool and enhanced with inference-time guidance, enabling fine-grained execution without retraining the underlying policy. RoboBridge grounds transferable skills in task semantics and interaction interfaces shared across simulation and reality. This representation preserves reusable task structure while allowing environment-dependent operations to be selectively revised through real-world execution feedback. We evaluate the framework on LIBERO-PRO and corresponding physical tasks, studying both skill evolution and post-transfer adaptation. Our framework provides a route from one-shot policy deployment to continual procedural learning across environments.

</details>


### [120] [Around the World: Unified Learned Locomotion on a 270 g Continuous-Rotation Quadruped](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02728)
*Arturo Flores Alvarez,Nathan Lintu,Dennis Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MiNI-Q，一款270 g四足机器人，利用连续旋转腿部实现身体两侧支撑构型，用单一姿态条件化的强化学习策略在机载运行，统一正走、倒走与落地恢复，无需状态机或相位切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环学习式运动控制在商用四足上已成熟，但在亚公斤级尺度上仍不常见；连续旋转腿部提供了跨越身体两侧的支撑构型，其运动范围未被充分开发，且多模态行为通常依赖状态机切换。

Method: 采用单一姿态条件化强化学习策略，完全在机载运行；在环面T^8上定义连续关节空间参考及其重力条件变换，从而统一正走、倒走与落地恢复，无需状态机与相位切换；设计姿态与释放协同课程学习，并辅以执行器辨识、跨引擎验证与嵌入式部署。

Result: 不足10万参数的网络实现前向速度跟踪，正走RMSE 0.037 m/s、倒走0.050 m/s；30次释放试验中24次恢复行走；可在四种可更换足部几何与四种室内地面上运行；硬件实验与仿真消融表明性能来源于表示、条件化与训练设计。

Conclusion: 结果表明，借助环面关节参考与重力条件变换的单一策略可覆盖正向/倒置行走与落地恢复，且能在亚公斤级硬件上机载实时运行，验证了表示与课程设计对运动范围利用的关键作用。

Abstract: Closed-loop learned locomotion is established on commercial quadrupeds but remains uncommon at the sub-kilogram scale. Continuous-rotation legs give MiNI-Q, a 270 g quadruped, access to supporting configurations on either side of the body. We exploit this range with a single posture-conditioned reinforcement-learning policy that runs entirely onboard. A continuous joint-space reference on the torus $T^8$ and its gravity-conditioned transformation connect upright walking, inverted walking, and landing recovery without state machines or phase switching. Coordinated posture and release curricula train this behavior family; identified actuation, cross-engine validation, and embedded execution support hardware transfer. The same sub-100k-parameter network tracks forward velocity with RMSE of 0.037 m/s upright and 0.050 m/s inverted, resumes walking in 24 of 30 release trials, and operates with four interchangeable foot geometries across four indoor surfaces. Hardware experiments and simulation ablations connect these capabilities to the representation, conditioning, and training choices that exploit the platform's motion range. Demonstration videos and supplementary material are available on the project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [121] [CSIR: Contextually and Socially Informed Robots for Efficient Person Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02750)
*Tyler Chung,Nahl Farhan,Hao Zhang,Mingfeng Yuan,Jinjun Shan,Amy Wu,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PersonNav 任务与规划框架，融合距离与语义（习惯、意图）信息并按用户信息可信度加权，构建合成仿真基准（含人物、物品、请求），真机验证了语义-距离自适应权衡能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有找人系统依赖对目标个体的精确先验知识，适用于室内递送但过于刚性；学习类方法又受限于隐私导致的数据不可得或稀疏。需要在信息可及性受限、语言不确定的真实条件下实现机器人人物目标导航。

Method: 提出规划框架（含 LLM 变体）：将目标的距离信息与语义信息（用户习惯、意图）结合，以用户所提供信息的信任度加权；构建包含 actor、item、request 的合成场景基准，模拟自然语言人机交互；显式信念表示天然支持后续贝叶斯滤波。

Result: 知情搜索优于经典距离图基线；仅用语义导致无依据、零散搜索；相对基线取得显著提升，LLM 变体性能相当；硬件测试表明方法可支撑真实具身场景，并能在线在语义与距离信息间权衡。

Conclusion: 本文朝更智能的移动服务智能体推进，使其在真实环境中具备类人、有依据的搜索能力，可用于日常服务部署。

Abstract: We address the Person Goal Navigation (PersonNav) problem, enabling robots to search for people under realistic constraints on information accessibility and language uncertainty. This tackles the current solutions revolving around rigid person finding systems requiring exact knowledge of the individual for item-delivery in indoor settings. While the focus in literature is on learned methods using unavailable public or sparse data due to human privacy. We propose a planning framework that combines distance and semantic information about the recipient (habits and intent) weighted by the trust of the user-provided information. A synthetic benchmark of scenarios is also developed including actors, items, and requests to evaluate performance before real-world deployment aiming to simulate natural language human-robot interactions. Results show our informed search outperforms classical distance-based graph baselines, while semantics alone lead to ungrounded, sporadic search. Our method achieves strong gains over baselines, with an LLM-based variant performing comparably, and its explicit belief representation naturally supports future Bayesian filtering. Hardware tests demonstrate our method supports real-world embodiment able to leverage between semantic and distance information in a real setting. This work moves toward more intelligent mobile service agents capable of human-like, informed search in realistic environments to be leveraged in day-to-day use. this https URL .

</details>


### [122] [Proprioceptive Sketches as Long-Horizon Intent for Generative Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02759)
*Fangyuan Wang,Songhao Huang,Haoxiang Sun,Shipeng Lyu,Chengyang He,Anqing Duan,Peng Zhou,David Navarro-Alarcon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAM 用单个 transformer 去噪器联合生成"无时序的弧长参数化关节路径草图"与"稠密可执行动作块"，在仿真与真实双臂任务上提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器人策略只预测短动作块，缺乏显式长程意图；用语言计划、子目标图像或视频预测来表达长程结构代价高且仍需翻译为机器人运动；直接预测未来机器人运动又面临稠密时序轨迹参数冗余、短期几乎重复动作块、指导性弱的问题。

Method: 提出 Proprioceptive Action Models (PAM)：在单一 transformer 去噪器中联合生成紧凑、与时间无关的剩余关节空间路径草图（按弧长参数化，而非按时间），以及稠密可执行动作块；采用 block-causal attention 与 staggered denoising schedule 维持有向的 sketch-to-action 依赖，使动作 token 在采样全程条件于逐步变干净的草图。

Result: 仿真中在 Push-T 与 LIBERO-Long 上优于仅动作的基线；在四个真实世界双臂任务上将成功率从 47.5% 提升到 75.0%。

Conclusion: 用弧长参数化的时序无关路径草图作为长程意图表示，可在不引入高成本语言/图像/视频生成的前提下，通过协同去噪有效指导动作生成，并在真实双臂操作上取得显著增益。

Abstract: Generative robot policies predict short action chunks but lack explicit long-horizon intent. Recent methods expose longer-horizon structure through language plans, subgoal images, or video forecasts, which are costly to generate and still need to be translated into robot motion. Predicting future robot motions avoids this translation, but a dense, time-indexed trajectory requires numerous parameters to cover the full remaining task, and over a short horizon it largely repeats the action chunk and adds little guidance for action generation. We propose Proprioceptive Action Models (PAM), which jointly generate a compact, timing-free sketch of the robot's remaining joint-space path and a dense executable action chunk within a single transformer denoiser. The sketch parameterizes the path by arc length rather than time, capturing geometric intent invariant to execution timing. Block-causal attention and a staggered denoising schedule maintain directed sketch-to-action dependence, ensuring the action tokens condition on a progressively cleaner sketch throughout sampling. In simulation, PAM improves over its action-only counterparts on Push-T and LIBERO-Long; on four real-world bimanual tasks, it raises success from 47.5% to 75.0%. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [123] [Localized Conformal Safety Monitoring with Vision-Language Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02765)
*Luís Marques,Rong Fang,Disha Kamale,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SLLCP——一种冻结VLM之上的事后校准层，通过标签条件化的局部分割共形预测，将不可靠的碰撞预测转化为具有概率覆盖率保证的安全预测集合，在CARLA 15k轨迹上显著提升碰撞轨迹召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监测规划驾驶轨迹需要准确估计与其它交通参与者发生碰撞的可能性，而这些参与者的运动又受自车运动影响。现有经典方法受限于预测模型质量；VLM虽能推理高层动作后果，但其近似预测不适合自动驾驶等安全关键场景，因此需要一种数据驱动的、能对黑盒模型输出进行不确定性量化的后处理框架。

Method: 提出Split Label-Localized Conformal Prediction (SLLCP)：在冻结VLM之上加装事后校准层。采用"split"式共形预测流程，并引入标签条件化(label-conditional)与局部化(localized)机制——根据观测到的驾驶场景对相关历史经验加权，进而计算不确定性阈值，使得校准依赖于场景与该标签类别。理论上在可交换性假设下给出标签条件的有限样本、无分布覆盖率保证。

Result: 在来自未见场景的15k条CARLA轨迹上评估：SLLCP以Qwen为骨干正确标记89.6%的致碰撞轨迹，以Cosmos为骨干为88.4%；而基础VLM仅分别标记4.6%和39.1%的致碰撞轨迹。

Conclusion: 结合局部化与标签条件的共形校准能大幅减少被漏检的不安全轨迹，说明在安全关键的驾驶预测中，把不可靠的VLM输出经共形校准转化为带覆盖率保证的预测集合是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Monitoring planned driving trajectories requires accurately estimating the collision likelihood with actors whose motion is itself impacted by the ego motion. Existing classical approaches are often limited by the quality of their forecasting model. Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promise in reasoning about the consequences of high-level actions, yet their approximate predictions are unsuitable for safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving. Conformal prediction (CP) has emerged as a data-driven framework for quantifying the uncertainty of black-box model predictions. We propose Split Label-Localized Conformal Prediction (SLLCP), a post-hoc calibration layer over frozen VLMs that transforms their unreliable predictions into probabilistically calibrated safety prediction sets. We consider how the ability to estimate safety can depend on the observed driving scene and introduce a localized procedure that upweights relevant past experience when calculating uncertainty thresholds. We provide label-conditional finite-sample distribution-free coverage under exchangeability. Evaluated over 15k CARLA trajectories from unseen scenarios, SLLCP correctly flags 89.6% of collision-causing trajectories with a Qwen backbone and 88.4% with a Cosmos backbone, while the base VLMs only flagged 4.6% and 39.1% of the collision-causing trajectories, respectively. These results indicate that local, label-conditional calibration can reduce missed unsafe trajectories.

</details>


### [124] [SimpleTouch: Can Vision-Language-Action Models Master Contact-Rich Manipulation Without Tactile Policy Pretraining?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02784)
*Chen Yang,Linzhe Shi,Changjie Wu,Hang Zhang,Ronghan Chen,Lingjun Zhang,Xu Hu,Mu Xu,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimpleTouch 在冻结的 $\pi_{0.5}$ VLA 上接入触觉专家，仅用任务示教做单阶段训练，即在六个 UniVTAC 任务和四个真机任务上超越需大规模触觉策略预训练的 FTP 系列方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将触觉引入预训练 VLA 存在跨模态鸿沟：直接微调可能增益有限甚至掉点，因此现有方法通常依赖大规模触觉策略预训练或独立的视触觉对齐阶段，带来额外数据与训练开销。作者希望验证这些额外阶段是否必要。

Method: 在 $\pi_{0.5}$ 上增加一个触觉专家模块；使用冻结的预训练触觉编码器的全部 token，由动作监督与多步未来触觉隐变量（multi-horizon tactile latent）预测共同驱动学习；单阶段训练，仅用任务示教，不做触觉策略预训练，也不做单独对齐。

Result: 每任务 50 条示教下，六个 UniVTAC 任务全部取得最高成功率，平均 77.5%，高于 FTP-$\pi_{0.5}$ 的 45.2% 与 FTP-1 的 66.7%，分别提升 32.3 与 10.8 个百分点；四个真机任务平均 71.3%，比 FTP-1 高 8.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: 在已有预训练 VLA 与触觉表征的条件下，额外的触觉策略预训练并非强性能的前提，为接触密集型操作提供了一条更简化的路径。

Abstract: Tactile sensing provides essential contact information for robotic manipulation, yet incorporating it into pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models remains challenging. A common concern is that simply introducing touch during task-specific fine-tuning may fail to bridge the cross-modal gap, yielding limited gains or even reduced success. Consequently, existing methods often rely on large-scale tactile policy pretraining or separate visuotactile alignment, adding data requirements and training stages. We introduce SimpleTouch, a simple VLA extension that augments $\pi_{0.5}$ with a tactile expert, to test whether these additional stages are necessary. Leveraging all tokens from a frozen pretrained tactile encoder, the expert learns from action supervision and multi-horizon prediction of future tactile latents. This single-stage training uses only task demonstrations, without additional tactile policy pretraining or separate alignment. With 50 demonstrations per task, SimpleTouch achieves the highest success rate among evaluated methods on all six UniVTAC tasks. Its average success rate reaches 77.5%, compared with 45.2% for FTP-$\pi_{0.5}$ and 66.7% for FTP-1, corresponding to gains of 32.3 and 10.8 percentage points, respectively. Across four real-world tasks, it averages 71.3%, exceeding FTP-1 by 8.8 percentage points. These results demonstrate that, given pretrained VLA and tactile representations, additional tactile policy pretraining is not a prerequisite for strong performance on these tasks, offering a simpler route to contact-rich manipulation. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [125] [Skill2Real: Agentic Skill Learning for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02788)
*Xincheng He,Siyu Ma,Chang Yu,Yunuo Chen,Yanjia Huang,Ying Nian Wu,Yin Yang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Skill2Real 提出以共享 API 为接口的智能体式策略框架，通过 Proposer-Verifier-Governor 循环学习可执行技能，实现无微调的仿真到真机技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真到真实的机器人技能迁移需要跨感知、动力学与本体差异仍可复用的任务知识；现有方法常依赖任务级策略微调，迁移代价高、泛化性弱。

Method: Skill2Real 以统一应用编程接口（API）为技能表示基础。Proposer-Verifier-Governor（PVG）闭环利用仿真特权证据诊断执行结果并验证更新，同时约束技能只能依赖公开观测与 API 语义。Cerebellum 先获取底层局部操作技能，随后冻结 Cerebellum 由 Brain 学习任务级组合。两段记忆直接迁移至真机，不做任务策略微调，也不更新技能记忆。作者还分别用 GPT-5.6 Sol 训练、用 GPT-6 Astra 评估各冻结检查点。

Result: 在 LIBERO-90 上学习技能后，用 ASTRA 评估每个冻结检查点可将 LIBERO-Pro Long 成功率从 2.0% 提升至 56.3%，且未在 Pro Long 上训练。独立 Robosuite 训练在七个任务上分别达到 85.1%（Sol）与 89.4%（Opus 5）平均成功率。冻结的 Sol 训练 LIBERO-90 技能在四项真实操作任务上取得 78.75% 平均完成率。消融显示，移除 Verifier 或 Governor 分别使 Pro Long 最终成功率下降 17.3 与 13.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过统一的机器人接口可以学习并迁移可执行技能层级；仿真特权证据驱动的验证与治理机制对技能质量与跨域迁移至关重要，且迁移无需任务级微调。

Abstract: Transferring robotic skills from simulation to reality requires task knowledge that remains usable across differences in perception, dynamics, and embodiment. We introduce Skill2Real, an agentic policy framework that learns executable skills through a shared application programming interface (API). A Proposer-Verifier-Governor (PVG) loop uses privileged simulation evidence to diagnose outcomes and validate updates, while keeping learned skills grounded in public observations and API semantics. The Cerebellum first acquires local manipulation skills; the Brain then learns task-level composition with the Cerebellum frozen. Both memories transfer to the real robot without task-policy fine-tuning or skill-memory updates. As GPT-5.6 Sol learns skills on LIBERO-90, evaluating each frozen checkpoint with GPT-6 Astra raises LIBERO-Pro Long success from 2.0% to 56.3%, without training on Pro Long. Independent Robosuite training reaches 85.1% and 89.4% mean success with Sol and Opus 5 across seven tasks, respectively. Frozen Sol-trained LIBERO-90 skills achieve 78.75% mean completion across four real-world manipulation tasks with Astra. Removing the Verifier or Governor during LIBERO-90 training lowers final Pro Long success by 17.3 and 13.3 percentage points, respectively. These results support learning and transferring a hierarchy of executable skills through a common robot interface.

</details>


### [126] [ManiPhysicsBench: Physics-Based Assessment of Object Preservation in VLA Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02802)
*Sangwu Park,Yeonjun In,Wonjoong Kim,Sungwon Kim,Sein Kim,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出ManiPhysicsZoo（可复用的物体物理属性资产库）与ManiPhysicsBench基准，用基于求解器的方法从几何、材质与抓取条件计算抓取相关损伤阈值并对比接触力，从而评估VLA模型在LIBERO和SimplerEnv中的"保物"能力，发现任务成功率与安全成功率之间存在显著差距，并显示二值夹爪监督导致抓取命令趋同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型评估多依赖刚体操作基准的任务成功率，但任务完成不等于物体被完好保存：模型可能在抓取或操作中造成物体变形、破裂等不可见损伤。论文希望把"物体保存"（object preservation）纳入评估，检验VLA是否真正具备物理一致的抓取与操作行为，并探究夹爪监督信号的粒度是否是问题根源。

Method: 1) 构建ManiPhysicsZoo：整合文献支持的材质属性、3D网格与参考文献，形成可复用物体资产；2) 基于求解器的评估：由物体几何、材质属性与记录的抓取条件计算抓取特定的损伤阈值（变形/断裂），并与记录的接触力比较，判定潜在损伤；3) 在此基础上建立ManiPhysicsBench，在LIBERO与SimplerEnv上沿三个物理维度、三个难度等级评估物体保存情况，提出"安全成功率"（任务完成且保物）指标；4) 分析公开VLA检查点的夹爪命令分布；5) 用物体特定的连续夹爪标签重训练一个VLA模型，对比其抓取行为与泛化性。

Result: 公开VLA检查点任务成功与安全成功之间存在显著差距；其夹爪命令大量集中于接近全开与全闭，且不同物体上的总体分布高度相似，与二值夹爪监督一致。使用物体特定连续夹爪标签重训练后，模型表现出更强的物体相关抓取行为与更高的安全成功率，但任务成功率下降，且保物行为的泛化能力有限。

Conclusion: 任务成功率不足以衡量VLA的物理合理性，物体保存应成为独立评估轴。该工作提供了资产库、求解器评估与基准工具链，并指出夹爪监督粒度是制约物体保物行为的关键因素；连续夹爪标签能部分改善保物行为，但会牺牲任务性能与泛化性，说明数据与监督设计仍有较大改进空间。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models aim to perform diverse manipulation tasks, but task success in existing rigid-body benchmarks does not indicate whether they preserve objects. We introduce ManiPhysicsZoo, which consolidates literature-supported material properties, 3D meshes, and supporting references into reusable object assets. Using these assets, a solver-based assessment computes grasp-specific damage thresholds from object geometry, material properties, and recorded grasp conditions and compares them with recorded contact forces to assess potential deformation and fracture. Building on these components, ManiPhysicsBench evaluates object preservation in LIBERO and SimplerEnv across three physics axes and three difficulty levels. Public VLA checkpoints show a substantial gap between task success and safe success, defined as task completion while preserving the object. Their gripper commands concentrate near full opening and closure, with largely similar aggregate distributions across objects, consistent with binary gripper supervision. We examine how object-specific continuous gripper labels change model behavior by retraining a VLA model. The retrained model shows more object-dependent gripping and higher safe success, but lower task success and limited generalization of object-preserving behavior.

</details>


### [127] [LOCUS: Landmark-Oriented Container Discrimination Using Spatial Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02803)
*Taylor Bergeron,Shibani Senthilbabu,Kevin Leahy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LOCUS 用 GNN 在环境场景图上按邻近关系更新 CLIP 嵌入，并融合家庭本体论语义，实现"地标导向的容器判别"，从而推理"物体可能被放在哪"，在仿真与真实移动操作机器人探索流程中优于 CLIP、Tidybot 和 LLM 规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在非结构化真实环境中操作与导航时，需要同时依据物理合理性与语义可能性来判断"未直接可见的目标物可能位于哪个容器中"。单纯 CLIP 余弦相似度难以区分空间与语义上都相近的候选容器，缺乏对空间关系与语义知识的联合推理。

Method: 提出 LOCUS：在完整的环境场景图上训练 GNN，按节点邻近性在节点间传递嵌入信息，以更新 CLIP 嵌入；同时将 CLIP 嵌入与家庭本体论融合以提供稳健的语义信号。评估在仿真中进行，使用仿真元数据构建的无噪声场景图，使方法对检测环节不可知；并在真实移动操作机器人上接入包含 Detic 场景图标签的探索流水线验证鲁棒性。

Result: 在仿真多数房间类别与配置下优于随机、CLIP、Tidybot 和 LLM 规划器；在真实移动操作机器人的端到端探索流程（含 Detic 标签）中验证了对场景图节点摆放与标签噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将空间图推理与语义本体融合于同一嵌入空间，可有效提升非结构化环境中的容器/地标判别与目标搜索决策，且对真实感知噪声具备一定鲁棒性。

Abstract: As robots are increasingly deployed in unstructured, real-world environments, the ability to reason about complex spatial and semantic relationships among objects remains a fundamental challenge in enabling robust and generalizable manipulation and navigation. For example, deciding where to search for an object that is not in plain sight depends on where it is physically plausible as well as semantically likely. Popular methods such as cosine similarity with CLIP struggle to disambiguate between spatially and semantically similar objects that could contain a target object. We propose Landmark-Oriented Container Discrimination Using Spatial Graphs (LOCUS). To jointly reason about spatial information and semantics, we train a GNN to update CLIP embeddings on a full environment scene graph by passing embedding information between nodes based on proximity. CLIP embeddings, augmented by semantic knowledge from a fusion of household ontologies provide a robust semantic signal. We evaluate in simulation on a noiseless scene graph from simulator metadata, making the approach detection-agnostic. Our approach outperforms random, CLIP, Tidybot, and an LLM planner in the majority of room classes and configurations in simulation. To show our approach is robust to scene graph node placement and label noise, we demonstrate a physical mobile manipulator running in an exploration pipeline start-to-finish including scene graph labels from Detic.

</details>


### [128] [SARI: Phase-Split Sim-Real Co-Training for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02804)
*Xingxin He,Yuxuan Jiang,Haonan Zhang,Chuhan Cui,Kaile Li,Zhongxing Zheng,Caihao Xu,Ziqi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SARI：阶段拆分的仿真-现实协同训练框架，用仿真生成多样化自由空间接近轨迹、用少量真实演示采集接触交互，再通过视觉外观对齐与相机相对动作表示将两者拼接为单一策略，显著降低真机数据采集成本并提升未见位置泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集型操作任务中，VLA 模型依赖昂贵的真机演示，且难以泛化到新的物体摆放位置。作者的核心洞见是：空间覆盖能力与接触物理特性应分别从最擅长的域中获取——自由空间接近需要空间多样性但对仿真误差容忍度高，接触交互需要精确物理但在不同摆放位置上差异小。

Method: 提出 SARI 阶段拆分协同训练框架：在照片级数字孪生中批量生成多样化的仿真接近阶段轨迹，仅少量真实摆放位置采集接触阶段演示；随后用阶段切分的演示数据后训练单一策略，借助视觉外观对齐和共享的相机相对动作表示，让策略自动拼接仿真接近与真实接触，无需显式阶段标签或人工切换逻辑。

Result: 在五个真实接触密集型操作任务上，真机数据采集时间减少 34.3%；在未见摆放位置上成功率 27.5%，而所有整任务 sim-real 基线方法完全失败（0%）。

Conclusion: 按物理特性与空间需求拆分仿真与真实域数据来源，并以表示对齐方式隐式拼接，是降低 VLA 真机数据成本、提升摆放泛化的可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often require costly real-world demonstrations to adapt to contact-rich manipulation tasks, particularly when generalization across object placements is needed. We propose SARI (Simulated Approach, Real Interaction), a phase-split sim-and-real co-training framework built on a simple insight: spatial coverage and contact physics should be acquired from the domains best suited to them. Specifically, free-space approaches require spatial diversity but tolerate modest simulation gaps, making them ideal for synthetic generation; conversely, contact interactions demand accurate physics but vary little across object placements, allowing a few real demonstrations to generalize across the workspace. SARI generates diverse simulated approaches in a photorealistic digital twin while collecting real contact interactions at only a few placements. Post-trained on these phase-segmented demonstrations, a single policy seamlessly stitches simulated approaches with real contact interactions using visual appearance alignment and a shared camera-relative action representation--without explicit phase labels or hand-coded switches. Across five real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, SARI reduces real-data collection time by 34.3% and achieves 27.5% success at unseen placements, where all full-task sim-real baselines fail completely (0%).

</details>


### [129] [Learning Reflexive Behavior for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02811)
*Quan Nguyen,Yunho Kim,Joonho Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在仿真中习得的本体感受反射策略（映射任务空间指令到关节位置目标，仅用状态历史，不测力/几何），作为冻结执行层：在双臂举箱、轴孔装配（0.02 mm）和曲面跟随中降低接触力并提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作中，机器人与环境交互蕴含局部几何信息（表面防穿透、孔引导销轴）。常用位置控制、力位混合控制、笛卡尔阻抗控制的预设跟踪目标/刚度/力控方向可能与局部约束不匹配，导致性能下降。

Method: 在仿真中针对三个简单交互基元（弹簧、平面、导轨）学习本体感受反射策略；策略以状态历史为输入，将任务空间指令映射为关节位置目标，不使用直接力或几何测量；训练后冻结，作为上层控制器之下的执行层。

Result: 双臂举箱：反射策略将力保持在阈值以下而基线失败；粗糙表面跟随：低于 10 N 参考，与调参后的力位混合控制相当；0.02 mm 轴孔装配：平均估计接触力降至基线一半以下，硬件成功率从最高 22% 提升到 36–58%。

Conclusion: 将接触响应与指令生成解耦，使反射策略为运动规划器、学习策略和遥操作者提供鲁棒的接触丰富执行能力（无直接力/几何传感）。

Abstract: During contact-rich manipulation, interactions between a robot and its environment carry information about local geometry: a surface prevents penetration, a bore guides a peg. A controller that exploits these interactions can comply with environmental constraints while preserving task intent. Robot-earning systems commonly use position, hybrid force-position, or Cartesian impedance control. Their prescribed tracking objectives, stiffness, or force-control directions may not match local constraints and may degrade performance. We learn a proprioceptive reflex policy in simulation on three simple interaction primitives: a spring, a plane, and a rail. The policy maps task-space commands to joint-position targets using state history, without direct force or geometrical measurements. Once trained, it serves as a frozen execution layer beneath higher-level controllers. We evaluate it in dual-arm box lifting, peg insertion, and surface following. In box lifting, the reflex kept the force below the threshold while the baseline failed. In rough-surface following it stayed below the 10 N reference, on par with tuned hybrid force-position control. In 0.02 mm peg insertion It reduced mean estimated contact force to less than half that of the baseline while increasing hardware success rates from at most 22 % to 36-58 %. By separating contact response from command generation, the reflex policy provides motion planners, learned policies, and teleoperators with robust contact-rich execution.

</details>


### [130] [Register-Routed Delayed Fusion: Rewiring Shortcut-Prone Observation Fusion in Visuomotor Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02813)
*Jieting Long,Weidong Cai,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RRDF融合拓扑：屏蔽视觉与紧凑token的直接注意力，用register工作区延迟路由跨模态交互，保留紧凑条件给动作生成器，提升视觉响应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动模仿策略中，视觉token与本体感等紧凑token的融合拓扑决定了跨模态信息何时、经哪些token交互；密集token融合会让紧凑线索过早影响空间视觉表征，需探究控制该路由能否改善视觉响应与策略行为。

Method: Register-Routed Delayed Fusion（RRDF）：屏蔽直接紧凑-视觉注意力，通过可学习register工作区分阶段交互，采用"隔离-收集-路由"调度——早期保持流分离前缀，后期仅允许register中介交换，紧凑条件仍直接供给原生动作生成器。

Result: 在5个仿真任务与3个真实机器人任务上，RRDF在标称条件下匹配或优于dense ACT；4个仿真任务的外观偏移评测与3个真实机器人任务的留出位置评测亦更优；相位匹配输入探针显示状态-图像敏感度更低；消融表明仅添加register无法复现全部增益。

Conclusion: 控制跨模态传播路径同时保留紧凑动作条件，可提升视觉运动模仿策略的视觉响应性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Visuomotor imitation policies combine high-dimensional visual observations with compact signals such as proprioception, and their fusion topology determines when and through which tokens these streams interact. In dense token fusion, visual tokens may attend directly to compact tokens from the first encoder layer, allowing action-predictive compact cues to influence spatial visual representations early in their formation. We ask whether controlling this route improves visual responsiveness and policy behavior. We introduce Register-Routed Delayed Fusion (RRDF), which masks direct compact-visual attention and stages cross-modal interaction through a learned register workspace. Its isolate-collect-route schedule protects an early stream-separated prefix and later permits only register-mediated exchange, while compact conditioning remains available to the native action generator. Across five simulation tasks and three real-robot tasks, RRDF matches or improves dense ACT under nominal conditions. Appearance-shift evaluations on four simulation tasks and held-out-position evaluations on three real-robot tasks also favor RRDF. Phase-matched input probes show lower measured state-to-image sensitivity, while ablations indicate that adding registers alone does not reproduce the full performance gain. These results support controlling cross-modal propagation while retaining compact action conditioning.

</details>


### [131] [FastOPD: On-Policy Distillation for Lightweight VLA Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02832)
*Yoojin Oh,Jeongsol Kim,Yeonwoo Seo,Jangho Park,Seonghyun Jin,Sunwoo Park,Youngmin Kim,Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FastOPD，一种"基础模型到轻量模型"的 VLA 蒸馏框架，通过流映射（flow map）实现单步教师监督并结合自一致性目标，将大型 VLA 压缩为少步（1-2 步）学生策略，在仿真与真机上验证部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 基础模型规模快速扩张虽提升操作性能与泛化性，但推理算力开销大，难以真实部署。现有方案多靠缩小架构或减少 flow-based 策略的迭代去噪步数，前者损害能力、后者牺牲精度。作者希望在不重训大模型的前提下，把大规模 VLA 的能力蒸馏到可实时运行的小模型。

Method: FastOPD 采用 on-policy 蒸馏：为教师策略适配 flow map 以支持单状态（single-state）监督，再引入自一致性（self-consistency）目标，使学生模型学习教师动力学并可用极少步数采样。理论上证明最小化该目标可使学生分布逼近理想少步教师所诱导的分布。实验覆盖多种基础策略：LIBERO 上以 π0.5 为教师、RoboTwin 2.0 上以 LingBot-VLA 为教师，并延伸至 World Action Model 与真机 MolmoAct2 学生模型。

Result: LIBERO 上仅 2 步推理即保持 π0.5 的 84% 性能，推理延迟降低 78.1%，平均成功率优于现有少步蒸馏基线；RoboTwin 2.0 上以 LingBot-VLA 为教师时，单步成功率较基础学生提升 15.9 个百分点；并展示了对 WAM 的适用性，以及在真机上部署由 MolmoAct2 蒸馏的紧凑学生模型。

Conclusion: FastOPD 表明结合 flow map 单状态监督与自一致性目标的 on-policy 蒸馏，可将大规模 VLA 有效压缩为少步/单步策略，在保持较高性能的同时大幅降低推理延迟，为 VLA 的实际部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models have scaled rapidly to enhance manipulation performance and generalizability, but this scaling incurs high computational costs that render real-world deployment increasingly challenging. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by designing smaller architectures or reducing the iterative denoising steps in flow-based policies. In this work, we propose FastOPD, a foundation-to-lightweight VLA framework that enables the practical deployment of large-scale VLAs through efficient on-policy distillation. Specifically, FastOPD adapts a flow map for single-state teacher supervision and combines it with a self-consistency objective to construct a compact student that learns the teacher dynamics. Furthermore, we theoretically demonstrate that minimizing this objective allows the distilled student to recover a distribution on par with that induced by an ideal few-step teacher model. We evaluate FastOPD across diverse foundation policies in simulation and real-world experiments. On LIBERO, FastOPD retains 84% of the performance of $\pi_{0.5}$ with only two inference steps, reducing inference latency by 78.1% while outperforming existing few-step distillation baselines in average success rate. With LingBot-VLA as the teacher, FastOPD improves the single-step success rate over the base student by 15.9 percentage points on RoboTwin 2.0. We further demonstrate its applicability to a World Action Model (WAM) and deploy a compact student distilled from MolmoAct2 on a real robot.

</details>


### [132] [PointWAM: 3D World Action Modeling for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02840)
*Chunghyun Park,Beomjun Kim,Seungcheol Park,Heeseung Kwon,Yashu Shukla,Seunghoon Sim,Jinwoo Shin,Minsu Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PointWAM 将世界分解为场景与手部两组 3D 点轨迹，在共享时空坐标系内联合预测，并用人类视频预训练，再重定向为机器人动作，在 DexJoCo 十个任务上超越先前 SOTA 11.7 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 world action model 多以 RGB 帧或隐式表征建模世界，动作输出为末端位姿或关节角，难以刻画灵巧操作所依赖的 3D 空间结构与接触几何，且预训练常依赖任务特定的物体或关键点标注。

Method: 提出 3D 世界动作模型 PointWAM：把世界显式解耦为场景（环境）与手部（执行者）；输入彩色点云与语言指令，在共享时空坐标系中联合预测两者的 3D 点轨迹随时间的协同演化，再将预测的手部运动重定向为机器人动作。该表征支持大规模人类演示视频预训练，无需任务特定的物体或关键点选择。

Result: 在人类视频上预训练使 DexJoCo 平均成功率提升 56.9 个百分点；相比仅预测手部，加入场景轨迹监督额外提升 10.9 个百分点；两者结合后在十个 DexJoCo 任务上超过先前 SOTA 11.7 个百分点，并在真机上优于强 VLA 基线。

Conclusion: 显式解耦的场景—手部 3D 点轨迹联合预测是一种有效的世界动作建模范式，可显著提升灵巧操作性能，并具备从人类视频到真机机器人的可迁移性。

Abstract: World action models jointly learn to forecast world dynamics and predict robot actions, such that the learned internal world dynamics guide accurate actions. Existing approaches typically represent the world as RGB frames or latent counterparts while predicting actions as end-effector poses or joint angles, but they often struggle to capture the 3D spatial structure and contact geometry central to dexterous manipulation. We introduce Point World Action Model (PointWAM), a 3D world action model that decomposes the world into a scene (i.e., environment) and hands (i.e., actor), and jointly forecasts both as 3D point trajectories within a shared space-time coordinate frame. This explicit, disentangled representation enables effective pre-training on large-scale human demonstration videos without requiring any task-specific object or keypoint selection. Given a colored point cloud and a language instruction, PointWAM predicts how the scene and hands co-evolve in 3D space over time, then retargets the forecast hand motion to robot actions. Pre-training on human videos improves average DexJoCo success by 56.9 percentage points, and scene-trajectory supervision adds 10.9 points over forecasting the hands alone. With both, PointWAM surpasses the prior state of the art on ten DexJoCo tasks by 11.7 points and outperforms strong VLAs on a real robot.

</details>


### [133] [Permutation Robustness Is Not Enough: Action Collapse in Multi-Agent Transformer Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02848)
*Amit Thakur,Mukesh Singhal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究揭示多智能体Transformer策略在智能体顺序置换下的评估陷阱：低置换误差可能源于所有智能体输出相同动作（动作坍缩），而非真正的置换鲁棒性。作者提出结合置换一致性指标与动作坍缩诊断（动作多样性、同动作比例、最大动作频率）进行联合评估。结果显示PPO-ID基线行为不坍缩但对顺序敏感；强等变正则化会导致行为同质化；N=3时弱等变惩罚能在保持动作多样性的同时提升鲁棒性，而N=4需要更小的正则权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体团队本质上是无序集合，而Transformer通常将智能体作为有序token序列处理，这一结构性错配会影响协同导航策略。更关键的是，现有评估仅依赖置换误差或回报，可能误判策略的置换鲁棒性——一个策略看似对顺序不敏感，实际只是因为所有智能体都选了同一动作（动作坍缩），这种"伪鲁棒"掩盖了多智能体协作应有的差异化行为。

Method: 以多智能体协同导航为任务，研究智能体顺序置换对Transformer策略的影响。在训练层面引入等变正则化（强/弱两档等变惩罚），并与PPO-ID基线对比。评估层面同时采用两类指标：一是置换一致性指标（置换误差），二是动作坍缩诊断，包括动作多样性、同动作比例（same-action fraction）和最大动作频率。实验分别在N=3和N=4智能体规模下进行，考察不同正则化强度对鲁棒性与行为多样性的权衡。

Result: 低置换误差可能具有误导性，因为策略可能因所有智能体选择相同动作而"看似"鲁棒。PPO-ID基线呈现非坍缩行为但对顺序敏感；强等变正则化虽提升置换一致性，却仍可能诱发行同质化（坍缩）行为。弱等变惩罚在N=3团队中能同时改善鲁棒性并保留更丰富的动作多样性；而N=4团队则需要显著更小的正则化权重才能避免行为同质化。

Conclusion: 多智能体Transformer策略的评估不能只看回报和置换鲁棒性，还必须检验其是否维持非坍缩、差异化的多智能体行为。团队规模会影响可行的正则化强度，规模越大所需的等变惩罚越小。该工作提示需要将动作坍缩诊断纳入多智能体Transformer策略的常规评估流程。

Abstract: Transformer policies are attractive for multi-agent robot learning because self-attention can model interactions among agents. However, multi-agent teams are unordered, while transformers typically process agents as ordered token sequences. We study how this mismatch affects cooperative navigation policies under agent-order permutations. Our results show that low permutation error alone can be misleading: policies may appear robust simply because all agents choose the same action. We therefore evaluate policies using both permutation-consistency metrics and action-collapse diagnostics, including action diversity, same-action fraction, and maximum action frequency. A PPO-ID baseline yields non-collapsed behavior but remains order-sensitive, while strong equivariance regularization can still induce homogeneous behavior. A weak equivariance penalty improves the robustness while preserving more diverse actions for teams with \(N=3\) agents, whereas teams with \(N=4\) agents require substantially smaller regularization weights. These findings suggest that multi-agent transformer policies should be evaluated not only by return and permutation robustness, but also by whether they maintain non-collapsed, differentiated multi-agent behavior.

</details>


### [134] [SceneFactory-3D: Lifting 2D Traffic Scenes into 3D Physical Counterfactuals for Scalable Physically Grounded Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02874)
*Yicheng Zhu,Linfeng Tian,Tianmu Zhao,Yang Chen,Fan Zuo,Tao Li,Zilin Bian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SceneFactory-3D 是一个 GPU 批处理、物理耦合的多智能体驾驶模拟器：车辆通过悬架与摩擦受限的轮胎接触力执行加/转向指令，支持空间变化摩擦、每世界 3D 高度场、重力与刚性接触，可在并行世界中对同一交通场景仅改变路面条件做匹配反事实评估。基于此对 3 类学习策略（1,024 个 12 车世界）和 2 类经典规划器在 21 种摩擦/坡度条件下做敏感性研究，摩擦从 1.0 降至 0.18 时安全通过施工区比例下降 6–90 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可扩展驾驶模拟器通常用预设的行为或运动学规则执行车辆指令，忽略轮胎-路面接触物理，因而无法刻画恶劣道路与环境条件如何改变车辆执行并沿交通流传播。作者希望提供物理接地且可并行做“受控变量”反事实评估的仿真平台，以检验控制器对路面条件的敏感性。

Method: 构建 GPU 批处理的物理仿真器 SceneFactory-3D：在每个轮-地接触点求解受悬架与摩擦限制的力来执行加速度与转向指令；以空间变化摩擦系数、每世界 3D 高度场、重力与刚性接触统一约束车轮运动与底盘碰撞。通过每世界地形隔离 + GPU 批处理，实现交通场景与控制器的匹配反事实并行运行（只改路面条件）。实验上用 3 个学习策略家族（每条件 1,024 个 12 车世界）与 2 个经典规划器（共享 32 世界子集），覆盖 21 种摩擦与坡度组合。

Result: 摩擦系数由 1.0 降至 0.18 时，学习策略中安全通过施工区的车辆比例下降 6–90 个百分点（经典规划器为 18–19 个百分点），且所有学习策略的近距离碰撞（near-collision）情形均变得更频繁，显示学习控制器对路面条件存在显著且不一致的敏感性。

Conclusion: 物理接地的并行反事实仿真可作为评估与诊断驾驶控制器鲁棒性的有效工具；忽略轮胎-路面物理的规则化执行会掩盖控制器在恶劣路面下的失效模式，SceneFactory-3D 提供了可扩展的匹配对照实验范式。

Abstract: Scalable driving simulators typically execute vehicle commands using prescribed behavioral or kinematic rules, overlooking the physics of tire-road interfaces, thereby limiting their ability to capture how adverse road and environmental conditions alter vehicle execution and propagate through traffic. To address this limitation, we present SceneFactory-3D, a GPU-batched, physics-grounded multi-agent driving simulator. Vehicles execute acceleration and steering commands via suspension- and friction-limited forces evaluated at each wheel-contact point. Spatially varying friction, per-world 3D heightfields, gravity, and rigid contact consistently govern wheel motion and chassis collisions. Per-world terrain isolation and GPU batching enable SceneFactory-3D to run matched physical counterfactuals in parallel: traffic scenario setup and vehicle controllers remain fixed while only the road condition changes, enabling the resulting closed-loop effects to be evaluated across parallel worlds. To demonstrate the advantage of the SceneFactory-3D-enabled counterfactual evaluation, we conduct an empirical study on vehicle controllers' sensitivity to road conditions. We study three learned-policy families on 1,024 matched 12-vehicle worlds per condition, and two classical planners on a shared 32-world subset, across 21 friction and grade conditions. When friction drops from 1.0 to 0.18, the share of vehicles that clear the work zone safely falls by 6 to 90 percentage points across learned policies (18-19 for classical planners), and near-collision situations become more frequent for every learned policy. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [135] [MixVLA: Adaptive Mixing of Non-Invariant Information for Generalizable Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02898)
*Pingrui Zhang,Yu Zhang,Pengyuan Wu,Bin Wang,Haoming Song,Xianqiang Gao,ZhaxiZhuoma,Zhigang Wang,Dong Wang,Bin Zhao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MixVLA 训练框架，通过自适应混合非不变表征（AMI）对 VLA 模型进行正则化，无需额外 OOD 数据或改架构即可提升零样本泛化与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在机器人操作任务中进展显著，但分布外（OOD）零样本泛化受限。其根因是模型将任务相关的不变结构与环境特有的非不变因素纠缠，导致动作预测依赖虚假的表观线索（spurious appearance cues）。作者希望在不增加 OOD 数据、不改动模型结构的条件下提升泛化能力。

Method: 核心组件为「非不变信息自适应混合」（Adaptive Mixing of Non-Invariant Information, AMI）。AMI 随机混合非不变表征，对分布特异性的可变性进行正则化，同时保留互补的预测线索；随后将混合后的非不变特征与不变表征融合，用于最终动作预测。整体为模型无关（model-agnostic）的训练框架，可即插即用。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 扰动套件以及真实世界任务等具有挑战性的操作设定上进行了大量实验。结果表明 MixVLA 提升了整体零样本鲁棒性，同时保持在域内（in-domain）性能不下降。

Conclusion: 通过解耦并正则化非不变表征，MixVLA 以低成本（无需额外 OOD 数据、无需架构改动）的方式改善了 VLA 策略的泛化性与鲁棒性，且不牺牲策略表达能力。属建模层面的通用训练策略，可扩展至不同 VLA 骨干。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved remarkable advances in robotic manipulation, yet their zero-shot generalization under out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions remains limited. These models often entangle task-relevant invariant structure with environment-specific non-invariant factors, causing policies to rely on spurious appearance cues during action prediction. In this work, we propose \textbf{MixVLA}, a model-agnostic training framework that improves the generalization of VLA models without requiring additional OOD data or architectural modifications. The key component of MixVLA is \textbf{Adaptive Mixing of Non-Invariant Information (AMI)}. AMI stochastically mixes non-invariant representations to regularize distribution-specific variability while preserving complementary predictive cues. The mixed non-invariant features are then fused with invariant representations for final action prediction, resulting in improved robustness without sacrificing policy expressiveness. Extensive experiments across challenging manipulation settings, including LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, the RoboTwin perturbation suite, and real-world tasks, demonstrate that MixVLA improves overall zero-shot robustness while retaining strong in-domain performance.

</details>


### [136] [From Language Priors to Field Adaptation: Preference Learning for Traversability Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02974)
*Simon Schwaiger,David Seyser,Alessandro Scherl,Zlatan Ajanović,Wilfried Wöber,Gerald Steinbauer-Wagner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用冻结的视觉-语言特征空间中的von Mises-Fisher混合原型表示可通行性，通过相对自然语言规则先验与稀疏相对图像标注，实现样本高效的目标域自适应估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像可通行性估计高度依赖机器人平台、部署域与任务偏好，导致专用训练模型难以跨域泛化；目标域标注成本高，作者希望用样本高效的偏好学习减少目标域所需标注量。

Method: 在冻结的视觉-语言特征空间中以von Mises-Fisher混合原型表达可通行性；相对自然语言规则提供常识先验，稀疏相对图像标注通过计算与样本高效微调调整原型方向与效用，实现域自适应。

Result: 在WayFAST数据集上，精度与端到端训练估计器相当，同时实现样本高效的图像域自适应；定性实验显示语言先验的零样本适用性，以及微调估计器在语义地图上更优的稠密预测；并通过语义接近的自然语言提示剖析原型进行语义解释。

Conclusion: 该工作表明结合视觉-语言原型与相对语言先验可在少量目标域标注下实现可通行性估计的域自适应，兼顾精度与样本效率，并提供可解释的原型语义分析，代码与训练估计器已公开。

Abstract: Image-based traversability estimation is inherently dependent on the robot platform, deployment domain, and mission preferences, which limits the applicability of purpose-trained models. To facilitate domain adaptation, this work aims to reduce the number of required annotations in the target domain using sample-efficient preference learning. Our method represents traversability through von Mises-Fisher mixture prototypes in a frozen vision-language feature space. Relative natural-language rules provide a commonsense prior, while sparse relative image annotations adapt the prototype directions and utilities to a target domain through computationally and sample-efficient fine-tuning. Experiments on WayFAST demonstrate accuracy competitive with end-to-end trained estimators while enabling sample-efficient image-based adaptation. Qualitative experiments further demonstrate the language prior's zero shot applicability and the fine-tuned estimator's improved dense prediction on semantic maps. Evaluation is complemented via semantic interpretation of learned prototypes by dissecting semantically close natural language prompts. Code and trained estimators available at this https URL

</details>


### [137] [On Representational Alignment among Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02985)
*Fulvio Mastrogiovanni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"关系充分性"理论框架，用交互特定的关系证据映射刻画异步异构表征间的残余歧义，并给出光滑系统下所需独立标量锚点的局部一阶下界；在异步交接场景中验证相对同步无需锚点、绝对时间交接恰需一个锚点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体对同一物理过程可能持有异构、异步、观察者相关的内部表征。传统观点假定表征必须全局对齐，但作者质疑这一假设，转而研究：为了实现连贯交互，究竟哪些表征差异必须被消除？即需要一个任务相对的"何时停止对齐"判据。

Method: 形式化"关系充分性"概念：由交互特定的关系证据映射定义比较后仍遗留的表征歧义，当每个残余歧义纤维都落在某个交互等价类内时即满足充分性。充分性不成立时，需引入感觉运动锚点精确分离会改变交互相关结果的那些歧义。对光滑系统推导所需独立标量锚点数目的局部一阶下界——该下界等于"关系证据不可见但交互相关映射可见"方向子空间的维数。在一个异步交接场景的两个变体中实例化该理论。

Result: 两个变体共享相同的观察者、表征、传输与关系证据，但结论不同：相对同步变体不需要任何额外锚点，而绝对时间交接变体恰好需要一个锚点，与理论下界一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果为表征对齐提供了任务相对的停止条件：当残余分歧在交互意义下不变时，无须将其消除，从而避免不必要的全局对齐成本。
