Abstract: Embodied agents interacting with the same physical process may maintain heterogeneous, asynchronous, and observer-relative representations. Rather than assuming that such representations should always be globally aligned, we investigate which distinctions among them must actually be resolved for coherent interaction. We formalize this question through relational sufficiency: an interaction-specific relational-evidence map defines the representational ambiguity left unresolved by comparison, and sufficiency holds when each residual ambiguity fiber lies within an interaction-equivalence class. When this condition fails, additional sensorimotor anchors must separate precisely the ambiguities that can change the interaction-relevant outcome. For smooth systems, we derive a local first-order lower bound on the number of inde- pendent scalar anchors required, given by the dimension of directions that are invisible to relational evidence but visible to the interaction-relevant map. We instantiate the theory in two variants of an asynchronous hand-off scenario that share the same observer agents, representations, transports, and relational evidence. Relative synchronization requires no additional anchor, whereas hand-off at an absolute time requires exactly one. The analysis there- fore provides a task-relative stopping condition for representational alignment: residual disagreement need not be eliminated when it is invariant with respect to the interaction.

</details>


### [138] [Subject-Specific Predictive Musculoskeletal Simulations of Lower-Limb Exoskeleton Assistance: Metabolic and Biomechanical Effects of Joint Assistance Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02991)
*Neethan Ratnakumar,Mariya Tohfafarosha,Xianlian Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究用受试者特异性肌骨模型开展预测性仿真，系统比较下肢髋、膝、踝不同组合（25/50 Nm）助力策略对步态与代谢消耗的影响，发现髋+踝（H+A）是最高效的双执行器配置，全关节助力在50 Nm下平均降低COT约48.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下肢外骨骼虽能降低步行能耗，但最优助力策略设计仍困难，主要受个体在人体测量学与生物力学上的差异影响，缺乏对不同关节助力组合效应的系统量化比较。

Method: 基于六名健康受试者的受试者特异性肌骨模型（按BMI进行肌力缩放），用预测性仿真生成自选速度步态；引入理想执行器，模拟峰值25 Nm与50 Nm下多种关节助力组合，并通过COT、关节运动学、肌肉激活、助力力矩与关节功率等指标评估各配置的生物力学与能耗影响。

Result: 50 Nm下H+K+A组合降COT最多，平均48.50±4.55%（个体42.22%–54.65%）；单关节中髋最有效（31.77±5.21%），膝、踝相近且较小（19.17%、17.02%）；双关节中H+A最优（44.60±7.08%）。提高力矩上限主要增加髋、踝的正助力功率，膝助力敏感度低且在pre-swing期才输出正功率。

Conclusion: 结果支持H+A作为高效的双执行器助力目标；膝在pre-swing期的非典型功率策略值得作为后续针对性实验验证的候选方案。

Abstract: Lower-limb exoskeletons have made considerable progress in reducing energy expenditure during walking. However, designing optimal assistance strategies remains challenging, particularly given inter-individual variability in anthropometry and biomechanics. This study explores energy-optimal lower-limb joint-assistance strategies using predictive simulations with musculoskeletal models. Subject-specific models of six able-bodied subjects, with BMI-based muscle strength scaling, were used in predictive simulations to generate gait at self-selected walking speeds. Ideal actuators were incorporated to simulate various combinations of joint assistance at peak levels of 25 Nm and 50 Nm to examine the effects of assistance on gait and metabolic savings. The effects of each assistance configuration were assessed through cost of transport (COT), joint kinematics, muscle activations, assistive torques, and joint-level power metrics to characterize the biomechanical and energetic impacts of different assistance strategies. At 50 Nm, combined H+K+A (hip-knee-ankle) assistance resulted in the greatest mean COT reduction of 48.50 +/- 4.55%, with individual reductions ranging from 42.22% to 54.65% across the subjects. Among single-joint conditions, assisting the hip was most effective, reducing COT by 31.77 +/- 5.21%; the knee and ankle produced smaller, comparable reductions (19.17% and 17.02%). H+A (hip-ankle) assistance (44.60 +/- 7.08%) emerged as the most effective two-joint assistive configuration. Increasing the torque bound increased positive assistive power primarily at the hip and ankle, while knee assistance showed little sensitivity and delivered positive power near pre-swing. These results support H+A assistance as an efficient two-actuator target, while identifying the knee's atypical pre-swing power strategy as a candidate for targeted experimental validation.

</details>


### [139] [Safe Streaming Flow Planning by Aligning Sampling Dynamics with Execution Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03132)
*Seunghwan Jang,Jeongyong Yang,Siddharth Ancha,SooJean Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SafeStreamingFlow，一种流匹配/扩散式目标条件规划器，通过将采样动力学与执行动力学对齐（顺序积分状态向量场 + 分层状态预测），并用高阶控制屏障函数仅对执行步施加安全约束，实现低延迟在线重规划与更高安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流匹配规划器虽能从示范中学习长时域轨迹，但实际部署要求：(i) 执行期强制安全约束；(ii) 高执行频率下的紧耦合在线重规划。既有安全扩散/流规划器一次性生成整条轨迹并反复扰动中间状态，计算开销大，且因学习采样动力学与系统执行动力学不一致而引入分布偏移。

Method: SafeStreamingFlow：目标条件规划器，顺序积分学习到的状态向量场，并结合分层状态预测，使流采样动力学与执行动力学对齐；安全约束仅作用于当前执行步，通过高阶控制屏障函数（HOCBF）实现。

Result: 在导航、竞速（racing）和运动控制（locomotion）三类基准上，相比现有方法降低规划延迟、提升安全性，同时保持有竞争力的目标到达成功率。

Conclusion: 将流采样与执行动力学对齐、并只对执行步施加高阶 CBF 安全约束，是兼顾实时重规划与安全的有效范式；在多个机器人任务基准上验证了延迟与安全性的双重优势。

Abstract: Generative planners based on diffusion/flow matching can learn to synthesize long-horizon trajectories from demonstrations. However, real-world deployment requires (i) enforcing safety constraints during execution and (ii) tight online replanning at fast execution rates. Prior safe diffusion/flow planners generate the agent's full trajectory at once, while repeatedly perturbing intermediate states to satisfy safety constraints. This approach is not only computationally intensive, but also introduces distribution shift since the learned sampling dynamics is distinct from the system's execution dynamics. We propose SafeStreamingFlow, a goal-conditioned planner that aligns flow sampling dynamics with execution dynamics by sequentially integrating a learned state vector field with hierarchical state prediction. Importantly, we need to enforce safety constraints only for the executed step via high order control barrier functions. Across navigation, racing, and locomotion benchmarks, SafeStreamingFlow reduces planning latency and improves safety compared to existing methods, while maintaining competitive goal-reaching success.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Reward Hacking: Proxy Divergence Across Four Layers of a Staged Humanoid Learning Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03196)
*Arunabh Bora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出强化学习legged robot流水线中的奖励、课程门控、评估统计与参考运动四层均为形式意义上的"代理量"，各自具有特征性发散机制，并给出针对性的重构方案，用单笔记本GPU训练1.91m人形机器人PPO策略的实验佐证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点仅把奖励视为被优化的代理，因而将规范失效（reward hacking）局限于奖励本身。作者认为课程门控（代理能力）、评估统计（代理鲁棒性）、参考运动（代理可达技能）同属代理量，其发散是系统性而非孤立问题，需要统一的形式化分析。

Method: 对四层代理逐一给出：传统表述、发散条件推导、替代方案。包括：二次核平坦处改用一阶（L1）代价；用峰值/结果统计替代课程门控的均值；加入门控可达性与信息量检查；采用确定性、相位去同步的评估；把课程状态纳入模型；可行性优先的参考设计配合残差前馈；以及保持函数性的输入加宽使策略可增长而非重训。

Result: 在单一连续谱系的PPO模拟人形机器人（1.91m）上，四个阶段共13,500次迭代单卡训练中实测到：基于平均误差的课程门控在技能实际缺失时仍按速率上限推进；批量推力测试因同步重置与步态相位混叠，将0.5 m/s推力评为比2.0 m/s更危险。

Conclusion: RL流水线中的四层代理构成同构的形式问题，需按各自发散机制分别重构；实测证据表明均值型门控与同步化评估会系统性地掩盖真实能力与鲁棒性。

Abstract: A reinforcement-learning (RL) pipeline for a legged robot is assembled from proxies. A reward stands in for intended behaviour, a curriculum gate stands in for competence, an evaluation statistic stands in for robustness, and a reference motion stands in for an achievable skill. The traditional view treats only the first of these as optimised against, and so locates specification failure (reward hacking) in the reward alone. I argue that all four are proxies in the same formal sense, that each has a characteristic divergence mechanism, and that each admits a reformulation that closes it. For every layer I state the traditional formulation, derive the condition under which it diverges from its target, and give the alternative: first-order (L1) costs where quadratic kernels are flat, peak and outcome statistics where curriculum gates average, gate reachability and information checks, deterministic and phase-desynchronised evaluation, curriculum state treated as part of the model, feasibility-first reference design with residual feed-forward, and function-preserving input widening that lets one policy grow instead of being retrained. The arguments are illustrated by measurements from one continuous lineage of a PPO policy for a simulated 1.91 m humanoid, grown over four stages and 13,500 iterations on a single laptop GPU. Among them, a curriculum gate built on averaged error advanced at its rate limit on every check while the skill it gated was absent, and a batched push test whose synchronised resets aliased the gait phase ranked a 0.5 m/s push as more dangerous than a 2.0 m/s one.

</details>


### [141] [DexJoCo-X: Benchmarking Action Representations for Multi-Hand Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03278)
*Xiangwei Jiang,Yao Mu,Lixin Duan,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexJoCo-X：跨灵巧手形态学习的受控基准，含7种手、6类任务、2100条平衡演示；实验表明共享动作接口不足，需坐标-预训练-架构协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手形态日益多样，逐形态采集数据与训练策略不可扩展。跨具身学习需要既能捕捉共享操作结构、又保留形态特有控制的统一表示。已有研究因手、任务、数据集、控制接口不一致，无法分离表征、预训练与架构各自的贡献。

Method: 构建DexJoCo-X基准与工具包：统一场景、成功判据与执行接口，覆盖7种代表性灵巧手、6个单臂/双臂任务、2100条均衡演示；重新设计手套到手映射；提供自动化流程将经审核演示扩展到随机化场景。基于π0.5、Ego-Pi、Being-H0.5三类策略做受控对比，考察共享动作接口是否足以支撑多手学习。

Result: π0.5扩展至80维双臂输出成功率近零；Ego-Pi通过交错预测保留预训练动作头，可做单手多任务，但七手联合训练无效；Being-H0.5结合跨具身预训练、统一动作空间与具身感知专家，实现单一策略控制全部7只手。该架构下功能对齐动作槽平均成功率47.7%，原生坐标47.0%，DexLatent 33.1%。

Conclusion: 跨具身表示取决于整个学习系统：动作坐标、预训练与架构必须协同，才能将共享操作结构与具身特有控制分离；共享动作接口本身不足以实现多手泛化。

Abstract: As dexterous hands proliferate, collecting data and training policies separately for every morphology becomes increasingly impractical. Scalable cross-embodiment learning therefore requires a unified representation that captures shared manipulation structure while preserving morphology-specific control. Differences in hands, tasks, datasets, and control interfaces prevent existing studies from isolating the effects of representation, pretraining, and architecture. We introduce DexJoCo-X, a benchmark and toolkit for controlled comparison across seven representative dexterous hands, six single-arm and bimanual tasks, and 2,100 balanced demonstrations. DexJoCo-X provides a matched multi-hand, multi-task protocol with common scenes, success criteria, and execution interfaces, redesigned glove-to-hand mappings, and an automated pipeline that expands reviewed demonstrations across randomized scenes. Using $\pi_{0.5}$, Ego-Pi, and Being-H0.5, we examine whether a shared action interface is sufficient for multi-hand learning. Expanding $\pi_{0.5}$ to an 80-dimensional bimanual output yields near-zero success. Ego-Pi preserves the pretrained action head through interleaved prediction and supports per-hand multi-task learning, but remains ineffective for seven-hand joint training. By contrast, Being-H0.5 combines cross-embodiment pretraining, a unified action space, and embodiment-aware experts, enabling one policy to control all seven hands. Within this architecture, function-aligned action slots achieve 47.7% mean success, compared with 47.0% for native coordinates and 33.1% for DexLatent. These results show that cross-embodiment representation depends on the entire learning system: action coordinates, pretraining, and architecture must jointly separate shared manipulation structure from embodiment-specific control.

</details>


### [142] [HexVIO: Towards All-Day Stereo-Inertial Tracking Through Commodity DSPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03283)
*Patrick Wolf,Mateo de Mayo,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HexVIO 将双目-惯性里程计的视觉前端卸载至手机/XR 常见的 Hexagon DSP，在保持后端于主 CPU 的前提下优化 DSP 实现，实现平均 67% 功耗下降或 86% 吞吐提升，可 0.83 W 持续 30 fps 实时跟踪约 18 小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VIO 是空间计算的基础能力，但 CPU-only 实现在功耗、散热与续航上受限，难以支撑机器人、可穿戴与 XR 设备的全天候运行。作者希望利用消费级设备中已存在的 DSP 协处理器提升能效，而非定制专用硬件。

Method: 将 stereo-inertial 里程计的视觉前端（特征提取/跟踪等）迁移并针对 Hexagon DSP 架构（向量化、稀疏内存访问、并行化等）做专门优化，后端优化仍留在主 CPU，形成异构 CPU+DSP 的流水线。论文属系统实现与工程优化类工作，未见新算法或学习方法。

Result: 在商用智能手机上相比 CPU-only 执行，功耗降低 67% 或吞吐提升 86%；以 0.83 W 功耗维持长期实时 30 fps 跟踪，约合测试设备上 18 小时连续跟踪。

Conclusion: 结果表明消费级 DSP 足以支撑机器人与移动设备上的全天候视觉-惯性跟踪，为空间计算设备的低功耗长续航设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The ability of a device to localize itself within its surroundings is a fundamental prerequisite for spatial computing. Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) has proven to be a cost-effective and accurate solution for this task. Robots, wearables, XR devices, and drones can benefit significantly from efficient implementations of VIO since they allow for cooler, lighter, and cheaper devices with longer battery life and a better user experience. In this work, we propose to enhance the efficiency of a VIO system by leveraging the Hexagon DSP, a commodity co-processor present in many modern smartphones and XR devices. Our approach offloads the visual frontend of a stereo-inertial odometry system to the DSP while keeping the backend on the main CPU. By optimizing the implementation for the DSP architecture, we achieve significant reductions in power consumption and latency compared to CPU-only execution. Our system, HexVIO, demonstrates a 67% reduction in power consumption or an 86% increase in throughput on a commodity smartphone, with the ability to sustain long-term real-time 30 fps tracking for 0.83 W, corresponding to ~18 hours of tracking on the testing device. These results highlight the potential of commodity DSPs for enabling all-day visual-inertial tracking in robotics and mobile devices.

</details>


### [143] [Equivariant Visual-Tactile Diffusion Policy for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03333)
*Lik Hang Kenny Wong,Yiyao Ma,Xiu-Shen Wei,Zelong Tan,Zhuheng Song,Dongsheng Xie,Kai Chen,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VISTA：工作空间级等变视触觉扩散策略，将视觉与触觉观测投影为球形token，经置换等变球面融合注入接触线索，并用末端执行器朝向旋转调和表示，从而以高数据效率学习接触丰富的操作技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作模仿学习依赖高质量专家数据，采集成本高，因此样本高效策略是关键问题。视觉与触觉观测的异构性以及空间等变性（随末端位姿变化的动作一致性）未被现有视触觉模仿学习方法充分利用。

Method: VISTA在球面谐波域构建工作空间级等变表示：将视觉与触觉观测投影为球形token；通过置换等变球面融合把触觉接触线索注入视觉球面方向；利用末端执行器朝向旋转融合后的谐波表示；该表示条件化等变扩散策略以预测空间一致的action。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人设置中，VISTA相对强视触觉模仿学习基线的数据效率有显著提升（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 球面调和域的视触觉等变融合是一种有效提升接触丰富操作模仿学习数据效率的表示学习范式，具备仿真与真机验证。

Abstract: Imitation learning for contact-rich manipulation requires high-quality expert data that is expensive to obtain. This makes learning a sample-efficient policy a key issue. To address this, we propose VISTA, a workspace-level equivariant visuotactile diffusion policy for data-efficient contact-rich imitation learning. VISTA projects visual and tactile observations into spherical tokens, injects tactile contact cues into visual spherical directions through permutation-equivariant spherical fusion, and rotates the fused harmonic representation using the end-effector orientation. The resulting representation conditions an equivariant diffusion policy to predict spatially consistent actions. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world robotic settings show that VISTA substantially improves data efficiency over strong visuotactile imitation learning baselines. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [144] [KungfuAthleteBot: learning high-dynamic humanoid motion from video with unified robust recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03388)
*Zhongxiang Lei,Lulu Cao,Xuyang Wang,Tianyi Qian,Jinyan Liu,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KungfuAthleteBot (KAB) 提出把"从视频学习高动态人形运动"作为核心问题，通过物理引导的抛物轨迹校正、物理驱动的伪低动能(LKE)采样初始化，以及统一策略内的抗扰与跌倒恢复直接训练，实现在人形机器人上从视频学习动态技能并在约0.7秒内从任意跌倒恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频是廉价且丰富的人体运动数据源，富含极限运动行为，但视频重建轨迹存在物理不一致、缺少驱动力信息、且不包含失败与恢复信息三个失效模式，简单重定向无法用于人形机器人。作者主张"修复与补偿视频数据"而非"采集更多数据"才是解锁高动态人形技能的关键。

Method: (C1) 构建 KungfuAthlete 数据集（国家级武术运动员视频），引入物理引导的抛物轨迹校正，消除腾空与落地阶段的高度漂浮、地面穿透和高频抖动。(C2) 提出物理驱动的伪低动能(LKE)采样：将策略初始化偏向动力学可行状态，使策略发现可行驱动模式而非模仿不可行轨迹，避免误差驱动初始化反复从不可行空中姿态重启。(C3) 直接训练范式：将抗扰与跌倒恢复学习嵌入追踪视频运动的同一策略中，无需恢复参考数据与人工模式切换。

Result: 在人形机器人上，KAB 从视频学习动态技能，并能从任意跌倒中约 0.7 秒恢复，为已报道的统一策略中最快的恢复速度；针对统一策略的消融实验证实各组件（轨迹校正、LKE 采样、统一训练范式）的必要性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，修复与补偿视频数据（而非单纯扩大数据采集）是解锁高动态人形技能的关键；统一策略可同时实现在线运动追踪与快速跌倒恢复。

Abstract: Video is an abundant, inexpensive source of human motion data that is rich in extreme athletic behaviors. Making it usable for humanoid robots, however, is not a matter of simply retargeting a reconstructed trajectory: video-derived motion is physically inconsistent, devoid of actuation information, and says nothing about failure or recovery. We present KungfuAthleteBot (KAB), a framework that treats learning high-dynamic motion from video as the central problem and resolves each of these three failure modes in turn. (C1) We build the KungfuAthlete dataset from videos of national-level martial artists and introduce a physics-guided parabolic trajectory correction that removes height floating, ground penetration, and high-frequency jitter from reconstructed aerial and landing phases. (C2) Because video carries no force information, strict tracking of a reconstructed trajectory is dynamically infeasible, and error-driven initialization keeps re-launching the policy from infeasible aerial poses. We introduce physics-driven pseudo-low-kinetic-energy (LKE) sampling, our central mechanism for making such references learnable: it biases initialization towards dynamically feasible states, letting the policy discover feasible actuation patterns instead of imitating infeasible ones. (C3) Finally, we introduce a direct training paradigm in which disturbance rejection and fall recovery are learned inside the same policy that tracks the video motion, requiring no recovery reference data and no manual mode switching. On a humanoid robot, KAB learns dynamic skills from video and recovers from arbitrary falls in about 0.7 s, the fastest reported recovery for a unified policy. Ablations on the unified policy confirm the necessity of its components, supporting the view that repairing and compensating video data, rather than only collecting more of it, is what unlocks high-dynamic humanoid skills.

</details>


### [145] [I2CD: Direct Image-to-Convex Decomposition for Simulation-Ready Collision Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03453)
*Qian Wang,Liam Merz Hoffmeister,Brian Scassellati,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: I2CD 冻结预训练 Hunyuan3D-2 的 DiT 与 shape decoder，仅训练 38M 参数的 cross-attention 头，用可学习的 "convex-slot" token 直接回归 K 个凸多面体的半平面参数，从而由单张 RGB 图像端到端预测凸分解，无需修复/简化/AQD 后处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理仿真器与运动规划器需要凸碰撞几何，而 image-to-3D 生成模型输出的是稠密、常非流形的视觉网格。现有 bridging 方案是"先重建再分解"的串行管线（修复、简化、近似凸分解），速度慢且脆弱；因此需要一个直接从图像预测凸分解的方法。

Method: 冻结 Hunyuan3D-2 的 image-conditioned DiT 与 shape decoder，训练轻量 cross-attention head（38M 参数，<10 GPU 小时）；由学习的 convex-slot token 输出 K 个凸多面体的 halfplane 参数，构造上保证凸性，输出紧凑，无需任何后处理即载入物理引擎，约 0.5s/图。

Result: 在 227 个留出的 OmniObject3D 与 Google Scanned Objects 实例上，volumetric IoU 为八种 reconstruct-then-decompose 管线中最高，端到端快 6–37×；跨仿真器（MuJoCo、PyBullet、Genesis、Isaac Sim）中所有引擎可直接使用 I2CD 几何，而原始生成网格多数被静默替换为其他碰撞形状或需数秒至数分钟预处理；物理 xArm7 上 20 物体杂乱场景生成规划可用几何 11s（最强基线 328s），抓取放置成功率 85/100 vs 90/100。

Conclusion: 直接在生成骨架之上预测凸分解，可省去脆弱的后处理管线，在精度、速度与跨仿真器可用性上同时占优，并已具备真实机械臂规划就绪的落地证据。

Abstract: Physics simulators and motion planners require convex collision geometry, yet image-to-3D generative models output dense, frequently non-manifold visual meshes. Bridging the two today takes a slow, brittle reconstruct-then-decompose pipeline of repair, decimation, and approximate convex decomposition. We present I2CD, which predicts a convex decomposition directly from a single RGB image. Rather than train a new image-to-3D model, I2CD freezes the pretrained Hunyuan3D-2 image-conditioned diffusion transformer and shape decoder and trains only a lightweight cross-attention head (38M parameters, under ten GPU-hours) whose learned "convex-slot" tokens emit the halfplane parameters of $K$ convex polytopes. The output is compact, convex by construction, and loads into physics engines without any post-processing, in ${\sim}0.5$s per image. On $227$ held-out OmniObject3D and Google Scanned Objects instances, I2CD attains the highest volumetric IoU among eight reconstruct-then-decompose pipelines while running $6$-$37\times$ faster end-to-end. In a cross-simulator study in MuJoCo, PyBullet, Genesis, and Isaac Sim, every engine uses I2CD geometry as delivered, whereas raw generated meshes "load" everywhere but are silently replaced by a different collision shape in most cases or need seconds to minutes of per-object preprocessing. On a physical xArm7, I2CD produces planner-ready geometry for a $20$-object cluttered scene in $11$s versus $328$s for the strongest baseline, at comparable pick-and-place execution success ($85$ vs. $90$ of $100$ trials).

</details>


### [146] [MobiAgent: Dual-Loop Recursive Policy Self-Improvement for Long-Horizon Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03476)
*Chenzhi Liu,Yue Zhang,Jiehong Lin,Jianan Wang,Bo Wang,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MobiAgent：面向长时程移动操作的双环智能体框架，内环以VLM重规划+视觉反思组合原子技能（流匹配专家共享VLM主干），外环自动分割/验证部署数据并聚类发现新技能，实现终身学习，在BEHAVIOR-1K、RoboCasa与真机上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程移动操作面临执行误差累积，以及移动与机械臂控制之间的容量干扰；现有VLA模型擅长短时程任务却缺乏多阶段目标所需的分层推理，而已有分层智能体存在子任务映射僵化、重规划不灵活、无法持续学习等问题。

Method: 双环智能体框架。内环（部署）：解耦高层推理与底层控制，用VLM做滚动时域规划与视觉反思，动态组合可复用原子技能；技能由共享统一VLM主干的专用流匹配专家执行，提升复用率并缓解容量干扰。外环（学习）：自主分割与验证部署轨迹，聚类发现原子技能，无人工标注地持续微调技能库。

Result: 在RoboCasa、BEHAVIOR-1K及真实世界任务上验证：BEHAVIOR-1K上超过π0.5-TA 22.5个百分点，并能从执行失败中稳健恢复；通过自主数据回收，RoboCasa成功率由7.50%升至27.50%，Astribot S1由32.5%升至57.5%。

Conclusion: 分层双环设计（可组合技能+视觉反思重规划+共享主干专家+自动数据回收）可有效提升长时程移动操作的鲁棒性与持续学习能力，且在仿真基准与真实机器人上均得到验证。

Abstract: Long-horizon mobile manipulation presents significant challenges due to compounding execution errors and capacity interference between locomotion and arm control. While recent Vision-Language-Action models excel at short-horizon tasks, they lack the hierarchical reasoning required for multi-stage objectives. Furthermore, existing hierarchical agents suffer from rigid sub-task mapping, inflexible replanning, and a lack of continuous learning. To address these limitations, we introduce MobiAgent, a dual-loop agentic framework that bridges robust deployment execution and recursive policy self-improvement. During deployment, the Inner Loop decouples high-level reasoning from low-level control through highly composable atomic skills. It employs Vision-Language models for receding-horizon planning and visual reflection, dynamically composing skills to ensure robust error recovery. These skills are executed by specialized flow-matching experts that share a unified VLM backbone, maximizing reusability while mitigating capacity interference. Concurrently, the Outer Loop drives automated lifelong learning by autonomously segmenting and verifying deployment rollouts, clustering them to discover atomic skills, and continuously fine-tuning the skill library without human annotations. Evaluations on RoboCasa, BEHAVIOR-1K, and real-world tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of MobiAgent. It outperforms $\pi_{0.5}$-TA by 22.5 percentage points on BEHAVIOR-1K and enables robust recovery from execution failures. Through autonomous data recycling, success improves from 7.50% to 27.50% on RoboCasa and from 32.5% to 57.5% on Astribot S1.

</details>


### [147] [Detect and Suppress: A Mechanistic Defense against Adversarial Patches in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03498)
*Yukiya Horiba,Koshiro Aoki,Shunsuke Yasuki,Bum Jun Kim,Taiki Miyanishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用稀疏自编码器(SAE)定位VLA模型中与对抗补丁相关的内部特征，并在检测到攻击时条件性地抑制该特征，从而在不微调的前提下提升LIBERO-10上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗补丁可操纵视觉观测使VLA策略失效，但其内部机制不明，且缺乏针对性干预手段；作者希望从可解释性角度找到可干预的表征目标，并避免微调带来的开销与"何时干预"的扰动问题。

Method: 在VLA表征上训练稀疏自编码器做机制分析，识别出一个激活与对抗补丁存在强相关的特征；推理时先用线性探针检测攻击，仅在判定为攻击时抑制该特征（条件干预），并与持续干预、不干预进行对比。

Result: 在LIBERO-10上对抗补丁攻击下，条件干预在间歇性攻击场景中提升了成功率；而持续施加相同干预会显著损害策略性能。

Conclusion: 攻击相关的内部表征可作为VLA对抗防御的有效靶点；干预的时机控制对限制对正常策略行为的破坏至关重要，条件性干预优于无条件持续性干预。

Abstract: Adversarial patches can disrupt Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models by manipulating visual observations, leading to failures in robot control. However, it remains poorly understood which internal mechanisms underlie these failures and how targeted interventions can mitigate them. In this work, we mechanistically analyze VLA representations using a sparse autoencoder (SAE) and identify a feature whose activation strongly correlates with the presence of an adversarial patch. Based on this analysis, we suppress the identified feature at inference time only when a linear probe detects an attack. This intervention improves robustness without the cost of fine-tuning the VLA. We evaluate our method against VLA adversarial patch attacks on LIBERO-10. Conditional intervention improves success rate under intermittent attacks, whereas continuously applying the same intervention substantially degrades policy performance. These results show that attack-related internal representations can provide useful targets for VLA adversarial defense and that controlling when to intervene is important for limiting disruption to nominal policy behavior.

</details>


### [148] [XGenAct: Geometry-Enhanced World Action Models through Cross-Task Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03516)
*Tingting Du,Ziyao Wang,Guoheng Sun,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XGenAct是一个world action model，通过确定性编码器将RGB观测、机器人动作、metric depth、表面法线和functional role segmentation统一表示为RGB视频，用单一视频扩散transformer和一个目标函数学习多空间时序预测，在RLBench上取得52%成功率（对比最强基线26%），并更准确预测未来深度与分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有world action model主要预测RGB和动作的未来演化，未显式建模机器人操作所需的空间理解；已有工作通过专用head或分支添加少量空间预测任务，导致空间监督范围有限、模型架构碎片化。

Method: 用确定性codec将RGB、机器人动作、metric depth、表面法线与functional role segmentation编码为RGB视频；训练时采样不同感知与动作任务，用单一视频扩散transformer和单一目标函数统一学习跨空间的时序预测，无需模态专用学习head。

Result: 在held-out RLBench任务上，结构化感知训练提升闭环平均成功率（优于纯RGB训练）；五任务外部对比中XGenAct达52%成功率，最强基线仅26%；未来深度与分割预测精度优于"先生成RGB再用冻结感知专家"的pipeline。

Conclusion: 统一的多空间视频表示与单模型训练可在无需模态专用head的情况下同时提升操作策略性能与空间预测质量，为world action model引入结构化空间监督提供了可行范式。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) have advanced robot control by predicting how observations and actions evolve over time. Despite this progress, RGB and action based future prediction does not explicitly address the spatial understanding needed for robot manipulation. Existing efforts often add a limited set of spatial prediction tasks through specialized heads or branches, leaving both the range of spatial supervision and the model architecture fragmented. We introduce XGenAct, a world action model that represents RGB observations, robot actions, metric depth, surface normals, and functional role segmentation as RGB videos through deterministic codecs. By sampling perception and action tasks during training, XGenAct uses one video diffusion transformer and one objective to learn temporal prediction across these spaces without modality specific learned heads. On held out RLBench tasks, structured perception training improves average closed loop success over RGB only training, and XGenAct achieves 52% success in the five task external comparison, versus 26% for the strongest evaluated baselines. It also predicts future depth and segmentation more accurately than the evaluated pipelines that generate RGB first and then apply a frozen perception expert.

</details>


### [149] [DR-IPC: Disturbance-Resilient Integrated Planning and Control for LiDAR-Based Quadrotor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03530)
*Peng Liu,Jingyan Wang,Qipeng Ye,Wen Li,Jinya Su,Zuo Wang,Shihua Li,Yunda Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DR-IPC框架，将轻量路径引导与NMPC统一，结合IEKF与非线性扰动观测器，直接输出角速度与推力，实现抗扰动LiDAR四旋翼导航，机载100Hz运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR四旋翼在杂乱环境+外部扰动下导航仍困难，主因是避障运动生成与抗扰控制被分为两层处理，导致耦合不足、扰动下失效。作者希望消除独立轨迹优化环节，实现规划-控制一体化并显式补偿扰动。动机清晰且切中实际痛点。

Method: DR-IPC：轻量路径引导 + 非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）直接生成角速度与推力；互联扩展卡尔曼滤波（IEKF）与非线性扰动观测器联合提供滤波状态估计与扰动重构，供NMPC预测；统一建模非线性四旋翼动力学、执行器约束、局部运动生成与带惩罚的安全飞行走廊残差，无需独立轨迹优化阶段。

Result: Gazebo与MARSIM仿真加室内外实验，覆盖风扰、悬挂负载、窄通道、球体撞击与动态障碍反应式避障。多目标导航带扰动任务完成数由1/10提升至9/10（Gazebo），实验高度RMSE由0.34m降至0.01m；全系统机载100Hz运行。

Conclusion: 验证了规划-控制一体化+扰动观测在抗扰四旋翼导航中的有效性，仿真与真机证据兼备，性能提升显著。局限在于扰动观测器依赖模型假设，走廊残差惩罚的调参敏感性、代码尚未开源、缺乏与分层基线在多种扰动下的系统性对比尚待补充。

Abstract: LiDAR-based quadrotor navigation in cluttered environments remains challenging under external disturbances, particularly when obstacle-aware motion generation and disturbance-rejection control are handled in separate layers. This article presents disturbance-resilient integrated planning and control (DR-IPC), which combines lightweight path guidance with nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) to directly generate angular velocity and thrust. An interconnected extended Kalman filter and nonlinear disturbance observer jointly provide filtered state estimates and reconstructed disturbances for NMPC prediction. The resulting formulation unifies nonlinear quadrotor dynamics, actuator constraints, local motion generation, and penalised safe-flight-corridor residuals without requiring a separate trajectory-optimization stage. Gazebo and MARSIM simulations, together with indoor and outdoor experiments, validate DR-IPC under wind, suspended payloads, narrow passages, ball impacts and reactive avoidance of a dynamic obstacle. In multi-goal navigation with disturbances, DR-IPC increases the number of completed missions from 1/10 to 9/10 in Gazebo and reduces the altitude RMSE from 0.34 to 0.01 m in experiments. The complete system operates onboard at 100 Hz. Supplementary videos are available on the project page this https URL , and the source code will be released.

</details>


### [150] [Autonomous Robotic Navigation for Endovascular Brain-Computer Interface Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03537)
*Harry Robertshaw,Weijie Qi,Nikola Fischer,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C. Booth,Sam E. John*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首次演示血管内脑机接口在脑静脉系统中的体外自主机器人导航：用Soft Actor-Critic分两阶段训练控制器，结合在线失败预测，实现仿真到体模的迁移并量化解剖泛化与sim-to-real校准问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血管内脑机接口可避免开颅，但器械需经解剖高度变异的脑静脉精准递送，人工操作难度大、风险高。作者希望验证自主机器人导航在脑静脉通路中的可行性，并探索在线失败预测以支持人类监督。

Method: 在仿真中训练Soft Actor-Critic控制器，覆盖右颈内静脉至矢状上窦的两个顺序子任务，训练时仅对单一解剖做几何增广。在训练解剖与未见过的留出解剖上评估：每任务-解剖条件250个仿真回合、5次透视引导体外机器人实验，共1000仿真回合与20次实体实验；另评估任务循环预测器用于在线识别即将发生的导航失败。

Result: 仿真成功率：任务A为训练解剖85.6%、留出解剖42.0%；任务B为98.4%与91.6%。20次体模实验中14次成功（70%），其中任务B在留出体模达80%。失败预测器在仿真中检出99.3-100.0%的失败，虚警率0.8-6.7%；体外实验中失败前预测风险上升，但部分成功回合概率偏高，显示迁移后校准下降。

Conclusion: 结果证明脑静脉自主通路可行，在线失败预测可辅助人类监督；但解剖泛化（尤其任务A骤降）与sim-to-real校准是临床前转化前需优先解决的问题。

Abstract: Endovascular brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) avoid craniotomy but require precise device delivery through anatomically variable cerebral veins. This work presents the first demonstration of in vitro autonomous robotic navigation for endovascular BCI access in the cerebral venous system. Soft Actor-Critic controllers were trained in silico for two sequential tasks spanning the right internal jugular vein to the superior sagittal sinus, using geometric augmentation of one training anatomy. Navigation was evaluated in a training anatomy and an anatomically unseen hold-out model over 250 in silico episodes and five fluoroscopy-guided in vitro robotic runs per task-anatomy condition, comprising 1,000 simulated episodes and 20 physical runs overall. Task recurrent predictors were also evaluated for online identification of impending navigation failure. In silico success rates for Tasks A and B were 85.6% and 98.4% in the training anatomy and 42.0% and 91.6% in the hold-out anatomy, respectively. Fourteen of 20 physical runs were successful (70% overall), including 80% success for Task B in the hold-out phantom. In silico the predictors detected 99.3-100.0% of failures with false-alarm rates of 0.8-6.7%. During in vitro evaluation, predicted risk increased before failed episodes, but elevated probabilities during some successful runs showed reduced calibration after transfer. These results demonstrate the feasibility of autonomous cerebral venous access and show how online failure prediction could support human oversight, while also identifying anatomical generalization and sim-to-real calibration as priorities before preclinical translation.

</details>


### [151] [RATE: Risk-Aware Tactile Encoding for Contact-rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03538)
*Yuyao Jiang,Haichao Liu,Jiarui Zheng,Zihan Ding,Weihao Yuan,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出风险感知触觉编码 RATE，学习任务条件化的交互风险表征，用历史条件预测获取交互上下文、告警监督关联风险，并通过轻量残差适配器与常规触觉特征互补，在仿真与真机上提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相近的触觉观测可能源自风险含义完全不同的交互条件（传感器噪声、任务必需变化、新出现的非期望接触），缺乏上下文风险信息时下游策略难以区分，导致对良性变化做多余修正、对真正危险接触响应延迟。

Method: RATE：以历史条件预测建模交互上下文；用告警（alert）监督将上下文与任务条件化风险关联；学习到风险感知表征通过轻量残差适配器与常规触觉特征结合。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验均显示任务成功率显著提升；控制消融验证了告警引导学习与预测式时序建模的互补收益。

Conclusion: 将任务条件化风险显式编码进触觉表征，可缓解触觉观测歧义，并与常规触觉特征互补，提升接触丰富操作任务表现。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is particularly valuable for contact-rich robotic manipulation. Recent work has made substantial progress in tactile representation learning for robotic manipulation. However, similar tactile observations can arise from interaction conditions with very different task-risk implications, such as sensor noise, task-necessary variations, and emerging undesirable contact. Without context-grounded risk information, these cases can be ambiguous to downstream policies, leading to unnecessary corrections to benign variations or delayed responses to genuinely risky contact. To address this limitation, we propose Risk-Aware Tactile Encoding (RATE), which learns tactile representations that encode task-conditioned interaction risk. Specifically, history-conditioned prediction captures interaction context, while alert supervision associates this context with task-conditioned risk. The learned risk-aware representation complements conventional tactile features through a lightweight residual adapter. Experiments in both simulation and the real world demonstrate substantial improvements in task success, with controlled ablations confirming the complementary benefits of alert-guided learning and predictive temporal modeling.

</details>


### [152] [A Unified Framework for Empowerment and Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03563)
*Wooyoung Chung,Tristan Shah,Volodymyr Makarenko,Stas Tiomkin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将empowerment（智能体输入与未来状态间的信道容量）构建为探测与执行共用同一策略的目标，使其只需一阶导数并可直接嵌入标准MPC求解器，可与任务代价联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的MPC性能依赖需要大量领域知识设计的信息量丰富的代价函数；empowerment作为目标无关的内在动机目标可产生有用行为，但现有方法存在两个限制：估计empowerment的探测策略与实际执行策略不一致，导致目标与行为脱节；且通常需要动力学的二阶导数，难以兼容标准MPC。

Method: 用单一策略同时承担探测与执行，使empowerment目标直接对应被执行的策略行为；由此得到的目标仅需一阶导数，可用标准MPC求解器优化，既可单独使用也可与任务特定代价组合。

Result: 在经典控制任务上评估，表明将empowerment与任务代价结合的成功率可匹配或超过单独使用任一目标的成功率。

Conclusion: 该形式化为标准MPC与内在动机控制之间提供了实用的桥梁，使目标无关的empowerment目标更易与常规MPC流程集成。

Abstract: Sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) is a powerful approach to trajectory optimization, but its performance depends on an informative cost function that often requires substantial domain knowledge to design. An alternative is to derive objectives directly from the system dynamics. Empowerment, defined as the channel capacity between an agent's inputs and its future state, provides such a goal-agnostic objective and has been shown to produce useful behaviors across a range of domains. However, existing empowerment-based controllers have two key limitations: empowerment is estimated using a virtual probing policy distinct from the executed control policy, obscuring its connection to the resulting behavior; and its computation typically requires second-order derivatives of the dynamics, limiting compatibility with standard MPC methods. We formulate empowerment using a single policy for both probing and acting, directly linking the empowerment objective to the executed behavior. The resulting objective requires only first-order derivatives and can be optimized with standard MPC solvers, either alone or in combination with a task-specific cost. We evaluate the proposed approach on classical control tasks and show that combining empowerment with task costs matches or exceeds the success rate achieved by either objective alone. Our formulation provides a practical bridge between standard MPC and intrinsically motivated control.

</details>


### [153] [AVL-JEPA: Preventing Causal Dynamics Information Collapse In Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03587)
*Yikang Qiao,Ling Zhang,Ziying Song,Duan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AVL（action-grounded vision-invariance latent）方法，用于解决JEPA世界模型中"因果动力学信息坍缩"问题，通过动作锚定与视觉不变性通路保留对规划有用的动力学信息，在四个机器人控制任务上提升视觉扰动下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA类世界模型在不重构观测的情况下预测未来潜在表示，但其高维视觉信息可能被过度保留，而关于动作物理后果的信息被丢弃。作者将这一失效模式命名为"因果动力学信息坍缩"（causal dynamics information collapse），即模型对视觉细节敏感却忽略动作的因果动力学效应，从而损害下游规划。

Method: 提出AVL：1）以实际执行的动作作为辅助动力学锚点（auxiliary dynamics anchor），促使模型保留动力学信息；2）设计视觉不变性通路，将扰动与干净的潜在预测对齐，且在对齐过程中不丢弃动力学信息，从而迫使模型充分利用因果动力学信息。

Result: 在TwoRoom、PushT、OGBench Cube、Reacher四个机器人控制任务上，AVL在视觉扰动条件下显著提升成功率，同时保持干净环境性能。作者进一步通过物理后果对齐、干净-噪声动力学一致性、以及针对特定转移子空间擦除的因果效应实验进行验证，表明与规划因果相关的动力学信息在AVL下得以保留。

Conclusion: AVL通过动作锚定与视觉不变性对齐，有效缓解JEPA世界模型中的因果动力学信息坍缩，保留对规划因果关键的动力学信息，并在视觉扰动下兼顾鲁棒性与原始性能。

Abstract: Joint embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) predict future latent representations without reconstructing observations, enabling world models to focus on high-level semantic dynamics. However, a JEPA can preserve high dimensional visual information while discarding information about the physical consequences of actions. We call this failure mode causal dynamics information collapse and propose action-grounded vision-invariance latent (AVL) to prevent this collapse. We first use the executed action as an auxiliary dynamics anchor that encourages the model to preserve dynamics information, and then use a vision-invariance pathway which aligns perturbed and clean latent predictions without discarding dynamics information, forcing the model to fully understand and utilize causal dynamics information. We validate AVL on four robotic control tasks (TwoRoom, PushT, OGBench Cube, and Reacher), showing that it substantially improves success rates under visual perturbations while preserving clean-environment performance. We further evaluate physical consequence alignment, clean-noisy dynamics consistency, and the causal effect of targeted transition subspace erasure. Collectively, these results indicate that dynamic information causally relevant to planning is preserved from collapse under AVL.

</details>


### [154] [World Action Learning via Interaction-Centric Spectral Latent Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03607)
*Zhiming Liu,Yikun Miao,Ying Chen,Hongrui Yin,Fangqi Zhu,Xiaoyi Pang,Quanxin Shou,Zhengyang Yan,Haodong Wang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WING 提出交互中心的光谱潜动作引导框架，从自我中心人类视频中剥离观察者运动、提取手-物交互潜动作，并利用跨具身共享的低频时间结构引导机器人动作生成，在 LIBERO 99.20%、RoboTwin 2.0 93.80%、RoboCasa-GR1 57.7% 及四个真机任务上取得强结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略学习依赖大规模真机交互数据，但机器人示教采集昂贵且难以规模化；自我中心视频蕴含丰富的任务相关人类交互经验，然而直接迁移面临两大障碍：一是基于帧重建推断的潜动作易被自我相机运动等无关变异主导，二是人类与机器人的行为时间动态存在差异。

Method: WING 先解耦观察者引起的相机运动与手-物交互，将交互中心成分蒸馏为潜动作；随后基于"跨具身任务语义集中于缓慢变化的时间结构"这一观察，在谱域中识别自我中心潜动作与机器人行为之间的共享低频分量，并用其引导动作生成。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上平均成功率 99.20%，RoboTwin 2.0 上 93.80%，RoboCasa-GR1 上 57.7%，并在四类真机操作任务的多种泛化设置下表现强劲。

Conclusion: 交互中心的光谱引导为从人类自我中心视频向机器人控制迁移物理交互知识提供了一条有效且可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Learning general-purpose robot policies requires large-scale real-world interaction data, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains expensive and difficult to scale. Egocentric videos offer abundant human interaction experience with task-relevant semantics for robotic manipulation, but direct transfer is challenging for two reasons: latent actions inferred from frame reconstruction can be dominated by nuisance variation such as ego-camera motion, and human and robot behaviors often exhibit different temporal dynamics. We propose WING (World Action Learning via INteraction-Centric Spectral Latent Guidance), a framework for transferring interaction knowledge from egocentric videos to robot policies. WING first separates observer-induced motion from hand-object interaction and distills the interaction-centric component into latent actions. It then exploits the observation that cross-embodiment task semantics are concentrated in slowly varying temporal structures, identifying shared low-frequency components between egocentric latent actions and robot behaviors in the spectral domain and using them to guide action generation. WING achieves average success rates of 99.20% on LIBERO, 93.80% on RoboTwin 2.0, and 57.7% on RoboCasa-GR1, and also performs strongly across four real-world manipulation tasks under diverse generalization settings. These results show that interaction-centric spectral guidance provides an effective and scalable way to transfer physical interaction knowledge from human egocentric video to robot control. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [155] [Bridging Frontier Reasoning and Robot Execution: From Autonomous Demonstration Generation to Dense Language Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03615)
*Bosung Kim,Alexander Trevithick,Ruiyi Wang,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出两种互补方案弥合前沿模型与实时机器人控制的延迟鸿沟：一是用前沿模型自主生成演示（含纠错片段）来扩充训练数据以训练快速本地策略，并在部署时以"harness"结合前沿指令与本地策略；二是引入primitive/atomic/composite三层嵌套、每层多方面的密集语言监督，提升策略的语言指令跟随可靠性。在RoboCasa 365与BEHAVIOR-1K长时程任务上，二者结合在oracle与前沿模型指令下均取得最高性能，并在结合语义规划与操作、限时的crossword任务上联合验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型已能用少量演示实现机器人操作，但推理延迟高，难以满足实时控制需求。作者希望在不牺牲前沿模型推理与纠错能力的前提下实现低延迟执行，因此研究两条互补路径：一是用前沿模型自主扩充演示数据以训练快速本地策略；二是提升本地策略对前沿模型多样化语言指令的可靠跟随能力（延迟瓶颈转移后，这一能力成为新瓶颈）。

Method: (1) 演示生成桥：用前沿模型自主生成演示补充人类演示来训练快速本地策略；在上下文示例中加入"纠错演示片段"以展示如何从物理错误中恢复，提高生成可靠性；成功示例在上下文中累积使生成时间与成本下降。部署时用harness将前沿生成指令与本地策略结合，由本地策略执行低延迟动作，前沿模型保留引导与纠错作用。(2) 语言监督桥：引入三层嵌套粒度（primitive、atomic、composite）的密集语言监督，每层含多方面描述；在指令随执行进程变化的长时程任务上训练与评估。

Result: 在RoboCasa 365与BEHAVIOR-1K的长时程任务（指令随执行推进而改变）上，组合监督在oracle指令与前沿模型指令两种设置下均取得最高性能，表明通过策略的语言接口可实现更可靠的指令跟随。演示生成方面，随着成功示例在上下文中累积，生成时间与成本下降。最后在结合语义规划与操作、受固定时间预算约束的crossword任务上联合评估了两条路径。

Conclusion: 自主演示生成与密集语言监督是两条互补组件，可共同将前沿推理连接到低延迟本地执行：前者负责数据扩充与高效数据采集，后者负责提升本地策略对多样指令的可靠跟随。二者联合在长时程、指令动态变化的任务上有效，并获得oracle与前沿模型指令下的一致增益。

Abstract: Recent advances in frontier models enable robot manipulation from only a few demonstrations, but high inference latency limits their use for real-time robot control. To bridge this gap, we study two complementary approaches that connect frontier reasoning with low-latency local execution. First, we use a frontier model to autonomously generate demonstrations that supplement human demonstrations for training a fast local policy. We augment its in-context examples with corrective demonstration segments that show how to recover from physical errors, improving generation reliability. Generation time and cost decrease as successful examples accumulate in context, suggesting a path toward more efficient data collection. At deployment, a harness combines frontier-generated instructions with a fast local policy, enabling efficient execution while preserving the frontier model's ability to guide and correct actions. With low-latency execution delegated to the local policy, the bottleneck shifts to its capacity to reliably follow the frontier model's diverse instructions. Our second bridge introduces dense language supervision across three nested granularities---primitive, atomic, and composite---with multi-aspect descriptions at each level. Across long-horizon tasks in RoboCasa 365 and BEHAVIOR-1K, where instructions change as execution progresses, the combined supervision achieves the highest performance under both oracle and frontier-model instructors, demonstrating more reliable instruction following through the policy's language interface. Finally, we evaluate both bridges together on a crossword task that combines semantic planning and manipulation within a fixed time budget. These results support autonomous demonstration generation and dense language supervision as complementary components for connecting frontier reasoning to low-latency local execution.

</details>


### [156] [LLA-MPC on Embedded Hardware: Rapid Adaptive Control with Thousands of Parallel Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03616)
*Henry Z. Liao,Maitham F. AL-Sunni,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LLA-MPC的通用化实机实现：模块化动力学与积分器，在F1TENTH平台上于受限算力与噪声状态估计下实时在线辨识轮胎参数，低摩擦变路面高速跟踪优于固定标称模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLA-MPC原方案仅在仿真中验证，且模型结构固定。作者希望证明这种"无学习"的实时自适应辨识与控制框架能在真实嵌入式平台上、在算力受限与状态估计有噪声的条件下工作，并适用于不断变化的路面/轮胎摩擦条件。

Method: 对Look-Back/Look-Ahead自适应MPC进行通用化实现：动力学模型与积分器模块化以便替换；在嵌入式计算机上实时评估数千个候选模型完成轮胎参数在线辨识；在F1TENTH缩比车辆平台上部署，处理计算约束与噪声状态估计；在低摩擦轮胎和变化路面上做高速跟踪实验，并与固定标称模型MPC对比。

Result: 实验表明LLA-MPC在低摩擦轮胎、路面对比变化的条件下完成高速跟踪任务，而固定标称模型在此情形下失败；验证了算法在嵌入式实时约束与噪声状态估计下的可行性。

Conclusion: LLA-MPC可作为一种免学习、实时的自适应辨识+控制框架，从仿真走向真实机器人平台，具备模块化扩展能力；适用于摩擦/参数时变的高速控制场景。

Abstract: We present a generalized implementation of Look-Back and Look-Ahead Adaptive Model Predictive Control (LLA-MPC), a learning-free framework for real-time, rapid adaptive system identification and control. The original formulation was demonstrated only in simulation, for autonomous racing with a fixed model structure. Our implementation has modular dynamics and integrators, and we validate it on the F1TENTH platform under constrained computation and noisy state estimation. The system identifies tire parameters online by evaluating thousands of candidate models in real time on an embedded computer. Experiments with low-friction tires across changing surfaces show that LLA-MPC completes high-speed tracking tasks where a fixed nominal model fails. Code, videos, and our related work are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [157] [CORNAV: Construction-Aware Reasoning for Robot Navigation on Active Worksites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03622)
*Parastoo Ali Pour,Deepak Prakash Kumar,Tommy Zhou,Pramod Khargonekar,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CORNAV：基于2D CAD蓝图与项目工期表、无需BIM的施工场景导航框架，通过蓝图-三维场景图对齐、时变约束与LLM安全校验提升任务成功率至72.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑行业劳动力短缺、年损失超1.6万亿美元的低生产率与高伤亡率，推动自主机器人应用；但现有语言导航系统仅依赖语义场景理解，缺乏建筑专属上下文（图纸、工期、安全约束），导致永久建筑特征定位不可靠、无法安全导航活跃工地。

Method: CORNAV从2D CAD图纸与项目工期表出发（无需BIM）：将建筑蓝图与层次化开放词表3D场景图对齐以grounding物体查询；把工期表转换为时变导航约束；用LLM安全模块校验请求并对危险区域升级处理；再由A*规划器强制执行禁入区并优先规避高风险区域。

Result: 在室内办公室与真实建筑工地两类场景中，蓝图grounding将任务成功率从仅语义检索的13.0%提升至72.2%；工期感知消除了所有硬性禁区违规；安全模块能正确拒绝由错误标注工期表引发的危险请求。

Conclusion: 显式引入建筑图纸、工期与安全约束等工程上下文，可显著弥补纯语义导航在施工场景中的定位与安全问题，为无BIM条件下的工地自主导航提供可行路径。

Abstract: The construction industry faces persistent labor shortages, low productivity that costs the global economy over $1.6 trillion annually, and one of the highest injury rates among major industries. These factors motivate the use of autonomous robots to improve efficiency and worker safety. Existing language-grounded navigation systems, however, rely on semantic scene understanding alone and lack access to construction-specific context such as architectural plans, evolving work schedules, and safety constraints. As a result, they localize permanent building features unreliably and cannot safely navigate active jobsites. We present CORNAV, a blueprint-grounded, schedule-aware navigation framework that operates from 2D CAD drawings and project schedules without requiring a Building Information Model. CORNAV aligns architectural blueprints against hierarchical open-vocabulary 3D scene graphs to ground object queries, converts project schedules into time-varying navigation constraints, and validates requests through an LLM-based safety module that escalates hazardous zones before planning. An A* planner then enforces mandatory exclusion zones while preferentially avoiding higher-risk areas. Across an indoor office and a real construction site, blueprint grounding raises task success from 13.0% to 72.2% over semantic retrieval alone, schedule awareness eliminates all hard-zone violations, and the safety module correctly rejects hazardous requests arising from mislabeled project schedules.

</details>


### [158] [EyeRobot 2.0: Active Gaze for Precise Manipulation without Wrist Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03710)
*Kush Hari,Justin Kerr,Nidhya Shivakumar,Samarth Mahapatra,Carmelo Sferrazza,Jiahui Lei,Jitendra Malik,C. Karen Liu,Ken Goldberg,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EyeRobot 2.0 用单个双目相机实现主动视觉注视(foveal gaze)，通过分层强化学习训练注视伺服与注视目标选择器，配合注视相对 SE(3) 夹爪动作表示，在真实与仿真双臂精细操作中大幅超越被动双目与 ego+wrist 相机策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准操作策略依赖腕部相机来获取局部精细视觉，但腕部视角易被夹爪或已抓取物体遮挡，且增加硬件负担。作者希望仅用单个立体相机，通过模仿人类"主动注视"机制将高分辨率计算集中到任务相关区域，从而在不牺牲精细操作能力的前提下减少相机数量。

Method: 构建 EyeRobot 2.0：两个眼视角可旋转对准 3D 注视点，图像按中心 foveal 方式分配更多视觉 token。注视控制分层进行：(1) 低层 gaze servoing 策略以目标物体为条件，用稠密几何奖励通过 RL 在真实数据上训练；(2) 目标选择器根据任务进度输出注视目标，并与 BC 抓取策略联合训练以发现类似人类的注视序列。夹爪信息被规范化到注视相对 SE(3) 坐标系，压缩动作分布。收集 7 个真实任务与 6 个仿真任务的遥操作数据。

Result: 超 1000 次真机试验与 1800 次仿真试验：仅用被动双目时标准策略真机成功率从 52% 降至 27%；EyeRobot 2.0 在真实提升 40%、仿真提升 20%；与腕部视角清晰的 ego+wrist 策略相当(69% vs 64%)，在抓取物体遮挡腕部相机时成功率翻倍以上(48% vs 22%)。

Conclusion: 主动视觉注视可作为腕部相机的有效替代，用更少传感器实现精细双臂操作，并对遮挡具有更强鲁棒性；分层 RL 注视控制与注视相对动作表示是关键设计。

Abstract: Inspired by human vision, we introduce a framework using active gaze to enable fine-grained bimanual manipulation with only a single stereo camera. EyeRobot 2.0 physically attends to a 3D fixation point in the scene by swiveling two eye viewpoints to center their gaze on it. The resulting images are processed foveally by allocating more visual tokens to the image centers, focusing computation on task-relevant features. Such Active Visual Fixation (AVF) requires carefully coordinated gaze during task execution, which we accomplish hierarchically by first training a low-level gaze servoing policy conditioned on a goal object, then training a target selector which emits fixation goals based on task progress. Both modules are trained with RL on real-world data: the first is trained with a dense geometric reward and the second co-trains with the BC gripper policy which allows it to discover fixation sequences that can resemble a human's fixation sequence while performing the task. EyeRobot 2.0 further takes advantage of fixation by canonicalizing gripper information into a fixation-relative SE(3) frame, which compacts the size of the action distribution to learn. We collect teleoperation data for 7 real-world and 6 simulated tasks, and conduct over 1000 physical and 1800 simulated robot trials comparing EyeRobot 2.0 against passive stereo and ego + wrist camera policies trained on the same data. Removing wrist cameras is costly for standard policies: with only passive stereo, real-world success drops from 52% to 27%. EyeRobot 2.0 closes this gap with only stereo, outperforming passive stereo by 40% in real and 20% in sim. It matches ego + wrist policies when their wrist views are clear (69% vs. 64%), and more than doubles their success when grasped objects occlude the wrist cameras (48% vs. 22%)

</details>
